- **Moon**: gravity 1.62, normal climate. Unique resources are Titanium, zinc and Lunar Sapphire (for the Navigator). Mobs and structures include Moon Eel, Lunargrade, Moon Snail, and the Lunar Base.
- **Mars**: gravity 3.71, normal climate. Unique resources are Titanium, Martian iron (for Martian Steel), and quartz. Mobs and structures include Mars Cobra, Moth, Toad, Worm, and the Martian Base.
- **Mercury**: gravity 3.70, scorching climate. Unique resources are Tungsten (only found here) and titanium. Mobs and structures include Mercury Raptor, Roach and Tortoise.
- **Venus**: gravity 8.87, scorching climate. Unique resources are Titanium, zinc, quartz and glowstone. Mobs and structures include Venus Mimic, Scorpion, Stone Bull and Vulture.
- **Earth Orbit**: space climate.

Star Maps for Ceres, Jupiter, Saturn and others exist as items, but none of them have a dimension in this version.

- Suits: the Iron Space Suit (oxygen plus insulation) covers the Moon and Mars. The Martian Steel Space Suit adds heat resistance and is required for Mercury and Venus. Create's netherite diving gear plus netherite leggings also works as a heat-proof suit. With Northstar Curios Compat, oxygen tanks work from curio slots, and the Space Walk enchant restores Earth gravity.
- Fuel: Methane, Hydrocarbon, Hydrogen and Biofuel all burn for 1,600. Liquid Hydrogen burns for 3,200, twice as long. Electrolysis splits water into oxygen and hydrogen.
- Bases: the Oxygen Sealer fills rooms with air (it needs oxygen and rotation). The Temperature Regulator and Large Fans extend its range.
- Titanium: mix rutile, zinc, chlorine and carbon into titanium tetrachloride, then sequenced-assemble it on a sturdy sheet.

## Where you can actually go
The pack runs four Northstar destinations beyond Earth: the **Moon**, **Mars**, **Mercury** and **Venus**. Each one now generates waystones, so once you have been somewhere you can warp back rather than flying every time. Northstar needs both Create and Valkyrien Skies to work, which is why the rocket is a build rather than a single item.

## Aircraft (Immersive Aircraft and Man of Many Planes)

In this pack, a datapack replaces most aircraft recipes with Create mechanical crafting, so check JEI rather than wikis. Parts: the Hull (andesite casing plus iron), the Engine (brass sheets, a precision mechanism, sturdy sheets, a boiler), the Sail (Create white sails), and the Propeller (iron sheets around a Create propeller). Fuel is any furnace fuel or blaze powder.

- **Biplane** (fast explorer): the cheapest fixed-wing plane, needs a runway. Tier A.
- **Gyrodyne** (early copter): muscle-powered, push it to take off, no fuel needed to start. Tier B.
- **Quadrocopter** (building): hovers precisely, but slow. Tier B.
- **Airship** (utility): slow, but easy to steer. Tier B.
- **Cargo Airship** (logistics): carries a full storage inventory, about 80 slots. Tier A.
- **Warship** (combat): a flying fortress with several weapon mounts, including a Rotary Cannon (5 damage per shot), a Heavy Crossbow, and a Bomb Bay. Tier S.
- **Bamboo Hopper** (seaplane): 3 seats, lands on water. Tier B.
- **Scarlet Biplane / Economy Plane** (Man of Many Planes): agile or fuel-efficient variants made from Immersive Aircraft parts. Tier C.

Upgrades: Eco/Nether Engines, Enhanced Propeller, Steel Boiler, Hull Reinforcement, Industrial Gears, Gyroscope HUD. Crashes and bombs don't break blocks in this pack.

## Physics ships (Valkyrien Skies 2)

- Build your hull out of ordinary blocks, then turn it into a moving ship with Clockwork's Wanderwand (select the area) plus Gravitron (assemble), or with Create: Propulsion's Physics Assembler plus Assembly Gauge.
- Thrust: Propulsion Thrusters (redstone-throttled, burn fuel), Propellers (wood blades for air, copper for water, andesite for both), Clockwork propeller/flap/wing bearings, hot-air balloons (Envelopes plus burners), and wings.
- Control: sit in a Command Seat. Stabilize with a Gyro and Reaction Wheel, and read height off the Altimeter. `C` is cruise, `V` is down.
- Community tip: keep ships symmetrical, with heavy parts low and central, or they'll tip.

## Ground and sea machines

- **Tunnel Digger** (Immersive Machinery): a rideable drill that bores tunnels and stores what it mines, with a big inventory and iron/diamond/netherite drill heads.
- **Bamboo Bee** (Immersive Machinery): a programmable delivery drone between containers.
- **Redstone Sheep** (Immersive Machinery): auto-harvests nearby crops.
- **Copperfin** (Immersive Machinery): a conduit-powered mini-sub with sonar.
- **Submarine** (Alex's Caves): a copper-block sub built around an Enigmatic Engine, fires torpedoes with the add-on.
- **Trains** (Create plus Steam 'n' Rails): an automated rail network with robot conductors.

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

# Ores and resources

This covers every modded ore, taken straight from the pack's world-generation files. Y range is where the ore can spawn (a "peak" range is densest in the middle). Vein is the maximum blocks per vein, and Veins is attempts per chunk, so more attempts means more common. JER in JEI shows the same data in-game.

- **Zinc** (Create, Overworld): Y −63 to 70, vein size 12, 8 veins per chunk. Used for Brass, andesite alloy, and everything else Create.
- **Uranium** (Create Nuclear, Overworld): Y −64 to 64, peaking around 0. Vein size 7, 6 veins per chunk. Used for uranium rods in the Create reactor.
- **Lead** (Create Nuclear, Overworld): Y −64 to 64, peaking around 0. Vein size 7, 6 veins per chunk. Used for reactor shielding and casings.
- **Uranium** (Bigger Reactors, Overworld): Y −64 to 48, peaking around −8. Vein size 8, 5 veins per chunk. Used for Bigger Reactors fuel, check JEI since the two uraniums differ.
- **Thorium** (Create: New Age, Overworld): Y 20 to 120, vein size 16, 2 veins per chunk. Used for New Age reactor fuel.
- **Magnetite** (a block, Create: New Age, Overworld): Y −20 to 60, vein size 4, 15 veins per chunk. Used for magnets in motors and generator coils.
- **Dimensional Shard** (RFTools): in the Overworld it's Y −15 to 40 with vein size 5 and 8 veins per chunk, in the Nether Y 2 to 40 with vein size 8 and 8 veins per chunk, and in the End Y 2 to 80 with vein size 10 and 8 veins per chunk. Used for every RFTools machine, and the End has the biggest veins.
- **Large Redstone** (Regions Unexplored, Overworld): Y from bedrock to 112, vein size 20, 16 veins per chunk. Extra-rich redstone.
- **Galena** (an iron ore, Alex's Caves, Magnetic Caves only): Y −24 to 56, vein size 14, 15 veins per chunk. Used for the Galena Gauntlet, magnets and iron.
- **Radrock Uranium** (Alex's Caves, Toxic Caves only): spawns through the whole biome, vein size 17, 30 veins per chunk, very rich. Used for uranium for the Raygun, the nuke and the Nuclear Furnace.
- **Coprolith Coal** (Alex's Caves, Primordial Caves): spawns through the whole biome, vein size 14, 25 veins per chunk. Used for coal.
- **Guanostone Redstone** (Alex's Caves, Forlorn Hollows): spawns deep, vein size 10, 6 veins per chunk. Used for redstone.
- **Cincinnasite** (BetterNether, Nether): spawns at all heights, vein size 8, 10 veins per chunk. Used for the first Nether armor and tools.
- **Nether Ruby** (BetterNether, Nether): Y 32 and up, with rare big veins higher up. Vein size 5 to 12, 3 to 5 veins per chunk. Used for Nether Ruby and Flaming Ruby gear.
- **Nether Lapis / Redstone** (BetterNether, Nether): Lapis spawns Y 32 and up with vein size 4 and 14 veins per chunk; Redstone spawns Y 8 to 40 with vein size 16 and 1 vein per chunk. Used for enchanting and redstone.
- **Thallasium** (BetterEnd, End islands): spawns at all heights, vein size 8, 24 veins per chunk. Used for the Thallasium to Terminite alloy chain.
- **Ender Ore** (BetterEnd, End islands): spawns at all heights, vein size 4, 12 veins per chunk. Used for Ender Shards (Eternal Crystal, Terminite).
- **Amber Ore** (BetterEnd, the End): spawns at all heights, vein size 6, 60 veins per chunk. Used for Amber gems.
- **Dragon Bone Block** (BetterEnd, the End): spawns at all heights, vein size 8, 24 veins per chunk. Used for bone blocks and decoration.
- **Titanium, Moon** (Northstar): Y −64 to 48, vein size 6, 7 veins per chunk. Used for rockets, engines and Martian Steel.
- **Titanium, Mars** (Northstar): near bedrock, in the bottom 100 levels. Vein size 13, 6 veins per chunk, bigger veins than the Moon.
- **Titanium, Venus** (Northstar): Y −63 to 60, vein size 15, 8 veins per chunk. The best titanium planet.
- **Titanium, Mercury** (Northstar): a narrow band, Y −10 to 10, vein size 10, 3 veins per chunk.
- **Tungsten** (Northstar, Mercury only): deep, around the bottom 110 levels. Vein size 9, with 50 plus 50 plus 10 veins per chunk across its variants. Very abundant on Mercury.
- **Martian Iron and Volcanic Ash** (Northstar, Mars): Martian Iron spawns near the bottom with vein size 13 to 26 and 18 plus 10 veins per chunk; Volcanic Ash spawns at the lava level with vein size 64 and 2 to 6 veins per chunk. Used for Martian Steel, along with titanium.
- **Zinc, copper, iron, gold, diamond, lapis and quartz** (Northstar, Moon/Mars/Mercury/Venus): Y varies by planet. Every planet has its own versions of these ores, so you can build out there.
- **Blue Ice, Obsidian and Asurine** (Northstar, Moon): Y −28 to 112, vein size 64, up to 12 veins per chunk. Blue ice is used for reactor coolers, plus obsidian.
- **Reefstone** (Spawn, Oceans): Y 10 to 63, vein size 46, 4 veins per chunk. Used for decoration.
- Ore doubling: Create crushing wheels turn raw ore into crushed ore plus bonus nuggets. Wash it with an encased fan and water for even more, the best ore multiplier in the pack.
- Finding ores fast: the Twilight Ore Magnet pulls ores to the surface. Digging Claws and Pickaxe Heater artifacts, the Tunnel Digger vehicle, RFTools quarry cards, CC mining turtles, and the Alex's Caves Magnetic Quarry all speed things up.

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

# Bosses
There are 40+ bosses. A lot of wikis have the wrong HP for our version, so these numbers were taken from the pack itself. The tier letter is a rough difficulty rating based on HP, damage, mechanics and how hard the boss is to get to. If a fight goes wrong, your gear waits in your corpse in the arena (`Shift` + `U` shows where), and you get some protection for a few seconds after you respawn.

- **Tremorzilla** (Alex's Caves): 500 HP, 10 armor, 30 damage. Found in the Toxic Caves; its egg only hatches from a nuclear blast, then you tame the baby with 4 or more Waste Drums. What you get is a tame kaiju with a 100-block beam. Tier S.
- **Luxtructosaurus** (Alex's Caves): 600 HP, 20 armor, 12 damage. Summoned at the Primordial Caves volcano by throwing in an Ominous Catalyst. Drops Tectonic Shards for the Extinction Spear and Tectonic armor. Tier S.
- **Helvar, Underworld Knight** (Bosses'Rise): 250 HP, 15 damage. Found at the Underworld Arena (Nether Wastes or Soul Sand Valley), needs the Arena Key. Drops the Knight Sword, which throws a slash wave; arena chests hold Knight armor. Tier S.
- **The Lich** (Corrupted Champion, The Graveyard): 400 plus 200 HP, 14 armor, 30 and 70 damage. Summoned at the Lich Prison altar at night with 3 Ominous Bone Staff fragments and a full Vial of Blood. Drops 1 of 5 Bone Staffs (20% each). Tier S.
- **Dark Doppelganger** (Dark Doppelganger): 6,000 HP, 20 armor, 20 damage, and it can't take more than 150 damage from a single hit. Summoned in the End by placing a Shadow Orb on a Shadow Altar. It gets a copy of the weapon and armor (enchants included) and the spell power of whoever offers the orb, plus 25% more spell power. Drops a Nether Star, 3 Echo Shards, 3 diamond blocks, a scroll, and one of the Doppelganger Ring (+30 HP, +150 mana), Elder's Necklace or Summoner Necklace. Tier S.
- **Adam, the Void Archmage** (Adam's Ars Plus): 2,000 HP, 23 armor, 15 toughness, 20 damage. Found in the Void Fortress on the outer End islands; do the Mage Summon ritual there with an Eye of Void. Drops 16 to 24 Void Souls for Void armor, the strongest armor in the pack. Tier S.
- **Josh** (Adam's Ars Plus): 1,800 HP, 23 armor, 15 toughness, 20 damage. No dungeon; the Mage Summon ritual with a Manipulation Essence calls him. Drops 4 to 8 Advanced Clockwork for the Enchanter's Stopwatch. Tier S.
- **Matt, the Holy Archmage** (Adam's Ars Plus): 1,225 HP, 23 armor, 15 toughness, 20 damage. Found in the Holy Mausoleum in the Soul Sand Valley, with an Eye of Holy. Drops 8 to 16 Hero Souls for Holy armor. Tier S.
- **Ender Dragon** (True Ending): about 300 HP. Found on the main End island (YUNG's redesign). Drops its egg (which respawns), a 50% Simply Swords roll, and End access. Tier A.
- **Nerakyss, the Kraken** (Bosses'Rise): 500 HP, 20 armor, 10 damage. Found on the Kraken Ship in deep oceans, the most common Bosses'Rise structure. Drops Kraken Teeth for the Kraken Trident. Tier A.
- **Hullbreaker** (Alex's Caves): 400 HP, 14 damage. Found in the Abyssal Chasm; hunts anything that glows. Drops the Enigmatic Engine (for the Submarine) and the Immortal Embryo. Tier A.
- **Echo of Tyros** (Iron's Spells): 1,000 HP, 15 armor, 10 damage plus fire spells. Summoned in the Nether Citadel with a Cinderous Soulcaller. Drops a Divine Soulshard (for Iron's best armor pieces), a 55% chance at the Decrepit Scythe or Signet of the Betrayer, and Cinder Essence. Tier A.
- **Wilden Chimera** (Ars Nouveau): 1,000 HP, 6 armor, 8 damage. Summoned with the Ritual of Summon Wilden plus a Wilden Spike, Horn and Wing. Drops the Wilden Tribute, which the Archmage spell book needs. Tier A.
- **Cam, the Lightning Archmage** (Adam's Ars Plus): 925 HP, 20 armor, 12 toughness, 15 damage. Found in the Nimbostratic Ruins in the mountains, with an Eye of Lightning. Drops Lightning Souls. Tier A.
- **Nick, the Earth Archmage** (Adam's Ars Plus): 900 HP, 17 armor, 10 toughness, 15 damage. Found in the Overgrown Barracks in the Deep Dark, with an Eye of Earth. Drops Earth Souls. Tier A.
- **Dead King** (Iron's Spells): 500 HP, 15 armor, 10 damage plus spells, 80% knockback resistance. There's also an ominous version with 1,000 HP. Found deep in the Catacombs; a Wayward Compass points the way. Drops a 50% chance at the Blood Staff or the Necronomicon, plus scrolls, runes and ink. Tier A.
- **Hydra** (Twilight Forest): 360 HP, its breath hits for 19. Lives in the Fire Swamp lair, which opens after the Minoshroom. Drops 7 to 10 Fiery Blood for Fiery armor and sword. Tier A.
- **Ashlord, Infernal Dragon** (Bosses'Rise): 250 HP, 15 armor, 14 damage. Found at the Dragon Tower (plains, jungle, swamp, savanna). Drops 8 to 19 Dragon Bones for Dragon Bones armor and a skull. Tier A.
- **Captain Cornelia** (Aquamirae): 200 HP, 16 armor, skill-based damage. Summoned in the Ice Maze by blowing the Shell Horn near water. Drops the Frozen Key, the Three-Bolt Helmet, and a 10% chance at a music disc. Tier A.
- **Ur-Ghast** (Twilight Forest): 250 HP. Found at the top of the Dark Tower, after Knight Phantoms. Drops Carminite and Fiery Tears. Tier A.
- **Frostmaw** (Mowzie's Mobs): 250 HP, 10 damage. A rare wild spawn in snowy biomes. Drops an Ice Crystal (only if killed while holding one) and a music disc. Tier A.
- **Skor, the Yeti** (Bosses'Rise): 250 HP, 2 armor, 18 damage. Found at the Yeti Hideout in snowy plains (very common). Drops the Ice Gauntlet, which freezes, throws ice spikes, and does a slam-leap or block. Tier B.
- **Forsaken** (Alex's Caves): 250 HP, 10 damage. Found in the Forlorn Hollows, created when Underzealots sacrifice a Vesper. Drops Pure Darkness for the Dreadbow. Tier B.
- **Snow Queen** (Twilight Forest): 200 HP, 7 damage. Found at the Aurora Palace, after the Alpha Yeti. Drops either the Triple Bow or the Seeker Bow, 50/50. Tier B.
- **Alpha Yeti** (Twilight Forest): 200 HP. Found at the Yeti Cave in the Snowy Forest. Drops 6 Alpha Yeti Fur for Yeti armor. Tier B.
- **Knight Phantoms** (Twilight Forest): 6 phantoms at 35 HP each, 0 armor. Found at the Knight Stronghold; place a trophy on the pedestal to summon them. The chest holds enchanted Knightmetal tools and Phantom armor. Tier B.
- **Void Worm** (Alex's Mobs): 160 HP. Summoned by throwing a Mysterious Worm into the End void. Drops a Void Worm Eye and 2 Mandibles for the Dimensional Carver. Tier B.
- **Umvuthi, the Sunbird** (Mowzie's Mobs): 150 HP, 2 damage. Found at the Umvuthana Grove in savanna. Drops the Sol Visage, which summons 10 followers. Tier B.
- **Ryan, the Flame Archmage** (Adam's Ars Plus): 500 HP, 15 armor, 10 toughness, 12 damage. Found in the Infernal Prison in the Nether Wastes, with an Eye of Flame. Drops Flame Souls. Tier B.
- **Sirok, the Sandworm** (Bosses'Rise): 150 HP, 20 armor, 10 damage. Found at the Sandworm Nest in desert. Drops the Sandworm Gauntlet, which does a poison barrage or an earthquake. Tier B.
- **Gum Worm** (Alex's Caves): 150 HP, 10 armor, 9 damage. Found in the Candy Cavity (neutral, and rideable with Candy Cane Hooks). Drops the Sweet Tooth for the Sack of Sating. Tier C.
- **Cade, the Frost Archmage** (Adam's Ars Plus): 325 HP, 12 armor, 5 toughness, 6 damage. Found in the Frozen Library in snowy biomes, with an Eye of Frost. Drops Frost Souls. The easiest Archmage. Tier C.
- **Minoshroom** (Twilight Forest): 120 HP, 5 to 7 damage. Found at the bottom of the Labyrinth, in the Swamp. Drops the Diamond Minotaur Axe (guaranteed) and Meef Stroganoff. Tier B.
- **Naga** (Twilight Forest): 120 HP, 5 damage. Found in the Naga Courtyard, in the starting biomes. Drops 6 to 11 Naga Scales and a trophy. Tier C.
- **Lich** (Twilight Forest): 100 HP, 3 damage. Found at the Lich Tower, after the Naga. Drops 1 of 4 scepters and enchanted gold gear. Tier C.
- **Mother of the Maze** (Aquamirae): 100 HP, 6 armor, 5 damage. Found deep inside the Ice Maze. Drops up to 8 Abyssal Amethyst, the core of the Abyssal set. Tier B.
- **Ferrous Wroughtnaut** (Mowzie's Mobs): 40 HP, 30 damage. Found in underground ruins, Y 20 to 50. Drops the Axe of a Thousand Metals and the Wrought Helm (both guaranteed). Tier B.
- **Tongbi, the Sculptor** (Mowzie's Mobs): 140 HP. Found at mountain peaks; trade a Bluff Rod to start a 60-block climb trial. Drops 1 random Geomancer piece per win (Staff, Beads, Robe, Belt or Sandals). Tier C.
- **Magnetron** (Alex's Caves): 30 to 100 HP, 6 armor, 2 or more damage. Found in the Magnetic Caves; grows by absorbing metal blocks. Drops the Heart of Iron for the Resistor Shield. Tier C.
- **Wither** (vanilla, Minecraft): 300 HP, 4 armor. Summoned with soul sand and 3 skulls. Drops a Nether Star and has a 5% Simply Swords roll. Tier A.
- **Warden** (vanilla, Minecraft): 500 HP, 30 damage. Found in the Deep Dark (Skreechers can call one). Drops a Sculk Catalyst. Tier S.
- **Legendary Blob** (RFTools Dimensions): 5,000 HP. Found in custom RFTools dimensions. Drops dimension materials. Tier S.

HP, armor and damage are read from each mod's code in this pack, except for the Graveyard Lich and Aquamirae bosses (pack config files) and the True Ending dragon (the mod's changelog, not confirmed in the pack files). The magic bosses (Iron's Spells, Ars Nouveau, Adam's Ars Plus and Dark Doppelganger) were read from their code and the pack's configs too.

## Boss mechanics worth knowing
- **Ferrous Wroughtnaut**: it's immune except from behind, after it buries its axe in a slam. Bait the slam, then hit its back. Below 50% HP it does double swings, below 25% triple swings.
- **Twilight Lich**: it has 3 shields; reflect its bolts back at it or splash Healing potions on it. Then it summons zombies, then fights in melee. A Twilight Scepter also breaks the shields.
- **Hydra**: every head you kill grows back as 2 (max 7). Heads only take damage with their mouths open. Keep moving, since the fire breath has travel time. Bring Fire Resistance.
- **Knight Phantoms**: only the one solid-looking phantom takes damage. Block the thrown tools with a shield. Their chest always drops, even with boss-drop chests on.
- **Alpha Yeti**: it's immune to arrows while throwing ice bombs, so switch to melee. It rampages at low HP.
- **Graveyard Lich**: it's invulnerable while casting, so don't waste hits then; strike during its hunt phase instead. Phase 2 hits for about 70.
- **Captain Cornelia**: she pulls you in, heals rapidly around 75% HP (burst her through it), and resists ranged attacks, so fight her in melee.
- **Umvuthi**: after it loses 45 HP it calls Umvuthana healers. Kill the adds first. Trading 7 gold blocks beforehand gives an hour of Sun's Blessing.
- **True Ending Dragon**: crystal cages now spawn phantom guardians you have to kill. It has a ground-slam dive (a triple dive below 75% HP), impact fireballs and a laser. It goes briefly immune at 10% HP until it perches, then hovers for the final blow.
- **Void Worm**: hit the head. Hitting body segments splits it into two half-HP worms. Fights happen over the void, so bring feather falling or slow falling.
- **Luxtructosaurus**: its tail spin disables shields and its enraged phase adds lava and fire breath. Bring Fire Resistance, fight in the open, and use ranged attacks (Raygun, bow).
- **Hullbreaker**: it hunts light and glowing. Turn off your Submarine floodlights and remove glowing effects to avoid it.
- **Forsaken**: it heals fast in darkness at low HP. Fight it somewhere lit.
- **Helvar**: it's a souls-like duel, with light and heavy combos, dodges, rift projectiles and a fake death, so don't celebrate early. Use the `Z` dodge roll.

- **Archmages** (Adam's Ars Plus): craft the Eye for that Archmage's school at the Enchanting Apparatus (an Ender Eye plus the matching essence, 500 Source) and it points to the dungeon. Inside, do the Mage Summon ritual with the Eye. While it charges, the ritual throws Mysterious Mages and Mage Knights at you. Each boss's souls make its armor and the next tier's materials, so the easy order runs Cade, Ryan, Nick, Cam, Matt, then Adam.
- **Wilden Chimera**: it has 4 phases (at 75, 50 and 25% HP) with a short invulnerable swap between them. It rams, dives with an explosion that breaks blocks in this pack, and later buffs itself with Regeneration and Speed. Summon it well away from anything you care about.
- **Echo of Tyros**: it has 50% extra resistance to fire magic, so leave your fire spells at home. It changes phase at 75, 50 and 33% HP.
- **Dark Doppelganger**: whatever weapon and armor you're wearing when you offer the orb, it gets a copy, enchants included, on top of its own 20 armor. It can't take more than 150 damage from one hit, so a single huge hit won't end it. Armor-ignoring damage (Ars Crush and Wind Shear, the Magic Edge enchant) is extra useful against it.

> The progression lock in the Twilight Forest is on: locked biomes hit you with Hunger, Frost, Darkness, Fire or acid rain until you beat the previous boss (check Advancements → Twilight Forest). Bosses drop loot into a chest at their spawn point. Unlock advancements go to everyone who damaged the boss, or anyone who picks up its trophy, so pass trophies around to unlock areas for friends.

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

# Weapons compared
Every notable melee weapon in the pack, ranked on the same scale. Dmg is the damage shown in the tooltip, Speed is attacks per second, and DPS is the two multiplied together (sustained damage with full-strength hits, before enchants and crits). A slow, heavy weapon wins on burst; a fast one wins on DPS. Abilities often matter more than either, so read that column too.

Baseline: a vanilla Netherite Sword is 8 damage times 1.6 speed, for 12.8 DPS.

> Most wikis get this wrong: in our version, Runic and Unique Simply Swords weapons get +5 damage from their material (netherite gets +4). Every Runic weapon hits 1 harder than its Netherite version, and uniques come out 1 higher than a lot of online damage calculators show.

- **Glass Sword** (Twilight Forest, sword): 40 damage, 1.6 speed, 64 DPS, but only 1 durability. Shatters on the first hit for a one-shot "nuke." Get it as a chance drop from a Snow Queen trophy on a cutting board, or as loot. Tier S.
- **Bramblethorn** (Simply Swords, rapier): 9 damage, 2.2 speed, 19.8 DPS, 3,270 durability. An orbiting toxic spore swarm with escalating damage, and it still works from the off-hand. Unique loot. Tier S.
- **Molten Edge** (Simply Swords, greataxe): 10 damage, 1.9 speed, 19.0 DPS, 3,270 durability. Strength and Speed scale with missing HP; its roar ignites and knocks back. Unique loot. Tier S.
- **Wickpiercer** (Simply Swords, spear): 10 damage, 1.9 speed, 19.0 DPS, 3,270 durability. Flicker Fury: every strike hits twice for a duration. Unique loot. Tier S.
- **Livyatan** (Simply Swords, greataxe): 10 damage, 1.9 speed, 19.0 DPS, 3,270 durability. Encases foes in ice; shatter early for bonus damage. Unique loot. Tier A.
- **Amethyst Rapier** (Iron's Spells, rapier): 8 damage, 2.3 speed, 18.4 DPS, 2,031 durability. Cheap: amethyst, chain and Weapon Parts. Tier A.
- **Hellrazor / Twilight Gale** (Iron's Spells): 13 damage, 1.4 speed, 18.2 DPS, 2,031 durability. Hellrazor is a smithing upgrade of the Decrepit Scythe from Echo of Tyros; Twilight Gale is crafted with mithril. Tier A.
- **Flame Tongue / Summer Rain** (Refined Mod, sword): 10 damage, 1.8 speed, 18.0 DPS. +10% Fire spell power or +10% spell power, and each has a spell built in. Craft with Arcane Ingots. Tier A.
- **Jade Guandao** (Magic From The East, polearm): 12 damage, 1.5 speed, 18.0 DPS, 2,031 durability. +2 armor toughness while held. Craft with Refined Jade. Tier A.
- **Spellbreaker** (Iron's Spells, sword): 10 damage, 1.8 speed, 18.0 DPS, 2,031 durability. +15% spell cooldown reduction while held. Craft with mithril and a diamond. Tier A.
- **Runic Katana / Twinblade / Cutlass** (Simply Swords, sword): 9 damage, 2.0 speed, 18.0 DPS, 2,031 durability. 1 random Runic Power, re-rollable. Smith a netherite weapon with a Runic Tablet. Tier A.
- **Storm's Edge / Whisperwind / Star's Edge / Dreadtide / Magiblade** (Simply Swords, twinblade/katana): 9 damage, 2.0 speed, 18.0 DPS, 3,270 durability. Dashes, lifesteal at night (Star's Edge), void stacks (Dreadtide), sonic boom (Magiblade). Unique loot, except Magiblade which comes from a Decaying Relic. Tier A.
- **Runic Rapier** (Simply Swords, rapier): 8 damage, 2.2 speed, 17.6 DPS, 2,031 durability. Runic Power. Smithing. Tier A.
- **Icewhisper / Arcanethyst / Thunderbrand** (Simply Swords, halberd): 13 damage, 1.3 speed, 16.9 DPS, 3,270 durability. Frost aura plus blizzard, levitate-and-slam, or a charged dash. Unique loot. Tier S.
- **Frostfall** (Simply Swords, greathammer): 11 damage, 1.5 speed, 16.5 DPS, 3,270 durability. Ice-encases foes for burst damage; self-encase for immunity and regen. Unique loot. Tier A.
- **Legionnaire Flamberge** (Iron's Spells, greatsword): 11 damage, 1.5 speed, 16.5 DPS, 2,031 durability. +4 armor while held. Smithing upgrade of the Keeper's Flamberge from Citadel chests. Tier A.
- **Flaming Ruby Sword** (BetterNether, sword): about 8 damage, 2.0 speed, about 16 DPS, 2,861 durability. The fastest netherite-tier sword, with enchantability 32. Smith a Nether Ruby sword with a template. Tier A.
- **Soulrender / Magiscythe** (Simply Swords, scythe): 10 damage, 1.6 speed, 16.0 DPS, 3,270 durability. Stacking weakness, then heal, or a magic storm that repairs gear. Unique loot, except Magiscythe which comes from a Decaying Relic. Tier A.
- **Netherite Katana / Twinblade / Cutlass** (Simply Swords, sword): 8 damage, 2.0 speed, 16.0 DPS, 2,031 durability. Smith a diamond weapon with netherite. Tier B.
- **Masamune / Muramasa** (Magic From The East, katana): 7.5 or 8 damage, 2.1 or 2.0 speed, about 16 DPS, 2,031 durability. +15% Spirit or Blood spell power. Masamune is crafted; Muramasa has no recipe. Tier B.
- **Soulkeeper** (Simply Swords, greathammer): 14 damage, 1.1 speed, 15.4 DPS, 3,270 durability. Absorb souls to toughen up, release them to debuff and heal. Unique loot. Tier S.
- **Decrepit Scythe / Keeper's Flamberge** (Iron's Spells): 11 damage, 1.4 or 1.3 speed, 15.4 DPS (14.3 for the Flamberge), 1,000 durability. Echo of Tyros drop, or a Citadel Rampart Chest. Tier B.
- **Poisoned Blade** (Aquamirae, sword): 5 damage, 3.0 speed, 15.0 DPS, 500 durability. Poison on hit, and it's the fastest weapon in the pack. Craft an Angler's Fang with iron. Tier B.
- **Soulstealer / Emberlash** (Simply Swords, rapier/sai): 6 damage, 2.5 speed, 15.0 DPS, 3,270 durability. Mark and blink to a target, or stacking smoulder with an evade-heal. Unique loot. Tier A.
- **Pirate Saber** (Bosses'Rise, saber): about 9 damage, 1.6 speed, about 14.4 DPS, 2,000 durability. Found on Kraken Ship pirates or in chests. Tier B.
- **The Watcher / Brimstone Claymore** (Simply Swords, claymore): 12 damage, 1.2 speed, 14.4 DPS, 3,270 durability. Executes low-HP foes and siphons health, or an explosive brimstone effect. Unique loot. Tier S.
- **Sunfire / Harbinger** (Simply Swords, longsword): 9 damage, 1.6 speed, 14.4 DPS, 3,270 durability. Plants a war standard that burns and slows foes while buffing allies. Crafted: a Dormant Relic into a Righteous or Tainted Relic. Tier A.
- **Boreal Blade** (Iron's Spells, greatsword): 16 damage, 0.9 speed, 14.4 DPS, 2,031 durability. The hardest hit of the Iron's weapons. Craft with mithril and an Ice Venom Vial. Tier A.
- **Aeternium Sword** (BetterEnd, sword): 8.5 damage, 1.6 speed, 13.6 DPS, 2,196 durability. The best End-metal sword. Smith a Terminite sword with an Aeternium ingot and the Aeternium template. Tier A.
- **Waxweaver / Caelestis** (Simply Swords, claymore): 12 damage, 1.1 speed, 13.2 DPS, 3,270 durability. A lethal hit heals you to full instead, or a chance to avoid damage with an invulnerable burst. Unique loot. Tier S.
- **Netherite Halberd** (Simply Swords, halberd): 11 damage, 1.2 speed, 13.2 DPS, 2,031 durability. The best non-unique heavy DPS. Smithing. Tier B.
- **Soul Pyre / Hiveheart** (Simply Swords, greataxe/hammer): 13 damage, 1.0 speed, 13.0 DPS, 3,270 durability. Swap places with a target and freeze it, or bee stings plus regen honey. Unique loot. Tier A.
- **Netherite Sword** (vanilla, Minecraft, sword): 8 damage, 1.6 speed, 12.8 DPS, 2,031 durability. The baseline. Smithing. Tier B.
- **Fiery Sword** (Twilight Forest, sword): 8 damage, 1.6 speed, 12.8 DPS, 1,024 durability. Built-in Fire Aspect II. Craft Fiery Ingots from Hydra blood. Tier A.
- **Enchanter's Sword** (Ars Nouveau, sword): 8 damage, 1.6 speed, 12.8 DPS, netherite durability. Casts its inscribed spell on every hit, with a free Amplify. Enchanting Apparatus. Tier A.
- **Ice Sword** (Twilight Forest, sword): 7.5 damage, 1.6 speed, 12.0 DPS, 32 durability. Frosted III for 10 seconds. Loot, or the Twilight Delight freezing trick. Tier B.
- **Fin Cutter** (Aquamirae, sword): 6 damage, 2.0 speed, 12.0 DPS, 1,000 durability. More damage the lower your HP. Craft Abyssal Amethyst with diamond and fins. Tier B.
- **Enchanter's Knife** (Ars Nouveau's Flavors & Delight, knife): 6 damage, 2.0 speed, 12.0 DPS. Casts a spell on hit and works as a cutting-board knife. Tier B.
- **Headcutter / Steeleaf Katana** (Twilight Spellbooks): 12 or 11 damage, 1.0 speed, 12 or 11 DPS, 2,031 durability. +20% Blood or Nature spell power and +100 mana while held. Crafted from Twilight Forest gear and Twilight Ink. Tier B.
- **Knight Sword** (Helvar's) (Bosses'Rise, greatsword): about 13 damage, 0.9 speed, about 11.7 DPS, 1,480 durability. Right-click throws a powerful slash wave. Helvar drop. Tier A.
- **Awakened Lichblade** (Simply Swords, greatsword): 13 damage, 0.9 speed, 11.7 DPS, 3,270 durability. Soul aura; send souls to lifesteal a distant target, and they return as absorption. Crafted: Slumbering to Waking to Awakened. Tier S.
- **Whisper of the Abyss** (Aquamirae, greatsword): 14 damage, 0.8 speed, 11.2 DPS, 1,400 durability. Minus 10% target armor per hit, up to 5 stacks, plus extra reach. Craft Abyssal Amethyst with netherite. Tier S.
- **Hearthflame** (Simply Swords, greathammer): 14 damage, 0.8 speed, 11.2 DPS, 3,270 durability. Tremors pull enemies in, then a fiery blast launches them. Unique loot. Tier S.
- **Knightmetal Sword** (Twilight Forest, sword): 7 damage, 1.6 speed, 11.2 DPS, 512 durability. Bonus damage against armored targets. Knight Phantom chest, or craft it. Tier B.
- **Aeternium Hammer** (BetterEnd, hammer): 11.5 damage, 1.0 speed, 11.5 DPS, 2,196 durability. An AoE mining tool. Craft. Tier B.
- **Spirit Crusher** (Magic From The East): 18 damage, 0.6 speed, 10.8 DPS, 2,031 durability. Extra soul damage based on the target's current HP, +20% spell resistance, 10% slower movement while held. Craft with Crystallized Souls. Tier A.
- **Ribboncleaver / Enigma** (Simply Swords, greathammer/halberd): 13 damage, 0.8 speed, 10.4 DPS, 3,270 durability. Plus 15% damage reduction and a plus 95% next hit, or a chasing twister. Unique loot. Tier A.
- **Diamond Minotaur Axe** (Twilight Forest, axe): 10 damage (17 on a charge), about 1,561 durability. Sprint-charge hits for up to 17. Minoshroom drop (guaranteed). Tier A.
- **Divider** (Aquamirae, sword): 7 damage, 1.4 speed, 9.8 DPS, 2,200 durability. Minus 5% target max HP per hit, up to 10 stacks for minus 50%. Craft Abyssal Amethyst with netherite. Tier A.
- **Kraken Trident** (Bosses'Rise, trident): about 9 damage, 1.1 speed, about 9.9 DPS. Loyalty I; the throw pulls and damages everything nearby. Craft with 2 Kraken Teeth. Tier B.
- **Extinction Spear** (Alex's Caves, spear): 9 damage (plus 3), 1.0 speed, about 12 DPS, 1,300 durability. A spirit follow-up; the throw hits for 10 plus a 1 to 12 explosion and returns, and you can charge up 3 hit-blocking spirit shields. Craft with Tectonic Shards from Luxtructosaurus. Tier S.
- **Ortholance** (Alex's Caves, lance): 6 damage, 1.6 speed, 9.6 DPS, 340 durability. Charge-dash about 12 blocks, then 4 to 9 waves at 5 each. Deep One gift, about 4%. Tier A.
- **Soul Breaker** (Magic From The East, sword): 6 damage, 1.5 speed, 9.0 DPS, 2,031 durability. +10% spell resistance and bonus soul damage. Tier C.
- **Spellcharged Greatsword** (Apprentice's Codex, greatsword): 8 damage, 1.1 speed, 8.8 DPS, netherite durability. Craft with netherite and Arcane Ingots. Tier C.
- **Axe of a Thousand Metals** (Mowzie's Mobs, axe): 9 damage, 0.9 speed, 8.1 DPS, unbreakable. Available very early. Ferrous Wroughtnaut drop (guaranteed). Tier A.
- **Desolate Dagger** (Alex's Caves, dagger): 4 damage (plus 2), 2.0 speed, about 12 DPS, 360 durability. Each hit summons a ghost blade that strikes for +2. Underzealot drop, 2.5%. Tier B.
- **Large Sword** (Bosses'Rise, greatsword): about 11 damage, 0.7 speed, about 7.7 DPS, 400 durability. Extra reach. Dragon Tower mobs or chests. Tier C.
- **Earthrend Gauntlet** (Mowzie's Mobs, gauntlet): 6 damage, 1.2 speed, 7.2 DPS, 400 durability. Tunnels through blocks. Grottol content. Tier B.
- **Terrible Sword** (Aquamirae, sword): 6 damage, 1.0 speed, 6.0 DPS, 750 durability. You take damage if the hit doesn't kill. Craft. Tier C.
- **Frostmint Spear** (Alex's Caves, spear): 4 damage, 1.0 speed, 4.0 DPS, unbreakable. Thrown freezing cloud; the craft makes 4. Craft with Frostmint and Candy Canes. Tier B.
- **Naga Fang Dagger** (Mowzie's Mobs, dagger): 3 damage, 2.0 speed, 6.0 DPS. Poison, 2x backstab damage. Craft with a Naga Fang. Tier C.

Magic weapon stats were read from Iron's Spells, Ars Nouveau and the addons' weapon code. Stats come from the pack's Simply Swords config, the Twilight Forest, BetterEnd, BetterNether and Aquamirae tier classes, and Mowzie's config. Values marked "about" are read from Bosses'Rise and BetterNether constructors, where the displayed value may differ by 1.

## Magic vs melee
Spells and swords don't fit on one damage-per-second scale, but a few things decide which one hits harder.
- **Armor still counts.** Most spell damage is reduced by armor, the same as a sword hit. The exceptions skip armor: Crush and Wind Shear (Ars Nouveau), Arcane Blast, Arcane Beam and Compound Phial (Apprentice's Codex), and Annihilate (Adam's Ars Plus). For melee, the Magic Edge enchant does the same. Against bosses with 20 or more armor (Luxtructosaurus, the Archmages, the Dark Doppelganger) that's a big deal.
- **Spells scale with gear, weapons with enchants.** A full matching Iron's school set adds +20% spell power and +40% to that school, and Dread Robes add +80% Blood power. On the Ars side each Amplify adds flat damage (Harm +2, Flare +3), and the Spell Power thread adds +2 per level. A sword gets +3 per hit from Sharpness V.
- **Hybrids use both.** Iron's Flaming Strike, Divine Smite, Shadow Slash and Raise Hell, Refined Mod's weapon spells and Magic From The East's Devastating Cut all hit harder with a stronger held weapon, enchants included. The Enchanter's Sword works the other way round and casts a spell with each swing.
- **Mana is the limit.** A sword never runs dry. A caster lives on max mana, regen and cooldowns: the Ancient Codex (-20% cooldowns), Ring of Recovery (-15%) and Spellbreaker (-15%) all help, and keeping your mana above 95% gives your spells a damage bonus (Ars 'n Spells).
- **Utility is where magic wins.** Blink, teleport, heal, freeze, snare, shields, summons and flight are things no sword does.

## Simply Swords: every weapon type by material
There are 15 weapon types in 5 materials. Iron, gold and diamond are crafted; Netherite is a smithing upgrade from diamond; Runic is a netherite weapon plus a Runic Tablet at a smithing table. It rolls one random Runic Power (Freeze, Swiftness, Zephyr, Stoneskin, Momentum, Immolation, Wildfire, Shielding, and more), which you can re-roll with another tablet.

- **Twinblade**: Iron 6, Gold 4, Diamond 7, Netherite 8, Runic 9. Speed 2.0, Runic DPS 18.0. A fast all-rounder.
- **Katana**: Iron 6, Gold 4, Diamond 7, Netherite 8, Runic 9. Speed 2.0, Runic DPS 18.0. A fast all-rounder.
- **Cutlass**: Iron 6, Gold 4, Diamond 7, Netherite 8, Runic 9. Speed 2.0, Runic DPS 18.0. A fast all-rounder.
- **Rapier**: Iron 5, Gold 3, Diamond 6, Netherite 7, Runic 8. Speed 2.2, Runic DPS 17.6. The fastest sword type.
- **Warglaive**: Iron 6, Gold 4, Diamond 7, Netherite 8, Runic 9. Speed 1.8, Runic DPS 16.2.
- **Sai**: Iron 3, Gold 1, Diamond 4, Netherite 5, Runic 6. Speed 2.5, Runic DPS 15.0. The fastest overall, and the lowest hit.
- **Longsword**: Iron 6, Gold 4, Diamond 7, Netherite 8, Runic 9. Speed 1.6, Runic DPS 14.4. Same as a vanilla sword.
- **Halberd**: Iron 9, Gold 7, Diamond 10, Netherite 11, Runic 12. Speed 1.2, Runic DPS 14.4. The best heavy DPS.
- **Claymore**: Iron 8, Gold 6, Diamond 9, Netherite 10, Runic 11. Speed 1.2, Runic DPS 13.2.
- **Scythe**: Iron 7, Gold 5, Diamond 8, Netherite 9, Runic 10. Speed 1.3, Runic DPS 13.0.
- **Glaive**: Iron 6, Gold 4, Diamond 7, Netherite 8, Runic 9. Speed 1.4, Runic DPS 12.6.
- **Spear**: Iron 6, Gold 4, Diamond 7, Netherite 8, Runic 9. Speed 1.3, Runic DPS 11.7.
- **Greataxe**: Iron 9, Gold 7, Diamond 10, Netherite 11, Runic 12. Speed 0.9, Runic DPS 10.8. Big hits.
- **Greathammer**: Iron 10, Gold 8, Diamond 11, Netherite 12, Runic 13. Speed 0.8, Runic DPS 10.4. The biggest standard hit.
- **Chakram**: Iron 5, Gold 3, Diamond 6, Netherite 7, Runic 8. Speed 1.0, Runic DPS 8.0. Thrown; dual-wield for an AoE spin.

## Simply Swords uniques: all 40
Every loot chest in the pack, except village houses, has a 0.1% chance per roll to include a unique (pack config). Community reports describe a hidden pity timer that raises the odds after repeated misses. The Wither has a 5% chance and the Ender Dragon a 50% chance to drop Simply Swords loot. Uniques found as loot start unawakened; use a Runic Tablet to restore their full power. All uniques have 3,270 durability.

- **Hearthflame**: greathammer, 14 damage, 0.8 speed. Tremors pull enemies in, then a fiery blast launches them. Loot.
- **Soulkeeper**: greathammer, 14 damage, 1.1 speed. Absorb souls (tougher, slower); release to cut enemy attack speed and heal. Loot.
- **Icewhisper**: halberd, 13 damage, 1.3 speed. Permafrost aura; channel a blizzard (costs hunger). Loot.
- **Arcanethyst**: halberd, 13 damage, 1.3 speed. Chance to levitate; channel lifts enemies and slams them down. Loot.
- **Thunderbrand**: halberd, 13 damage, 1.3 speed. Charge up for AoE, then dash for huge damage. Loot.
- **Awakened Lichblade**: greatsword, 13 damage, 0.9 speed. Soul Anguish: aura damage, command souls to lifesteal a target. Crafted chain.
- **Soul Pyre**: greataxe, 13 damage, 1.0 speed. Tether: swap places with and freeze a target, ignite and pull it on arrival. Loot.
- **Hiveheart**: greathammer, 13 damage, 1.0 speed. Bee stings on hit; bees brew regenerating honey. Loot.
- **Ribboncleaver**: greathammer, 13 damage, 0.8 speed. Passive +15% damage reduction; dash, shrug off hits, next hit +95%. Loot.
- **Enigma**: halberd, 13 damage, 0.8 speed. A chasing twister; the longer a foe is trapped, the more damage. Loot.
- **The Watcher**: claymore, 12 damage, 1.2 speed. Executes low-HP foes into absorption; siphons nearby health. Loot.
- **Brimstone Claymore**: claymore, 12 damage, 1.2 speed. Chance on hit to spew explosive brimstone. Loot.
- **Waxweaver**: claymore, 12 damage, 1.1 speed. Strength and Haste against burning foes; a lethal hit heals you to full instead, on cooldown. Loot.
- **Caelestis**: claymore, 12 damage, 1.1 speed. Chance to dodge damage: brief invulnerability, then an explosion scaled to damage negated. Loot.
- **Frostfall**: greathammer, 11 damage, 1.5 speed. Ice-encase foes for burst; self-encase for immunity plus regen. Loot.
- **Sword on a Stick**: longsword, 11 damage, 1.4 speed. A joke weapon with no ability, but it still hits hard. Loot.
- **Molten Edge**: greataxe, 10 damage, 1.9 speed. Stronger and faster at low HP; roar ignites and knocks back. Loot.
- **Livyatan**: greataxe, 10 damage, 1.9 speed. Ice-encase with early shatter for more damage. Loot.
- **Wickpiercer**: spear, 10 damage, 1.9 speed. Big attack-speed buff; every strike hits twice. Loot.
- **Soulrender**: scythe, 10 damage, 1.6 speed. Stacking weakness; consume the stacks to damage and heal. Loot.
- **Magiscythe**: scythe, 10 damage, 1.6 speed. Magic storm; attacking inside it can repair your gear. Decaying Relic.
- **Magispear**: spear, 10 damage, 1.5 speed. Leap-slam AoE, immune while leaping. Decaying Relic.
- **Twisted Blade**: claymore, 10 damage, 1.4 speed. Hits stack attack speed, up to 15; consume for Strength. Loot.
- **Bramblethorn**: rapier, 9 damage, 2.2 speed. Orbiting toxic spore swarm. Loot.
- **Storm's Edge**: twinblade, 9 damage, 2.0 speed. Jolt forward with brief invulnerability, speed and haste. Loot.
- **Whisperwind**: katana, 9 damage, 2.0 speed. Dash with resistance and absorption, stacking Echo. Loot.
- **Star's Edge**: twinblade, 9 damage, 2.0 speed. Bonus damage by day, lifesteal by night; dash or evade. Loot.
- **Dreadtide**: rapier, 9 damage, 2.0 speed. Voidcloak stacks (minus 10% damage taken each); release for an eldritch burst. Loot.
- **Magiblade**: longsword, 9 damage, 2.0 speed. Repels attackers; a delayed sonic-boom line. Decaying Relic.
- **Stormbringer**: sword, 9 damage, 1.6 speed. Timed parry deals damage plus a launch, and chains stronger. Loot.
- **Emberblade**: longsword, 9 damage, 1.6 speed. Channel, then launch fire shrapnel. Loot.
- **Sunfire**: longsword, 9 damage, 1.6 speed. Righteous Standard: burns foes, heals and strengthens allies. Crafted (Righteous Relic).
- **Harbinger**: longsword, 9 damage, 1.6 speed. Abyssal Standard: pulls and slows foes, hastes allies. Crafted (Tainted Relic).
- **Flamewind**: rapier, 9 damage, 1.4 speed. Seeds a fire DoT that detonates and spreads. Loot.
- **Mjolnir**: hammer, 9 damage, 1.0 speed. Lightning on hit in rain; channel a lightning storm plus resistance. Loot.
- **Soulstealer**: rapier, 6 damage, 2.5 speed. Mark a target; leap plus invisibility, or blink to it and blind it. Loot.
- **Emberlash**: sai, 6 damage, 2.5 speed. Smouldering stacks; evade back and heal a percentage of max HP. Loot.
- **Tempest**: twinblade, 6 damage, 1.5 speed. Elemental DoTs recalled into a growing vortex. Loot.
- **Shadowsting**: rapier, 4 damage, 2.3 speed. Armor-scaling magic damage, blinding mist plus shadowstep. Loot.

Damage equals 1 plus 5 for the Unique material, plus the per-weapon modifier. Speed equals 4 plus the configured attack-speed value.

### Gem sockets (late-game upgrades)
1. Smelt a spare unique in a furnace for an Empowered Remnant (this destroys the weapon).
2. Combine the Remnant, Crying Obsidian and a Runic Tablet at a smithing table for a Runefused Gem (a random Runic Power), or the Remnant, a Netherite Ingot and a Runic Tablet for a Netherfused Gem (stronger passives: Berserk, Echo, Onslaught, Spellshield, Soulshock, War Standard, and more).
3. Drag the gem onto a unique that has an empty socket of that type. Re-roll a gem with another tablet.

# Ranged weapons and gadgets
Everything that fights from a distance or casts something: bows, beam guns, staffs, scepters, summon items, gauntlets and machine weapons. These don't share one damage formula, so they're compared on what they do and how strong that is. The spells themselves are in the Magic sections; this list is items.

- **Dreadbow** (Alex's Caves, bow): a full charge (2 seconds or more) rains 30 shadow arrows (3 damage each) down on the target, and they ignore hit-invulnerability. 500 durability, uses normal arrows. Craft with Vesper Wings, Pure Darkness from the Forsaken, and Shadow Silk. Tier S.
- **Raygun** (Alex's Caves, beam gun): a continuous 25-block piercing beam (about 6 hits per second) that applies Irradiated. 1,000 charge; 1 Uranium Rod is a full refill. Craft with Polymer Plates, Uranium Rods and a Fissile Core. Tier S.
- **Triple Bow** (Twilight Forest, bow): 3 arrows per shot for the cost of 1, and it can triple-hit through i-frames. Uses arrows. Snow Queen drop (50%), or Trader Tokens. Tier S.
- **Extinction Spear** (Alex's Caves, thrown): 10 damage plus a 1 to 12 explosion, a Subterranodon spirit lifts the target 12 blocks, and the spear returns. 1,300 durability. Craft it after beating Luxtructosaurus. Tier S.
- **Nuclear Bomb** (Alex's Caves, explosive): a 48-block radius in this pack (the config is 3x, stock is about 36). 15-second fuse, or instant if it falls 20 or more blocks. Leaves Irradiated III. Single use. Craft, or use Create mechanical crafting. Tier S.
- **Sea Staff** (Alex's Caves, staff): homing water bolts, 6 AoE damage, no cooldown, and it works underwater. 850 shots. Deep One Mage gift at an Abyssal Altar, about 4%. Tier A.
- **Ortholance** (Alex's Caves, lance): charge to launch yourself about 12 blocks, leaving 4 to 9 damaging waves at 5 each. 340 durability. Deep One Knight gift, about 4%. Tier A.
- **Seeker Bow** (Twilight Forest, bow): homing arrows that can't crit. Uses arrows. Snow Queen drop, 50%. Tier A.
- **Ender Bow** (Twilight Forest, bow): a hit swaps your position with the target's. Uses arrows. Aurora Palace loot. Tier A.
- **Twilight Scepter** (Twilight Forest, scepter): 6-damage bolts that break Lich and Fortification shields. 99 charges, recharge with ender pearls. Lich drop (25%), or Trader Tokens. Tier A.
- **Scepter of Fortification** (Twilight Forest, scepter): a shield that absorbs physical hits. 9 charges, recharge with a golden apple. Lich drop (25%), or Trader Tokens. Tier A.
- **Scepter of Life Draining** (Twilight Forest, scepter): a beam that drains a mob's HP into yours. 99 charges. Lich drop (25%), or Trader Tokens. Tier B.
- **Zombie Scepter** (Twilight Forest, summon): summons a Loyal Zombie ally. 9 charges. Lich drop (25%), or Trader Tokens. Tier B.
- **Ice Gauntlet** (Bosses'Rise, gauntlet): freezes on hit; sneak-use for an ice-spike wave; jump plus use for a slam-leap; blocks like a shield. 1,200 durability. Skor drop. Tier A.
- **Knight Sword** (throw) (Bosses'Rise, slash wave): right-click throws a powerful slash projectile. 1,480 durability. Helvar drop. Tier A.
- **Sandworm Gauntlet** (Bosses'Rise, gauntlet): hold to charge a poison barrage; also has an earthquake mode. 1,000 durability. Sirok drop. Tier B.
- **Kraken Trident** (Bosses'Rise, trident): Loyalty I; the impact pulls and damages everything nearby. Craft with Kraken Teeth. Tier B.
- **Sol Visage** (Mowzie's Mobs, mask/summon): summon and command up to 10 Umvuthana warriors. Unbreakable. Umvuthi drop. Tier A.
- **Ice Crystal** (Mowzie's Mobs, focus): freezing ice breath that freezes targets for 2.5 seconds. 600 durability. Frostmaw drops it only if it still holds its crystal (you can also steal it while it sleeps). Tier A.
- **Geomancer set + Earthrend Gauntlet** (Mowzie's Mobs, geomancy): earth-shaping abilities; the gauntlet tunnels through blocks. Sculptor trial, or Grottol content. Tier B.
- **Blowgun** (Mowzie's Mobs, dart gun): poison darts. Uses darts. Craft. Tier C.
- **Magic Conch** (Alex's Caves, summon): summons 3 Deep Ones, 2 Knights and a Mage for 60 seconds. 5 uses. Deep One gift. Tier B.
- **Galena Gauntlet** (Alex's Caves, off-hand): launches your held iron or netherite tool 10 to 20 blocks (up to 50 with the enchant), as an attack or a miner. 400 durability. Craft it at the Magnetic Caves. Tier A.
- **Resistor Shield** (Alex's Caves, shield): a magnetic slam (6 AoE) plus a pulse; sneak to swap push and pull. 1,000 durability. Craft with the Heart of Iron from Magnetron. Tier A.
- **Totem of Possession** (Alex's Caves, control): remote-control any non-boss mob, and in this pack it works on players too. 1,000 durability. Craft with an Occult Gem and Moth Dust. Tier B.
- **Sugar Staff** (Alex's Caves, staff): orbiting peppermints; sneak-use for a damaging hex spiral. Licowitch drop, 2.5%. Tier B.
- **Shot Gum** (Alex's Caves, gun): two ricocheting gumballs per shot. 1 pile equals 5 shots. Craft. Tier C.
- **Pulsargun / Torpedoes** (AC: Torpedoes, gun/sub ammo): a magnetic shockwave, 7 charges. Submarine torpedoes come in TNT, Nuclear, or Pulsar (EMP). Craft. Tier B.
- **Maze Rose / Poisoned Chakra** (Aquamirae, thrown): summons damaging chakras, or a poison chakram. 1,800 and 1,200 durability. Craft. Tier B.
- **Hemolymph Blaster** (Alex's Mobs, sprayer): the top-tier Blood Sprayer upgrade (it can't be duplicated with Mimicream). Craft with a Blood Sprayer and Warped Mosco parts. Tier A.
- **Stink Ray / Pocket of Sand** (Alex's Mobs, debuff): nausea gas, or blinding sand. Craft. Tier C.
- **Bone Staffs (5)** (The Graveyard, staff): named necromancer staffs. Graveyard Lich drop, 20% each. Tier B.
- **Potato Cannon** (Create, gun): fires any food as ammo; dual-wield for about 50% faster fire; uses Backtank air. Craft. Tier B.
- **Chemical Sprayer / Turret** (Diesel Generators, sprayer): sprays any fluid; add a Lighter plus fuel for a flamethrower. The turret auto-targets. Craft. Tier B.
- **Electro-Baton / Zapper** (Power Grid, electric): shock melee or ranged. Needs a worn Portable Battery. Mechanical crafting. Tier B.
- **Tesla Coil** (Crafts & Additions, defense block): zaps mobs in a 3-block radius (3 hearts) every second. 1,000 FE per zap. Mechanical crafting. Tier B.
- **Rotary Cannon / Heavy Crossbow / Bomb Bay** (Immersive Aircraft, aircraft weapons): 5 damage per bullet (gunpowder); heavy bolts; TNT that doesn't break blocks in this pack. Uses gunpowder, arrows or TNT. Craft, mount it on a Warship and so on. Tier B.
- **Slingshot / Bombs / Cannon** (Supplementaries, throwables): fling any item; bombs, including the spiky variant; a boat- or raft-mounted Cannon that fires whatever you load. Uses items. Craft. Tier C.
- **Enchanter's Bow / Crossbow** (Ars Nouveau, bow): inscribe a spell and every arrow carries it, as long as you have the mana. Special arrows (Amplify, Pierce, Split) power it up further. Enchanting Apparatus. Tier A.
- **Blood Staff** (Iron's Spells, staff): the strongest summoning staff in the mod. Dead King drop only. Tier A.
- **Spellcaster guns** (Apprentice's Codex, gun): Copper, Iron, Gold, Diamond and Malignant tiers that fire spells. Craft. Tier B.
- **Soulpiercer** (Magic From The East, crossbow): mana-fuelled shots that scale with Spirit spell power. Craft with a Crystallized Soul. Tier B.
- **Hunter's Bow** (Refined Mod, bow): craft from a Graybeard Staff, string and a Nature Rune. Tier C.

# Armor compared
Every full armor set, on one scale. Armor is the full-set total (vanilla netherite is 20). Toughness is per piece, so a full set has four times that; it reduces damage from big hits. KB res is knockback resistance (1.0 is immune). Chest dur. is the chestplate's durability, a good proxy for how long the set lasts. The pack raises the usual armor and toughness caps, so sets above 30 armor or 20 toughness still count in full.

- **Adam (Void)** (Adam's Ars Plus): 34 armor, 6.0 toughness, 0 KB res, 2,560 chest durability. The strongest armor in the pack, and it also gives max mana, mana regen, spell damage and warding. Made at the Enchanting Apparatus from Ars tier 3 robes, Void Souls from Adam and Mana Diamonds (7,000 Source). Tier S.
- **Matt (Holy)** (Adam's Ars Plus): 30 armor, 5.0 toughness, 0 KB res, 1,920 chest durability. Hero Souls from Matt. Tier S.
- **Nick (Earth) / Cam (Lightning)** (Adam's Ars Plus): 24 armor, 4.0 toughness, 0 KB res, 1,280 or 1,920 chest durability. Edges out Aeternium. Earth or Lightning Souls. Tier S.
- **Aeternium** (BetterEnd): 24 armor, 3.5 toughness, 0.2 KB res, 640 chest durability. Beats netherite on every stat, and there's also an Armored Elytra version (900 durability). Smith Terminite gear with an Aeternium ingot (terminite and netherite alloyed in the End Stone Smelter) using the Aeternium template, found in End Village loot. Tier S.
- **Fiery** (Twilight Forest): 24 armor, 1.5 toughness, 0 KB res, 400 chest durability. The highest raw armor in the Twilight Forest, fire-themed. Craft Fiery Ingots from Hydra's Fiery Blood and iron. Tier S.
- **Dread Robes** (Alex's Caves Spellbooks): 23 armor, 0 toughness, 0 KB res, 160 chest durability. Each piece gives +150 mana, +5% spell power and +20% Blood spell power. Smith Iron's Cultist robes with Pure Darkness and Shadow Silk. Tier A.
- **Heavy** (Immersive Armors): 18 armor, 4.0 toughness, 0.5 KB res, 320 chest durability. The highest toughness and knockback resistance of any set. Craft with iron blocks, or find it as armorer or stronghold loot. Tier S.
- **Abyssal** (Aquamirae): 16 armor (+25%), 3 toughness (+4), 0.1 KB res, 640 chest durability. 2 pieces give +25% armor and +4 toughness. The Heaume set delays a fatal hit; the Tiara set blinds and slows nearby foes. Craft with Abyssal Amethyst (from Mother of the Maze, or 4 Ship Graveyard Echoes plus an amethyst shard) and netherite; the Brigantine also needs Astral Dust from the Frozen Chest. Tier S.
- **Netherite** (vanilla, Minecraft): 20 armor, 3.0 toughness, 0.1 KB res, 592 chest durability. The baseline, and it's fireproof. Smithing. Tier A.
- **Ryan (Flame)** (Adam's Ars Plus): 20 armor, 3.0 toughness, 0 KB res, 1,280 chest durability. Netherite-level, from Flame Souls, with mana and spell damage on top. Tier A.
- **Netherite Battlemage** (Iron's Spells): 20 armor, 3.0 toughness, 0 KB res. Each piece gives +125 mana and +5% spell power. Smith Wizard robes with netherite. Tier A.
- **Holy / Exalted Aurum / Raijin / Anubis / Quetzalcoatl** (Armor of the Ages): 20 armor, 3.0 toughness, 0.1 KB res, 592 chest durability. Exact netherite stats in a historic look, cosmetic alternatives. Craft, or find as structure loot. Tier A.
- **Yeti** (Twilight Forest): 20 armor, 3.0 toughness, 0 KB res, 320 chest durability. The highest-toughness Twilight set. Craft with Alpha Yeti Fur. Tier A.
- **Flaming Ruby** (BetterNether): 20 armor, 2.6 toughness, 0.3 KB res, 480 chest durability. Triple netherite's knockback res, with enchantability 32. Smith Nether Ruby with a template. Tier A.
- **Phantom** (Twilight Forest): 20 armor, 2.5 toughness, 0 KB res, 480 chest durability. Durable, and it drops pre-enchanted (level 20 to 30). Knight Phantom chest. Tier A.
- **Martian Steel Space Suit** (Northstar): 20 armor, 2.5 toughness, 0.05 KB res, 560 chest durability. Oxygen-sealed, insulated, and heat-resistant for Mercury and Venus. Craft with Martian Steel and a Copper Backtank. Tier A.
- **Martian Steel armor** (Northstar): 20 armor, 2.5 toughness, 0.05 KB res, 480 chest durability. Netherite-class combat armor from Mars metal. Craft with Martian Steel. Tier A.
- **Three-Bolt** (Aquamirae): 20 armor, 2.0 toughness, 0.1 KB res, 1,200 chest durability. The helmet and suit give water breathing; the full set means no freezing in Ice Maze water. The most durable set in the pack. The helmet comes from Cornelia; the rest is crafted from iron blocks and leather. Tier A.
- **Crystalite** (BetterEnd): 20 armor, 1.2 toughness, 0.1 KB res, about 480 chest durability. The helmet gives blindness immunity, the chest gives Haste, the boots give Speed, and the full set gives Regeneration (reported buggy). Craft at an Infusion Pedestal: a Terminite piece with Crystal Shards and Amber. Tier A.
- **Prismarine** (Immersive Armors): 20 armor, 0 KB res, 288 chest durability. Spikes damage attackers, and it has a built-in enchant. Craft with a Heart of the Sea, or find it as ocean ruin loot. Tier A.
- **Knightmetal** (Twilight Forest): 20 armor, 1.0 toughness, 0 KB res, 320 chest durability. Craft with Knightmetal (Armor Shards). Tier B.
- **Steeleaf** (Twilight Forest): 20 armor, 0 toughness, 0 KB res, 160 chest durability. A cheap early 20-armor set, but fragile. Craft with Steeleaf. Tier B.
- **Iron's school robes** (Iron's Spells: Pyromancer, Cryomancer, Electromancer, Priest, Shadow-Walker, Cultist, Archevoker, Plagued): 20 armor, 0 toughness, 0 KB res. Each piece gives +125 mana, +5% spell power and +10% to its school. Wizard robes plus a school rune. Tier A for casters.
- **Cade (Frost)** (Adam's Ars Plus): 16 armor, 2.0 toughness, 0 KB res, 1,280 chest durability. The first Archmage set. Tier B.
- **Arcanist's Robes** (Ars Nouveau): 15 armor, 0 toughness, 0 KB res, 400 chest durability. Iron armor plus Magebloom Fiber. Tier C.
- **Wandering Magician** (Iron's Spells): 15 armor, 0 toughness, 0 KB res. +25 mana per piece. Leather armor plus Arcane Essence. Tier C.
- **Sorcerer's Robes** (Ars Nouveau): 11 armor, 0 toughness, 0 KB res, 400 chest durability. Gold armor plus Magebloom Fiber. Tier C.
- **Primordial Mage** (3 pc) (Alex's Caves Spellbooks): 9 armor, 2.0 toughness, 0 KB res. Each piece gives +85 mana, +5% Nature spell power and +5% summon damage. Tier C.
- **Pharaoh** (Armor of the Ages): 20 armor, 2.0 toughness, 0 KB res, 528 chest durability. Diamond-class. Craft, or find as desert loot. Tier B.
- **Diamond** (vanilla, Minecraft): 20 armor, 2.0 toughness, 0 KB res, 528 chest durability. Craft. Tier B.
- **Ars Elemental armor** (Ars Elemental, Fire, Water, Air or Earth): 20 armor, 2.0 toughness, 0 KB res (Earth 0.02), 528 chest durability. Each piece gives +100 mana and +4 mana regen, and boosts and discounts its element. Upgrade Ars tier 3 robes with a Mark of Mastery, a netherite ingot and essences. Tier A.
- **Battlemage's Robes** (Ars Nouveau): 20 armor, 2.0 toughness, 0 KB res, 528 chest durability. Diamond armor plus Magebloom Fiber. Mana and regen go up with each upgrade, and threads add perks. Tier B.
- **Terminite** (BetterEnd): 19 armor, 1.0 toughness, 0.05 KB res, 528 chest durability. The base for Crystalite and Aeternium. Craft with Terminite. Tier B.
- **Divine** (Immersive Armors): 18 armor, 0 KB res, 288 chest durability. Deflects one hit every 60 seconds. Craft with Robe armor, a Totem and gold, or find as temple, bastion or mansion loot. Tier A.
- **Nether Ruby** (BetterNether): 18 armor, 1.4 toughness, 0.2 KB res, 288 chest durability. Craft with Nether Ruby. Tier B.
- **Terrible** (Aquamirae): 17 armor, 0 toughness, 0 KB res, 400 chest durability. The half set gives a speed boost when hurt in water; the full set poisons attackers. Craft with an Angler's Fang, fins and iron. Tier B.
- **Cincinnasite** (BetterNether): 17 armor, 1.0 toughness, 0.05 KB res, 192 chest durability. The first Nether upgrade. Craft with Cincinnasite. Tier C.
- **Iron Space Suit** (Northstar): 16 armor, 0.5 toughness, 0 KB res, 320 chest durability. Oxygen-sealed and insulated, for the Moon and Mars. Craft with iron armor, plates and a Copper Backtank. Tier B.
- **Ironwood / Arctic** (Twilight Forest): 16 armor, 0 or 2.0 toughness, 0 KB res, 320 or 160 chest durability. Arctic is dyeable. Craft. Tier C.
- **Warrior** (Immersive Armors): 15 armor, 1.0 toughness, 0 KB res, 240 chest durability. Berserk: up to +20% damage when injured, plus +5% axe damage. Craft with leather and iron. Tier B.
- **Diving** (AC) (Alex's Caves): 15 armor, 0 toughness, 0 KB res, 300 chest durability. The helmet gives 40 seconds of air, the legs give extra swim speed, and the boots let you walk on muck. Abyssal Ruins chests, or a Drowned drop (20%). Tier B.
- **Iron Plate / O-Yoroi / Centurion** (Armor of the Ages): 15 armor, 0 toughness, 0 KB res, 240 chest durability. Iron-class cosmetic sets. Craft. Tier C.
- **Slime** (Immersive Armors): 14 armor, 0 toughness, 0.25 KB res, 320 chest durability. Bounces attackers back. Craft with slimeballs. Tier C.
- **Steampunk** (Immersive Armors): 14 armor, 0 toughness, 0 KB res, 160 chest durability. The head gives an entity radar, the chest a clock, the legs a double jump, and the boots' steam jets cancel fall damage. Craft with gold, a clock, a compass and a piston. Tier B.
- **Hazmat** (Alex's Caves): 13 armor, 0.5 toughness, 0 KB res, 300 chest durability. The full set gives complete immunity to Irradiated. Craft with Polymer Plates. Tier B.
- **Anti-Radiation Suit** (Create Nuclear): 12 armor, 0 toughness, 0 KB res, 240 chest durability. Blocks reactor Radiation, and comes in 16 colors. Craft. Tier B.
- **Thallasium** (BetterEnd): 12 armor, 0 toughness, 0 KB res, 240 chest durability. Craft. Tier C.
- **Wither** (Immersive Armors): 11 armor, 0 toughness, 0 KB res, 192 chest durability. Attackers get Wither. Upgrade Bone armor with nether brick. Tier C.
- **Primordial** (3 pc) (Alex's Caves): 10 armor, 0 toughness, 0 KB res, 300 chest durability. Up to +3 hunger and +4.8 saturation from raw meat. Craft with Tough Hide. Tier C.
- **Robe** (Immersive Armors): 8 armor, 0 toughness, 0 KB res, 224 chest durability. Magic resistance, and the highest enchantability at 50. Craft with blaze powder. Tier C.
- **Bone / Wooden** (Immersive Armors): 7 armor, 0 toughness, 0 KB res, 128 chest durability. Bone blocks some arrows. Craft. Tier C.

Read from armor-material classes and configs in the pack's jars: BetterEnd, BetterNether, Twilight Forest, Northstar, Aquamirae, Create Nuclear, Immersive Armors, Armor of the Ages. Alex's Caves numbers come from its wiki (2.x branch). Magic armor comes from the Adam's Ars Plus, Ars Nouveau, Ars Elemental, Iron's Spells and Alex's Caves Spellbooks material classes.

## Special pieces and mixed sets
- **Gilded Netherite** (Bygone Nether): netherite and gold armor at a smithing table. Full netherite stats, and Piglins stay neutral until the gold wears off.
- **Wrought Helm** (Mowzie's): an unbreakable helmet from the Wroughtnaut. Sol Visage is unbreakable too, and commands 10 Umvuthana.
- **Create Netherite Diving gear plus netherite leggings**: counts as an oxygen-sealed, heat-resistant space suit for Northstar.
- **Hood + Cloak of Darkness** (Alex's Caves): charge 50 seconds in total darkness for 10 seconds of very fast flight (special ability key).
- **Gingerbread armor** (Candy Cavity): the more damaged it is, the faster you move. Rainbounce Boots bounce instead of taking fall damage.
- **Alex's Mobs pieces**: Centipede Leggings (wall-climb), Outback Leggings (45% projectile dodge), Unsettling Kimono (undead turn neutral), Froststalker Helmet, Rocky Chestplate (sprint-roll), Flying Fish Boots (glide out of water), Roadrunner Boots (sand speed).
- **Tectonic armor** (AC: Torpedoes, from Luxtructosaurus shards) is advertised as better than netherite with fire resistance, but its stats couldn't be confirmed from the pack files. Check the tooltip in-game.

- **Iron's special pieces**: the Lightbringer chestplate (+4 toughness, +150 mana, +10% spell power, +40% knockback resistance), Boots of Speed (+25% move speed, +60% move speed while casting), the Infernal Sorcerer chestplate (fire spell hits build up to an explosion) and the Tarnished Crown (+150 mana, +25% mana regen, -15% melee damage). The first three need a Divine Soulshard from Echo of Tyros.
- **Ars threads**: Ars robes take threads at the Alteration Table: Spell Power, Warding, Magic Capacity, Life Drain, Undying (cheats death once per sleep), Gliding (elytra glide) and more. Adam's Ars Plus adds six boss threads as curios, including flight (Cloud Steps) and mana-fed absorption hearts (Immortality).

> Recommended builds: Tank, Aeternium chest plus Heavy pieces for knockback immunity. Boss hunter, Aeternium or netherite plus a Totem of Undying in the charm slot plus a Crystal Heart artifact. Explorer, Aeternium Armored Elytra plus Centipede Leggings plus Steampunk boots. Ocean, the Three-Bolt set, then Abyssal. Caster, a full Iron's school set for your main school (or Dread Robes for Blood) plus an Ancient Codex. Battle mage, Adam's Ars Plus Archmage armor, which beats netherite and still gives mana.

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

# Enchantments

Enchanting works the same as vanilla (table, anvil, books), and the pack doesn't change any enchanting settings. On top of the vanilla enchants you get 37 more from Ensorcellation, plus item-specific ones from Alex's Caves, the magic mods and a few others. All of it is below.

Enchantments also change how the weapon and armor comparisons in this guide play out, so that's covered first.

## How enchanting changes the weapon comparison

The weapon numbers earlier in the guide are unenchanted. Sharpness V adds a flat +3 damage to every hit, and a flat bonus helps fast weapons much more than slow ones, because they hit more often. Here's the same damage times speed number (hits per second, full strength) before and after Sharpness V:

- **Bramblethorn**: 19.8 without, 26.4 with Sharpness V (+6.6)
- **Molten Edge / Wickpiercer / Livyatan**: 19.0 to 24.7 (+5.7)
- **Runic rapier**: 17.6 to 24.2 (+6.6)
- **Runic katana / twinblade / cutlass**: 18.0 to 24.0 (+6.0)
- **Storm's Edge / Whisperwind / Star's Edge**: 18.0 to 24.0 (+6.0)
- **Poisoned Blade**: 15.0 to 24.0 (+9.0, the biggest jump in the pack because it swings 3 times a second)
- **Runic sai**: 15.0 to 22.5 (+7.5)
- **Flaming Ruby sword**: 16.0 to 22.0 (+6.0)
- **Frostfall**: 16.5 to 21.0 (+4.5)
- **Icewhisper / Arcanethyst / Thunderbrand**: 16.9 to 20.8 (+3.9)
- **Soulkeeper**: 15.4 to 18.7 (+3.3)
- **Aeternium sword**: 13.6 to 18.4 (+4.8)
- **The Watcher / Brimstone Claymore**: 14.4 to 18.0 (+3.6)
- **Netherite sword (vanilla)**: 12.8 to 17.6 (+4.8)
- **Fiery sword**: 12.8 to 17.6 (+4.8), and it already has Fire Aspect II built in
- **Hearthflame / Whisper of the Abyss**: 11.2 to 13.6 (+2.4)
- **Runic greathammer**: 10.4 to 12.8 (+2.4)
- **Axe of a Thousand Metals**: 8.1 to 10.8 (+2.7)

So once everything is enchanted, the fast weapons pull even further ahead on sustained damage. Big hammers are still better for single big hits and their abilities, but they gain the least from Sharpness.

Other enchants that matter for weapons:
- **Smite V** adds +12.5 damage against undead. That covers a lot of bosses and mobs here: the Wither, the Graveyard Lich, the Twilight Lich, Rotten Creatures zombies, Graveyard undead, skeletons. Smite beats Sharpness by a lot on those.
- **Bane of Arthropods V** is the same +12.5, but against spiders and other bug-type mobs.
- **Magic Edge** (Ensorcellation) turns your damage into magic damage, and magic damage ignores armor. That's worth a lot against the high-armor bosses: Luxtructosaurus, Nerakyss and Sirok (20 armor each), Captain Cornelia (16), Ashlord (15), the Graveyard Lich (14).
- **Instigating** gives bonus damage on your first hit against a full-health target, good with slow, heavy weapons.
- **Vorpal** randomly does powerful hits and decapitations.
- **Leech** heals you on kills. **Frost Aspect** slows and weakens.
- **Cavalier** adds damage while you're riding, which is good if you fight from a dragon or other mount.
- **Sweeping Edge** works on sword-type weapons, including Simply Swords ones.
- **Backstabbing** (Farmer's Delight) boosts damage when you hit from behind. It goes on knives.
- **Mending** matters most on low-durability gear: Steeleaf (131), the Ice Sword (32), Fin Cutter (1,000), Poisoned Blade (500).

Most sword-type weapons (Simply Swords, the Twilight, BetterEnd and BetterNether swords, Aquamirae swords) take the normal sword enchants. Tridents take trident enchants. The Alex's Caves weapons mostly use their own enchants (listed further down). If you're not sure whether something accepts an enchant, try it on an anvil with a book.

## How enchanting changes the armor comparison

Two things decide how much damage you actually take: your armor and toughness, then your Protection enchants.

- Armor can block at most 80% of a hit. Bigger hits cut into your effective armor, and toughness lowers how much they cut. That's why armor above 20 and extra toughness still matter: they keep you near that 80% cap when something hits hard.
- Protection IV on all four pieces takes off another 64% of whatever gets through. The enchantment part caps at 80%. Fire, Blast and Projectile Protection count double against their own damage type.

Damage you'd actually take from a 10, 20 or 30 damage hit, without enchants and then with Protection IV on all four pieces. The toughness shown here is for the full set, four times the per-piece number.

- **Adam's Void** (34 armor, 24 toughness): 2.0 / 4.0 / 6.0. With Prot IV: 0.7 / 1.4 / 2.2
- **Matt's Holy** (30, 20): 2.0 / 4.0 / 6.0. With Prot IV: 0.7 / 1.4 / 2.2
- **Nick's or Cam's** (24, 16): 2.0 / 4.0 / 7.2. With Prot IV: 0.7 / 1.4 / 2.6
- **Aeternium** (24, 14): 2.0 / 4.0 / 7.7. With Prot IV: 0.7 / 1.4 / 2.8
- **Fiery** (24, 6): 2.0 / 5.4 / 11.5. With Prot IV: 0.7 / 1.9 / 4.1
- **Abyssal with the 2-piece bonus** (20, 16): 2.7 / 6.7 / 12.0. With Prot IV: 1.0 / 2.4 / 4.3
- **Netherite, Ryan's, Netherite Battlemage, Yeti, Armor of the Ages netherite-tier sets** (20, 12): 2.8 / 7.2 / 13.2. With Prot IV: 1.0 / 2.6 / 4.8
- **Flaming Ruby** (20, 10.4): 2.9 / 7.5 / 13.8. With Prot IV: 1.0 / 2.7 / 5.0
- **Phantom, Martian Steel** (20, 10): 2.9 / 7.6 / 14.0. With Prot IV: 1.0 / 2.7 / 5.0
- **Heavy** (18, 16): 3.5 / 8.3 / 14.4. With Prot IV: 1.2 / 3.0 / 5.2
- **Diamond, Three-Bolt, Ars Elemental, Battlemage's Robes** (20, 8): 3.0 / 8.0 / 15.0. With Prot IV: 1.1 / 2.9 / 5.4
- **Crystalite** (20, 4.8): 3.2 / 9.0 / 17.2. With Prot IV: 1.2 / 3.2 / 6.2
- **Terminite** (19, 4): 3.7 / 10.1 / 19.2. With Prot IV: 1.3 / 3.6 / 6.9
- **Cade's Frost** (16, 8): 4.6 / 11.2 / 19.8. With Prot IV: 1.7 / 4.0 / 7.1
- **Dread Robes** (23, 0): 2.8 / 9.6 / 20.4. With Prot IV: 1.0 / 3.5 / 7.3
- **Steeleaf, Iron's school robes** (20, 0): 4.0 / 12.0 / 24.0. With Prot IV: 1.4 / 4.3 / 8.6

For scale, the Ferrous Wroughtnaut and Tremorzilla hit for 30, and the Graveyard Lich hits for up to 70 in its second phase. On Hard difficulty mob hits are 1.5 times stronger. Against that 70 damage hit, Adam's or Matt's armor lets 14 through, Aeternium 38 and netherite 53.

What this means:
- Adam's and Matt's Archmage armor are the best in the pack. Their armor is high enough that even a 70 damage hit stays at the 80% cap.
- Aeternium is the best you can get without the magic boss chain. Against a 30 damage hit it lets through about 40% less than netherite.
- Fiery's 24 armor is great against normal hits, but its low toughness shows against huge ones.
- Caster robes (Iron's school sets, Dread Robes) have decent armor but no toughness, so big hits go through them. That's the price of the mana and spell power.
- Heavy has less armor than the top sets but 0.5 knockback resistance, so it's more about not getting launched than taking less damage.
- Once everything has Protection IV, the gaps shrink a lot in actual health. Set bonuses (Abyssal's death delay, Divine's deflect, Crystalite's buffs) start to matter more than the raw stats.
- Vanilla magic damage (potions, some boss attacks) skips armor completely, but Protection still reduces it. Ensorcellation's Magic Protection is made for that. Most Ars and Iron's spell damage is reduced by armor like a normal hit.

## Enchantability

Enchantability only affects what you roll at the enchanting table. Higher means better and more enchants per roll. Books on an anvil don't care about it, so low-enchantability gear is best enchanted with books.

Weapons and tools:
- Highest: Flaming Ruby 32, Simply Swords uniques 30, Twilight Glass Sword 30, Aquamirae Dagger of Greed 30, Simply Swords Runic 25, Twilight Ironwood 25
- Middle: gold 22, Aquamirae Fin Cutter 20, Aeternium 18, Nether Ruby 18, Terrible Sword 18, Cincinnasite 16, netherite 15, iron 14, Terminite 14, Divider 14, Whisper of the Abyss 14
- Low: Thallasium 12, diamond 10, Fiery 10, Steeleaf 9, Knightmetal 8, Poisoned Blade 6, Remnant's Saber 5, Giant tier 5, Ice Sword 5

Armor:
- Highest: Immersive Armors Robe 50, Divine 30, Flaming Ruby 30, Terminite 26, Nether Ruby 25, Martian Steel (and both Northstar space suits) 25, gold 25, Crystalite 24
- Middle: Cincinnasite 20, Aeternium 18, Thallasium 17, netherite 15, Armor of the Ages netherite-tier sets 15, Yeti 15, Naga 15, Ironwood 15, Bone 15, Create Nuclear anti-radiation suit 12, Aquamirae Abyssal and Terrible 12
- Low: diamond 10, Fiery 10, Pharaoh 10, Slime 10, iron 9, Three-Bolt 9, Steeleaf 9, Phantom 8, Knightmetal 8, Arctic 8, Prismarine 8, Heavy 6, Warrior 5

Magic gear: Ars Elemental armor 40, Ars robes and Adam's Ars Plus armor 30, Dread and Primordial Mage robes 25, Iron's robes 15. Iron's weapons are 10 to 16, Magic From The East weapons 12, and the Twilight Spellbooks weapons 15.

Some gear already comes enchanted: Phantom armor from the Knight Phantom chest (level 20 to 30 enchants), Cornelia's Three-Bolt Helmet (treasure enchants), the Fiery Sword (Fire Aspect II) and the Steeleaf Pickaxe (Fortune II). The Twilight Forest knives come with their own enchants too.

## Ensorcellation (all 37)

Weapons:
- **Cavalier**: more damage while riding
- **Ender Disruption**: more damage to Ender mobs and stops them teleporting
- **Vigilante**: more damage to illagers
- **Outlaw**: more damage to villagers
- **Frost Aspect**: slows and weakens the target
- **Instigating**: extra damage if the target is at full health
- **Leech**: heals you when you kill a mob
- **Magic Edge**: a bit more damage, and it becomes magic damage (ignores armor)
- **Vorpal**: random powerful hits and decapitations
- **Curse of Mercy**: the weapon can't land a killing blow

Bows:
- **Quick Draw**: faster draw
- **Trueshot**: more accurate, faster arrows that pierce
- **Volley**: fires 3 arrows in an arc for 1
- **Hunter's Bounty**: chance of extra drops from animals

Armor:
- **Magic Protection**: less damage from magic
- **Displacement** (chest): randomly teleports attackers away
- **Flaming Rebuke** (chest): sets attackers on fire and knocks them back
- **Chilling Rebuke** (chest): slows and weakens attackers and knocks them back
- **Vitality** (chest): more max health
- **Reach** (chest): you can reach blocks from further away
- **Air Affinity** (helmet): no mining slowdown when you're off the ground
- **Insight** (helmet): more XP
- **Gourmand** (helmet): food fills you up more
- **Curse of Foolishness** (helmet): no XP gain
- **Frost Walker** (boots): freezes water and lava so you can walk on them

Shields:
- **Bulwark**: no knockback while blocking, and it can disable the attacker's weapon
- **Phalanx**: move faster while blocking

Tools:
- **Excavating**: mines extra blocks around the one you break
- **Smashing**: ores get smashed for more yield
- **Smelting**: auto-smelts what you mine
- **Tilling**: tills an area
- **Furrowing**: tills a line
- **Weeding**: clears plants while tilling

Fishing rods:
- **Angler's Bounty**: chance of extra catches
- **Pilfering**: can pull armor off whatever you hook

Anything:
- **Soulbound**: you keep the item when you die
- **Preservation**: repairs don't keep getting more expensive

## Alex's Caves enchantments (only work on that mod's items)

- **Raygun**: Energy Efficiency (uses charge slower), Solar (recharges in sunlight), X-Ray (beam goes through walls), Gamma Ray (stronger blue radiation)
- **Galena Gauntlet**: Field Extension (moves items further), Ferrous Haste (thrown items attack and mine faster), Crystallization (can also move crystal items like diamond tools)
- **Resistor Shield**: Heavy Slam (first slam hits harder), Arrow Inducting (deflected arrows turn into seeking arrows)
- **Primitive Club**: Swiftwood (swings much faster), Bonking (mobs can drop their heads), Dazing Sweep (stuns a bigger area)
- **Extinction Spear**: Chomping Spirit (stronger Tremorsaurus bite), Herd Phalanx (more Grottoceratops shields), Plummeting Flight (Subterranodon carries targets higher and longer)
- **Ortholance**: Flinging (launches you further), Second Wave (a second delayed wave), Sea Swing (swing sends a forward wave), Tsunami (one giant wave)
- **Sea Staff**: Bouncing Bolt (bolt bounces to a second target), Enveloping Bubble (traps the target in a drowning bubble), Soak Seeking (better homing), Triple Splash (fires 3 bolts), Seapairing (repairs itself when you're underwater)
- **Magic Conch**: Charting Call (summons more Deep Ones), Lasting Morale (they stay longer), Taxing Bellow (costs Deep One reputation instead of durability)
- **Dreadbow**: Dark Nock (fires faster), Precise Volley (arrows land more centrally), Relentless Darkness (fires a straight continuous stream instead), Shaded Respite (no arrow or durability cost in darkness), Twilight Perfection (perfectly timed shots glow red and do double damage)
- **Desolate Dagger**: Double Stab (two ghost blades), Impending Stab (ghost blade is delayed but hits harder), Sated Blade (ghost hits restore saturation)
- **Totem of Possession**: Sightless (control without line of sight), Rapid Possession (possessed mobs move faster), Detonating Death (possessed mobs explode before dying), Astral Transferring (swap possession to a healthier mob)
- **Candy Cane Hook**: Far Flung (launches further), Straight Hook (flies straight, ignores gravity), Sharp Cane (3 damage per level when it hooks a target)
- **Shot Gum**: Bouncy Ball (+2 bounces per level), Targeted Ricochet (bounces toward the nearest target), Triple Split (splits into 3 after the first hit), Explosive Flavor (one slow explosive gumball)
- **Sugar Staff**: Multiple Mint (+1 peppermint per level), Peppermint Punting (peppermints fly straight ahead), Seekcandy (peppermints chase what you look at), Humungous Hex (hexes get 1 block wider per level), Spell Lasting (hexes last 3 seconds longer per level)

## Other mods' enchantments

- **Create**: Capacity (more Backtank air), Potato Recovery (Potato Cannon ammo can be reused)
- **Farmer's Delight**: Backstabbing (more damage from behind, on knives)
- **Supplementaries**: Stasis (slingshot shots ignore gravity), Lumisene Fire Aspect and Lumisene Flame (extra lumisene fire damage on melee hits and arrows)
- **Twilight Forest**: Fire React (attackers can catch fire), Chill Aura (attackers can get Frosted), Destruction (the Block and Chain can break tougher blocks)
