- **BetterEnd**: End Veil (look at Endermen without making them angry)
- **BetterNether**: Obsidian Breaker (mine obsidian faster), Ruby's Fire (auto-smelting on pickaxes; flame and knockback on armor and swords)
- **Northstar**: Frostbite (frost damage on hit)
- **Northstar Curios Compat**: Space Walk (Earth gravity on other planets; level I also brings back normal fall damage)
- **Alex's Mobs**: Straddle Jump, Serpent Charmer, Lavawaxed and Returning Board, all for the Straddleboard (higher jumps, Bone Serpents stay neutral, fire resistance while riding, the board comes back when it breaks)
- **Obscure API (Aquamirae)**: Distance, Fast Spin and Mirror, for orbiting projectiles like the Maze Rose chakras (wider orbit, faster spin, or a duplicate at half damage)
- **Alex's Caves: Stuff & Torpedoes**: Universal Magnetism, for the Magnetic Helmet

## Magic mods' enchantments
- **Ars Nouveau**: Mana Boost and Mana Regen (armor, more max mana or faster regen), and Reactive (a chance to cast the item's inscribed spell when you use it or get hurt). These go on at the Enchanting Apparatus, not the enchanting table, one level at a time.
- **Ars Elemental**: Spell Reflection (Enchanter's Shield only, 25% chance per level to send spell projectiles back while blocking) and its own Soulbound (keep the item when you die).
- **Magic From The East**: Soul Flame, Ghostly Cold, Spiritual Focus, Wisely Will and Expanding for its spirit bows, and Inner Impact for the Soul Breaker and Spirit Crusher.
- **Apprentice's Codex**: around 19 enchants for its own magic gear, like Alacrity (shorter cooldowns), Surge (more spell power), Reservoir (more mana), Reflux (faster mana regen), Transcendence (higher spell levels), Plunder (more loot) and several for its flask, scepter and Mana Shield Charm.

Iron's Spells itself adds no enchantments. Its gear gets stronger through Upgrade Orbs at the Arcane Anvil instead (up to 3 per item).

-# The Enchantment Descriptions mod also shows short explanations on enchanted book tooltips in game.

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

# Powers and accessories

This covers every way to get player powers in the pack, grouped by what they do: flight, double jumps, dying without dying, base-wide buffs, all of it. Curio items go in accessory slots (`G`), so they stack with your armor.

## Powers by type

- **Flight** (strongest first): Aeternium Armored Elytra (armor and wings in one slot), the RFTools Flight module (creative flight inside the field), an Elytra in the Elytra Slot curio (keeps your chestplate), Tarantula Hawk Elytra, Crystalite Elytra, a ridden dragon (Dragon Mounts), Hood + Cloak of Darkness (10 second bursts), aircraft, and a tamed Bald Eagle (Falconry Glove). Magic adds Iron's Angel Wings spell, the Ars Ritual of Flight and Gliding thread, and the Cloud Steps thread (Adam's Ars Plus).
- **Jumping and falling** (strongest first): Cloud in a Bottle (double jump), Steampunk legs (double jump), Clark Wine (double jump), Bunny Hoppers (fall immunity plus jump), Rainbounce Boots, Steampunk boots, RFTools Featherfalling, Sunbird Blessing (stand near a Sunbird), Helium Flamingo (air swim).
- **Movement** (strongest first): Cave Centipede Leggings (wall climb), Grappling Squok or Candy Cane Hook (grapples), Aqua-Dashers (run on water and lava), Running Shoes, Panic Necklace, Rocky Chestplate (roll), Ortholance / Storm's Edge / Whisperwind dashes, Bosses'Rise `Z` dodge roll, Waystones, Dimensional Carver (teleport home).
- **Death protection** (strongest first): a Totem of Undying worn in the charm slot (Charm of Undying mod), Twilight Forest's Charm of Life (survive a death) and Charm of Keeping (keep your items), the Abyssal Heaume set (delays a fatal hit), Waxweaver (a lethal hit heals you to full instead), Chorus Totem (teleports you away from a fatal hit), the Soulbound enchant, and the Corpse mod (your items wait for you where you died). The Ars Undying thread cheats death once per sleep.
- **Tankiness** (strongest first): Crystal Heart (+max HP), the Vitality enchant, Stal Wine (+4 max HP), Solaris Wine (Health Boost), Divine armor (deflects a hit every 60 seconds), Steadfast Spikes (knockback immunity), Cross Necklace (longer invulnerability after a hit), Frostfall self-encase, RFTools Regeneration+ field, Farmer's Delight Comfort.
- **Damage boosts** (strongest first): Power Glove, Feral Claws (attack speed), Fire Gauntlet, Vampiric Glove (lifesteal), the Leech enchant, Plate of Fiery Snakes (Strength III plus Regen II for 5 minutes), Chocolate 113 (Strength V for 30 seconds), Raging (Brewin' & Chewin'), Apple Cider (Strength), Sun's Blessing (an Umvuthi trade).
- **Allies and summons** (strongest first): Sol Visage (10 Umvuthana), Magic Conch (a Deep One squad), Zombie Scepter, a tamed Tremorzilla, dragons, Tremorsaurus / Subterranodon / Vallumraptor tames, Alex's Mobs tames (Grizzly, Tarantula Hawk, Mantis Shrimp, Elephant, and more), Totem of Possession (control any mob). Magic adds Iron's Raise Dead, Summon Vex and Summon Swords, Magic From The East's summons, and Ars familiars.
- **Vision and detection** (strongest first): Night Vision Goggles, Echolocator (reveals caves), Ore Magnet (pulls ores to the surface), Steampunk helmet radar, Beholder (remote eye), Lava Vision potion, RFTools Night Vision field, Nature's Compass (finds biomes), Cave Maps.
- **Resistances** (strongest first): Hazmat suit (radiation), Anti-Radiation Suit, Obsidian Skull or Flame Pendant (fire), Antidote Vessel (shorter debuffs), Shock Pendant (lightning), Snorkel / Diving helmet / Cristel Wine (water breathing), Unsettling Kimono (undead stay neutral), Kitty Slippers (creepers avoid you), Gilded Netherite (piglins stay neutral).
- **Area buffs (base)**: the RFTools Environmental Controller covers Flight, Regeneration+, Speed+, Haste+, Saturation+, Featherfalling+, Peaceful (no hostile spawns), Water Breathing and Night Vision, all in one field.
- **Magic powers**: a lot of powers come from spells rather than items. Iron's Spells has Teleport, Blood Step, Frost Step, Angel Wings (temporary flight), Heal, Fortify, Oakskin, Invisibility, Evasion and Recall (warp to spawn). Ars Nouveau has Blink, Leap, Glide, Heal and Invisibility, plus the Ritual of Flight. Ars threads put perks like Undying and Gliding on your robes, and mana curios (Iron's rings, Ars Trinkets rings and lotuses, the Doppelganger Ring) raise your mana and cut cooldowns. The Magic sections list all of it.

## Artifacts: every accessory and where it drops

Artifacts can't be crafted. Mimics (chests that bite) always drop a random one. Otherwise each structure's chests have their own shortlist, and the percentage below is the chance per chest.

- **Crystal Heart** (+max health): Bastion treasure 65%, End City 30%, Pillager Outpost 25%.
- **Chorus Totem** (teleports you away from a fatal hit): Woodland Mansion 40%, End City 30%.
- **Cloud in a Bottle** (double jump): Underwater Ruin 45%, End City 30%, Outpost 25%.
- **Power Glove** (+melee damage): Underwater Ruin 45%, Stronghold 30%, Outpost 25%.
- **Feral Claws** (+attack speed): Buried Treasure 25%, Shipwreck 15%.
- **Vampiric Glove** (melee lifesteal): Mineshaft 30%, Desert Pyramid 20%.
- **Fire Gauntlet** (melee sets targets on fire): Bastion 65%, Underwater Ruin 45%.
- **Steadfast Spikes** (knockback immunity): Bastion 65%, Outpost 25%.
- **Cross Necklace** (longer invulnerability after a hit): Bastion 65%, Underwater Ruin 45%.
- **Antidote Vessel** (much shorter negative effects): Bastion 65%, Stronghold 30%.
- **Panic Necklace** (speed after taking damage): Bastion 65%, Mineshaft 30%.
- **Golden Hook** (+XP from mobs): Bastion 65%, Buried Treasure 25%.
- **Bunny Hoppers** (no fall damage plus a higher jump): Jungle Temple 35%, Hoglin stable 25%.
- **Aqua-Dashers** (sprint across fluids): Mineshaft 30%, Stronghold 30%.
- **Running Shoes** (sprint speed plus step height): Nether Bridge 15%, Shipwreck 15%.
- **Helium Flamingo** (timed air-swimming): End City 30%, Stronghold 30%.
- **Superstitious Hat** (+Looting): Underwater Ruin 45%, Mineshaft/Stronghold 30%.
- **Lucky Scarf** (+Fortune): Underwater Ruin 45%, Igloo/Stronghold 30%.
- **Digging Claws** (+mining tier and speed): Mineshaft 30%, Buried Treasure 25%.
- **Pickaxe Heater** (auto-smelts mined ore): Mineshaft/Stronghold 30%, Hoglin stable 25%.
- **Onion Ring** (mining speed after eating): Mineshaft 30%, Desert Pyramid 20%.
- **Universal Attractor** (item magnet): Desert Pyramid 20%, Ruined Portal/Shipwreck 15%.
- **Night Vision Goggles** (night vision): Mineshaft 30%, Nether Bridge/Ruined Portal/Shipwreck 15%.
- **Snorkel** (water breathing): Underwater Ruin 45%, Stronghold 30%.
- **Flippers** (fast swimming): Underwater Ruin 45%, Buried Treasure 25%.
- **Charm of Sinking** (walk freely underwater): Desert Pyramid/Village Temple 20%.
- **Obsidian Skull** (temporary fire immunity after catching fire): Mineshaft 30%, Ruined Portal 15%.
- **Flame Pendant** (fire resistance plus burns attackers): Hoglin stable 25%, Desert Pyramid 20%.
- **Shock Pendant** (lightning immunity plus zaps attackers): Desert Pyramid 20%, Ancient City 15%.
- **Thorn Pendant** (damages attackers): Desert Pyramid 20%, Ruined Portal 15%.
- **Pocket Piston** (+knockback dealt): Outpost 25%, Village Toolsmith 15%.
- **Rooted Boots** (restores hunger while walking on grass): Jungle Temple 35%, Stronghold 30%.
- **Snowshoes** (walk on powder snow): Igloo 30%, Ancient City ice box 20%.
- **Kitty Slippers** (creepers avoid you): Jungle Temple 35%, Buried Treasure 25%.
- **Scarf of Invisibility** (invisibility): Outpost 25%, Desert Pyramid 20%.
- **Umbrella** (glide slowly, or use as a shield): Buried Treasure 25%, Desert Pyramid 20%.
- **Cowboy Hat** (faster mounts): Hoglin stable 25%, Nether Bridge 15%.
- **Angler's Hat** (better fishing): Underwater Ruin 45%, Jungle Temple 35%.
- **Villager Hat** (cheaper trades): Igloo 30%, village houses 5 to 8%.
- **Everlasting Beef** (infinite food): 0.2% from cows, Hoglin stable 25%.
- **Whoopee Cushion** (joke item): Spawn bonus chest 100%.

## Ensorcellation enchantments

This mod adds 38 extra enchantments through the normal enchanting table, books and villagers. The standouts are Soulbound (keep the item on death), Smelting, Vorpal, Trueshot, Volley, Leech, Vitality, Reach, Preservation (no rising anvil cost) and Excavating.

- **Soulbound** (on anything): the item stays with you on death.
- **Preservation** (on anything): removes the growing anvil repair cost.
- **Vorpal** (on weapons): random powerful strikes, decapitations.
- **Leech** (on weapons): heals you on kill.
- **Instigating** (on weapons): extra damage against full-HP targets.
- **Magic Edge** (on weapons): a little extra damage, dealt as magic.
- **Frost Aspect** (on weapons): slows and weakens the target.
- **Cavalier** (on weapons): more damage while mounted.
- **Ender Disruption / Vigilante / Outlaw** (on weapons): bonus damage vs endermen / illagers / villagers.
- **Trueshot** (on bows): accurate, faster arrows that pierce.
- **Volley** (on bows): 3 arrows for the cost of 1.
- **Quick Draw / Hunter's Bounty** (on bows): faster draw, or extra animal drops.
- **Vitality** (on chestplates): +max health.
- **Reach** (on chestplates): +interaction distance.
- **Displacement / Flaming or Chilling Rebuke** (on chestplates): teleports, burns, or freezes attackers.
- **Magic Protection** (on armor): less magic damage.
- **Air Affinity / Insight / Gourmand** (on helmets): no airborne mining penalty, +XP, or better food.
- **Frost Walker, improved** (on boots): freezes water and lava.
- **Bulwark / Phalanx** (on shields): no knockback plus disarm, or move fast while blocking.
- **Smelting / Smashing / Excavating** (on tools): auto-smelt, better ore yield, or area mining.
- **Tilling / Furrowing / Weeding** (on hoes): area tilling, clears plants.
- **Angler's Bounty / Pilfering** (on fishing rods): more catches, or steal armor off hooked mobs.
- **Curse of Foolishness / Curse of Mercy** (curses): no XP gain, or can't land killing blows.

> Shield Expansion adds shield tiers and a parry: block right as a hit lands. Parry timing is strict in this pack (lenient parry is off), and shields go on cooldown after blocking.

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

# How to get every weapon and armor

This covers the full crafting chains for the major gear, straight from the pack's recipe files. Every non-vanilla ingredient is traced back to its source: ore, mob drop, structure loot, or boss. Exact grids show up in JEI (`R`). Station shorthand: craft means a crafting table, smith means a smithing table (base + addition + template), metal anvil means a BetterEnd or BetterNether anvil of the right tier, alloy means the End Stone Smelter, and infuse means a BetterEnd Infusion Pedestal ritual (the input goes in the middle, catalysts go on the 8 surrounding pedestals).

## BetterEnd

### Thallasium (tier 1 End metal)
- Ore: Thallasium Ore spawns in the End, from 8 above bedrock to 8 below the height limit, evenly across that range (see the Ores section).
- Ingot: smelt or blast Thallasium Ore or raw Thallasium in a normal furnace (200 ticks smelting, 100 blasting) for 1 ingot. You can also alloy 2 items tagged as Thallasium alloying material in the End Stone Smelter for 3 ingots, turn 9 nuggets into 1 ingot, or break a block into 9 ingots.
- End Stone Smelter (the alloying station): craft it from End Stone Bricks around the edges, Thallasium Ingots, and a vanilla furnace in the middle.
- Thallasium Anvil: craft it from Thallasium Block or Tile plus Thallasium Ingot.
- Forged Plate / tool heads: smith on the Thallasium Anvil (needs anvil level 2, tool level 2). 1 ingot makes a forged plate, 3 ingots make an axe head, and the other tool heads work the same way.
- Armor (helmet, chestplate and boots use Forged Plate; leggings the same way): craft normal armor shapes from Forged Plates.
- Tools and the hammer: smith a head plus a stick, using the handle attachment smithing template, on a vanilla smithing table.
- Upgrade Smithing Template: found in End Village chest loot (weight 8 out of 22, 1 to 2 rolls). You can duplicate it by crafting the template with a Thallasium Ingot and 6 sticks for 2 templates.

### Terminite (tier 2 End metal)
- Ingot: alloy Iron Ingot with Ender Dust, or Thallasium Ingot with Ender Dust, in the End Stone Smelter, 450 ticks, for 2.5 XP. There's no raw ore, it's a pure alloy metal.
- Ender Dust: smith on any metal anvil (this damages the anvil): an Ender Pearl becomes Ender Dust (5 damage to the anvil), or an Ender Shard becomes Ender Dust (3 damage; Ender Shard is an End loot/drop item).
- Forged plates, tools and armor follow the same pattern as Thallasium, but use Terminite items and a Terminite-tier anvil (crafted the same way, from Terminite block or ingot).

### Aeternium (top-tier End metal)
- Ingot: alloy Terminite Ingot with a Netherite Ingot, 850 ticks, for 4.5 XP, in the End Stone Smelter, so Aeternium gear needs a Nether trip for Netherite too. It also drops directly as loot: End biome chests (chorus forest, common, foggy mushroomland, lantern woods, shadow forest, umbrella jungle biomes, weight 1 out of 5, 0 to 4 count) and End Village bonus loot (weight 8 out of 14, 1 to 2 rolls, times 1 to 4 count, the best farm spot).
- Aeternium Upgrade Smithing Template: End Village template-loot chest, weight 2 out of 22, 1 to 2 rolls (rarer than Thallasium's).
- Armor, tools and hammer: smith a Terminite-tier item with an Aeternium Ingot at the Aeternium Upgrade Smithing Template, i.e. upgrade Terminite gear on a vanilla smithing table like a Netherite upgrade.
- Aeternium Armored Elytra: smith a vanilla Elytra with an Aeternium Ingot at the Aeternium Upgrade Smithing Template.

### Crystalite (infusion-only endgame upgrade, not a smithing-table recipe)
This needs the Infusion Pedestal: craft it from an Ender Eye, 2 Ender Pearls, and 2 Obsidian. Put the input item on the pedestal's center and catalyst items on the surrounding sub-pedestals (north, south, east, west, and the diagonals) for however many ticks the recipe calls for.
- Crystalite Helmet/Boots: infuse an undamaged Terminite Helmet or Boots with 1 Amber Gem (north) and 2 Crystal Shards (east/west), 150 ticks.
- Crystalite Leggings: same plus 3 Crystal Shards (east/west/south), 225 ticks.
- Crystalite Chestplate: same plus 4 Crystal Shards (all diagonals) and an Amber Gem (north), 300 ticks.
- Crystalite Elytra: infuse a vanilla Elytra with an Amber Gem (north), 4 Crystal Shards (diagonals), and 3 Enchanted Membrane (east/south/west), 500 ticks, the longest infusion in the mod.
- Enchanted Membrane: infuse a Phantom Membrane with 4 Crystal Shards (north/east/south/west), 75 ticks.
- Amber Gem: craft 2×2 of raw Amber (mined from amber ore/vein in the End) into gems, or get 4 gems from 1 Amber Block.
- Crystal Shards: no crafting recipe, mined directly from natural Crystal formations in End biomes (Crystal/Lucernia-type biomes), a pure world-gen drop.
- Note: Crystalite upgrades a Terminite item, not Aeternium, so your best-in-slot choice is between Aeternium (higher raw stats) and Crystalite (its own effect line, see the Weapons/Armor sections for stats). Pick one branch per piece, since Crystalite consumes the Terminite item itself.

## BetterNether

### Cincinnasite
- Ore: Cincinnasite Ore, in the Nether, from 10 above bedrock to 10 below the height limit, evenly across that range (see the Ores section).
- Ingot: smelt or blast Cincinnasite Ore (200/100 ticks) straight to an ingot, or smelt/blast a Cincinnasite shard to an ingot, or craft 4 Cincinnasite Forged into 4 ingots (shapeless). Forged comes from smelting/blasting a Cincinnasite Block, or crafting a 2×2 of ingots into forged.
- Tools and armor: plain craft from ingots plus a Nether Reed Stem handle (a sword, for example, takes 2 ingots and 1 stem). No special anvil needed, Cincinnasite is a base-tier vanilla-style craft, not a BCLib alloy metal.
- Cincinnasite-Diamond upgrade (tool/armor upgrade): smith a Cincinnasite item with a Diamond at the Cincinnasite Diamond Upgrade Smithing Template on a vanilla smithing table.
- Template source: Nether Fortress "city" chests (weight 3 out of 8), city_common chests (weight 3 out of 8), Library chests (weight 4 out of 10, 2 to 3 rolls), Wither Tower chests (weight 9 out of 14, the best source, up to 3 per roll). Duplicable by crafting the template with a Cincinnasite Ingot and 6 diamonds for 2 templates.

### Nether Ruby / Flaming Ruby
- Ore: 3 variants, all in the Nether and all dropping the Nether Ruby Ore item family: regular Nether Ruby Ore (Y from 32 above bedrock to 32 below the height limit), Large Nether Ruby Ore (Y from 32 above bedrock to 0 below the height limit), Rare Nether Ruby Ore (trapezoid distribution, Y from 70 above bedrock to 0 below the height limit), and Soul Nether Ruby Ore (Y from 32 above bedrock to 0 below the height limit). Smelt or mine it to get Nether Ruby gems (a block converts to 9 gems and back, both ways craftable).
- Tools/armor: plain craft from Nether Ruby gems and a Nether Reed Stem, same pattern family as Cincinnasite, base tier.
- Flaming Ruby upgrade: smith a Nether Ruby item with a Sculk Catalyst at the Flaming Ruby Upgrade Smithing Template. You need a Sculk Catalyst (vanilla, from Deep Dark/Warden fights or trading) as the upgrade material, not another gem.
- Template source: Library chests (weight 1 out of 10, 2 to 3 rolls), Wither Tower chest (weight 1 out of 1, 1 to 2 rolls), and the Wither Tower bonus chest (weight 1 out of 1, 1 to 4 rolls, the best and most reliable source, tied to beating the Wither Tower's boss). Duplicable the same way: template plus a Nether Ruby and 6 diamonds for 2 templates.
- Both the Nether Ruby and Flaming Ruby lines run parallel to Cincinnasite/Cincinnasite-Diamond as an independent tier. Flaming Ruby is generally the stronger of BetterNether's two upgrade lines (fire-immunity themed, see the Weapons/Armor sections).

## Twilight Forest (plus Twilight's Flavors & Delight, TF Dungeons & Villages)
Boss HP, attacks and drops are in the Twilight Forest section. This part is just the crafting chains.
- Naga armor (chest and legs only, 2 pieces): craft 9 (chest) or 7 (legs) Naga Scale in the standard chest/legs pattern. The item carries a baked-in enchant (Fire Protection III on the chest, Protection III on the legs). Naga Scale: a Naga boss drop (6 to 11 per kill), or cut a Naga Trophy on a cutting board with any knife for 9 scales (a Twilight's Delight recipe).
- Ironwood Ingot: smelt or blast raw Ironwood (found as Hollow Hills entry loot, a bundle of liveroot, raw iron and a gold nugget), or craft 9 from an Ironwood Block. Ironwood armor and tools: plain craft from ingots, no special station.
- Steeleaf Ingot: craft 9 from a Steeleaf Block (compressed from Steeleaf leaves, gathered in the Dark Forest). There's no ore or smelting path, just a leaf-to-block-to-ingot loop.
- Knightmetal: an ingot comes from a Knightmetal Block (9 per block) or from armor shards (a 0 to 2 drop from Blockchain Goblins in the Highlands) that combine into ingots. There's no direct craft recipe for turning a shard straight into an ingot in this pack; treat the shard as the raw material and the ingot as the refined form. Tools, armor and the ring are a plain craft from ingots plus a wooden rod (the ring uses all 4 sides of the grid).
- Fiery Ingot: craft 1 Fiery Vial (a tagged item) with 1 Iron Ingot (shapeless), or get 9 from a Fiery Block. Fiery Vial has no crafting recipe found; it's bottled from Fiery Blood (a Hydra drop, 7 to 10 per kill) through a Twilight Forest-internal mechanic (fill the vial at the Fire Swamp lava/blood pool, per the wiki). Treat the Hydra kill as the real gate here.
- Arctic armor: craft from Arctic Fur (tag), farmed from Arctic-biome mobs (Penguins), or get 9 from an Arctic Fur Block.
- Yeti armor: craft from Alpha Yeti Fur (all 4 pieces, with baked-in Protection II, and the boots also get Feather Falling IV). Fur is an Alpha Yeti boss drop (6 per kill), or cut an Alpha Yeti Trophy on a cutting board for 9 fur plus a 50% chance of 4 Ice Bombs.
- Charm of Life I: not craftable, chest loot only (Aurora Room weight 1 out of 8, 2 rolls; Dark Tower Key chest weight 1 out of 4; Fancy Well weight 75 out of 300; Hollow Hill tiers 2 and 3; Stronghold Room; Tower Room; Tree Cache). Charm of Life II is a shapeless craft of 4 Charm of Life I.
- Charm of Keeping I: not craftable, chest loot (Aurora Cache, Basement, Dark Tower Cache/Key, Foundation Basement, Hollow Hill 3, Labyrinth Dead End/Room, Stronghold Cache, Tower Room, Tree Cache) plus a guaranteed drop from the Maze Slime. Charm of Keeping II is 4 Charm I, and III is 4 Charm II, both shapeless crafts.
- Ore Magnet: not craftable, Hollow Hill chest loot only (hill 1/2 weight 1 out of 5, 2 rolls; hill 3 weight 1 out of 6, 2 rolls).
- Moonworm Queen: not craftable, a rare Hollow Hill 3 chest drop (weight 25 out of 370) or from the Dark Tower's "tower room" chest (weight 25 out of 750, about 3.3%).
- Uncrafting Table: not craftable at all, a naturally generating block found in Hollow Hills loot rooms (mine it with any pickaxe to move it). It's confirmed enabled in this pack. Right-click to reverse a crafting recipe, which costs XP (the cost multiplier is set to 1.0 in this pack), and a few recipes are blacklisted, like turning a giant log back into oak planks.
- Minotaur Axe: the golden-tier version drops from regular Labyrinth Minotaurs, and the Diamond Minotaur Axe is a guaranteed 100% drop from the Minoshroom boss (weight 1 out of 1 in its loot table).
- Knightmetal Block and Ball (the flail): no crafting recipe was found for this, it's likely a code-registered special recipe or Uncrafting-Table-exclusive craft. Flag this one as uncertain.
- Lamp of Cinders: not craftable, only found in "Troll Vault" loot rooms that specifically contain the lamp (guaranteed once you find that vault variant, in Troll Caves/Highlands).
- Magic tree cores (Minewood, Sortingwood, Timewood-Clock, Heart of Transformation): these are world-generated blocks, not crafted or looted items. Each grows naturally as the trunk base of its rare magic tree (Mining, Sorting, Time and Transformation groves scattered through the forest). Mine the core block with a pickaxe to relocate and replant it and start your own farm. Transformation Powder (a separate consumable) is craftable though: a Twilight's Delight cooking recipe using Nether Wart, a Fermented Spider Eye, a Pufferfish, a Poisonous Potato, a Rotten Tomato and Borer Essence, cooked for 800 ticks in a Farmer's Delight-style pot on a canvas, or found as chest loot (Fancy Well, Graveyard, Hollow Hill 1 to 3, Tower Room).
- Twilight's Delight knives (craft from 1 ingot of the matching tier plus 1 wooden rod, no other station needed): Ironwood, Steeleaf, Knightmetal and Fiery knives all work this way.

## Aquamirae
Boss and mob info is already in the Boss Compendium, this adds the exact recipes. Everything below is a plain crafting-table recipe, no special station.
- Three-Bolt Suit: the helmet is a guaranteed 100% drop from the Ghost of Captain Cornelia (pre-enchanted, with treasure enchants level 10 to 30). The Suit (chestplate), Leggings and Boots craft from Iron Blocks and Leather only, no boss material needed: Suit takes 3 Iron Blocks, 2 Oxygen Tanks and 4 Leather; Leggings take 3 Iron Blocks and 4 Leather; Boots take 2 Iron Blocks and 2 Leather. The Oxygen Tank itself is a Cornelia drop-table item (see the Boss Compendium).
- Terrible set: every piece crafts from Angler's Fang, Fin and Iron Ingot (the chestplate also needs 1 Ship Graveyard Echo). Angler's Fang: an Anglerfish drop (weight 25 out of 100, 1 to 2 per kill). Fin: an Eel/Maw drop (a common Ice Maze fish drop). Terrible Sword: craft from 2 Ship Graveyard Echo, 1 Angler's Fang, 2 Iron Ingot, 1 Ender Eye and 1 stick.
- Ship Graveyard Echo (a key mid-tier material): drops from Eel (guaranteed, 4 to 12 per kill, the best farm), Captain Cornelia (weight 1 out of 3, 1 to 2), Anglerfish (weight 5 out of 100), Abyssal Scyphoid (weight 5 out of 100), Tortured Soul (weight 1 out of 10), Maw (rare), and Mother of the Maze (0 to 2). It's also frozen chest loot (3 to 6) and maze_common_chest loot (weight 1 out of 13, 2 to 4).
- Abyssal Amethyst, the core Abyssal-tier material, is both a Mother of the Maze drop (2 rolls of 2 to 4) and directly craftable: 4 Ship Graveyard Echo plus 1 vanilla Amethyst Shard. That means the whole Abyssal set is farmable through Eel-grinding for echoes, without ever needing to re-kill Mother of the Maze, a solid progression shortcut.
- Abyssal set (every piece also needs Netherite Ingots, so a Nether/Bastion trip is required):
  - Boots: 2 Abyssal Amethyst, 2 Netherite Ingot.
  - Heaume (helmet): 3 Abyssal Amethyst, 2 Netherite Ingot.
  - Leggings: 5 Abyssal Amethyst, 2 Netherite Ingot.
  - Brigantine (chestplate): 5 Abyssal Amethyst, 2 Netherite Ingot, 1 Astral Dust.
  - Tiara: not turned up directly, but it's the alternate helmet slot to the Heaume, a mutually exclusive choice (Heaume gives a fatal-damage delay/near-totem effect with the full set, Tiara gives an AoE blind and slow on nearby hostiles with the full set; see the Boss Compendium for the split).
- Astral Dust: no crafting recipe anywhere in the pack, obtained purely as Frozen Chest loot (weight 1 out of 8, a small stack when it rolls). This gates the Brigantine specifically behind opening a Frozen Chest.
- Frozen Key: a 100% guaranteed drop from the Ghost of Captain Cornelia. Frozen Chest: the structure/container the key opens (found in the Ice Maze's frozen ship wrecks); confirmed loot includes Astral Dust, Ship Graveyard Echo (3 to 6), Dagger of Greed (about 20%), and a guaranteed Rune of the Storm (per the Boss Compendium).
- Divider: craft from 2 Ship Graveyard Echo, 4 Abyssal Amethyst, 2 Netherite Ingot and 1 stick.
- Whisper of the Abyss: craft from 2 Ship Graveyard Echo, 3 Abyssal Amethyst, 2 Netherite Ingot and 2 sticks.
- Fin Cutter: craft from 2 Ship Graveyard Echo, 1 Abyssal Amethyst, 1 diamond, 3 Fin and 1 stick.
- Remnant's Saber: craft from 2 Sharp Bones, 1 leather and 1 stick, a cheap early-game recipe with no boss material. Sharp Bones: a Mother of the Maze drop (6 to 24) or a general Ice Maze skeleton-type mob drop.
- Poisoned Blade: craft from 4 Angler's Fang, 2 Iron Ingot and 1 stick.
- Maze Rose: craft from 4 Abyssal Amethyst and 4 diamonds.
- Poisoned Chakra: craft from 4 Angler's Fang and 4 diamonds.
- Shell Horn: found exploring frozen ship wrecks (a loot item, not a Cornelia drop). Use it near water inside an Ice Maze to summon Ghost of Captain Cornelia.
- Coral Lance / Sweet Lance: confirmed absent from this pack's extracted recipes and loot, likely unobtainable (per the Boss Compendium, flag as cut content).

## Simply Swords
Almost none of this mod's loot sits in static loot tables. It uses a code-side loot-injection system instead, the mod's own settings confirm this: if the "add weapons to loot tables" option is on, Simply Swords injects its loot into any loot table with "chests" in its filepath (including other mods' loot tables), and items can also be injected into entity loot tables (the Wither at 5%, the Ender Dragon at 50% chance per kill, in this pack's config). Four rarity tiers roll independently per eligible chest, and this pack's exact values are: standard weight 1.0%, rare weight 0.4%, runic weight 0.7%, unique weight 0.1%. All 43 unique weapons are individually toggled on in this pack, including the Slumbering Lichblade and Dormant Relic. Village-house chests are explicitly blacklisted (every village chest set to weight 0.0), so don't farm villages for this mod's loot on BSCraft.
- Runic Tablet: also purely loot-injected, governed by the same general loot modifiers per the config's own comment ("standard iron to diamond gear, and Runic Tablets, are controlled by the loot modifiers in the general config"). It drops from any eligible chest or entity roll like the weapons do, not from a fixed table. You use it at a vanilla smithing table to etch runic powers onto Netherite weapons (the Runic Grimoire Patchouli book, under "Rune Etching"), to re-roll Runic weapons, and to repair Unique weapons.
- Lichblade chain (each step crafts from 2 Runic Tablet plus the previous item and the listed extras):
  - Slumbering Lichblade: a loot-injected starting item, no recipe.
  - Waking Lichblade: 4 Soul Sand, the Slumbering Lichblade, 2 Runic Tablet, 1 Skeleton Skull.
  - Awakened Lichblade: 4 Soul Sand, the Waking Lichblade, 2 Runic Tablet, 1 Nether Star.
- Relic chain (same 2-Runic-Tablet-per-step pattern):
  - Dormant Relic: a loot-injected starting item, no recipe.
  - Righteous Relic: 4 Gold Ingot, the Dormant Relic, 2 Runic Tablet, 1 Enchanted Golden Apple. It then upgrades to Sunfire: 4 Enchanted Golden Apple, the Righteous Relic, 2 Runic Tablet, 1 Dragon's Breath (this needs both a Wither and an Ender Dragon kill across the chain).
  - Tainted Relic: 4 Phantom Membrane, the Dormant Relic, 2 Runic Tablet, 1 Ender Eye. It then upgrades to Harbinger: 4 Ender Eye, the Tainted Relic, 2 Runic Tablet, 1 Sculk Catalyst.
- Decaying Relic into Magiscythe / Magispear / Magiblade: not a crafting-table recipe at all. The Decaying Relic is a loot-injected item that randomly transforms while you carry it, depending on where you explore, per its own tooltips: the base item says to keep it in your inventory while exploring the deepest, darkest depths of the Overworld, and in-game event messages confirm each outcome has its own trigger location: Magiscythe reacts to "this area," Magiblade "quivers" and gravitates to the block beneath you (implying a specific block or structure), and Magispear is "influenced by something" reacting to the darkness (likely deep dark or cave related). In practice: carry a Decaying Relic and explore varied deep underground terrain (caves, deep dark, ravines) until it announces a transformation. The exact trigger conditions are code-side and unconfirmed from JSON, so flag this as uncertain and worth checking a wiki or community source.
- Contained Remnant: craft from 4 Iron Ingot in the corners, 3 Empowered Remnant in the center column, and 2 glass on the sides. Empowered Remnant comes from smelting any item tagged as a Simply Swords unique (melt down any Unique-tier weapon you don't want) in a furnace, 1000 ticks, for 35 XP. Its own tooltip says it then transforms further while carried alongside a Runic Tablet in the End, the same "carry and explore" mechanic as the Relic and Lichblade lines.

## Immersive Armors (all 10 sets, every piece a plain crafting-table recipe, no boss drops)
All of these are confirmed pure-vanilla-material crafts (the chestplate recipe is shown below; helmet, leggings and boots reuse the same core material in the standard armor-grid shapes).
- Bone: chestplate needs 8 bone in a full ring around a hollow center.
- Wither: chestplate needs 8 Nether Brick around 1 Bone Chestplate (this upgrades the Bone set).
- Warrior: chestplate needs 3 Iron Ingot, 2 leather and 1 leather chestplate (this upgrades a leather chestplate).
- Heavy: chestplate needs 2 Iron Block and 4 Iron Ingot.
- Robe: chestplate needs 8 wool (any color) around 1 blaze powder center.
- Slime: chestplate needs 8 slime balls.
- Divine: chestplate needs 4 Gold Ingot, 1 Robe Chestplate and 1 Totem of Undying (this consumes a Totem, an endgame upgrade of the Robe set).
- Prismarine: chestplate needs 8 prismarine shards around 1 Heart of the Sea center.
- Wooden: chestplate needs 8 logs (any type).
- Steampunk: chestplate needs 4 Gold Ingot, 1 compass, 1 clock and 1 piston.
In practice: Bone, Slime, Wooden, Prismarine and Heavy are early, cheap, vanilla-material sets. Wither upgrades from Bone, and Divine upgrades from Robe and burns a Totem of Undying (best in its category, see the Weapons/Armor sections for stats), so plan your Totem farming (Elder Guardian trades, Evoker drops, or Create-based Totem loops, see the Create section below) before committing one.

## Armor of the Ages (3 sets plus 1 hat, all crafting-table upgrades)
- Iron Plate: craft from 6 Iron Block and 1 Iron Chestplate, upgrading a vanilla Iron Chestplate. The other pieces follow the same "iron block ring around the vanilla iron piece" pattern.
- Exalted Aurum: craft from 1 red wool, 6 Gold Block and 1 Netherite Chestplate, upgrading a Netherite Chestplate, i.e. this is a post-Nether endgame set (6 gold blocks is worth 54 gold ingots per piece).
- Holy: craft from 4 Gold Block, 3 Iron Block and 1 Netherite Chestplate, also upgrading a Netherite piece, a mixed gold-and-iron shell.
- Bamboo Hat: craft from 4 bamboo, a novelty cheap helmet-slot item unrelated to the 3 main sets.
All three main sets consume a full vanilla top-tier armor piece per slot (Iron or Netherite) plus a shell of gold or iron blocks, no ore or mob-drop material, it's purely a spend-blocks-to-upgrade system.

## Mowzie's Mobs
Boss encounters and stats are already in the Boss Compendium, this adds exact recipes and loot.
- Blowgun: craft from 4 bamboo in a cross plus 2 string, a cheap early ranged weapon, darts poison for 40 ticks.
- Spear: craft from 1 flint, 1 feather and 2 sticks, a cheap early weapon.
- Naga Fang Dagger: craft from 1 Naga Fang, 1 Iron Ingot and 1 stick. Naga Fang is dropped by the regular (Twilight Forest) Naga mob, a cross-mod ingredient link, not necessarily the TF Naga boss specifically (though it likely also qualifies).
- Earthrend Gauntlet: a guaranteed drop (weight 1 out of 1) from the sculptor_test entity loot table, obtained during or at the end of Tongbi the Sculptor's mountain-peak platforming trial (the same encounter that pays out Geomancer pieces), rather than a separate source as sometimes claimed elsewhere.
- Geomancer set (Staff, Beads, Robe, Belt, Sandals): not craftable, 1 random piece per successful completion of Tongbi's trial (pay 1 Bluff Rod to start, repeat climbs for the full set).
- Umvuthana Masks (Fury, Fear, Rage, Bliss, Misery, Faith): each is a 100% guaranteed drop from killing the matching named Umvuthana variant mob (Umvuthana Grove, savanna biome), and also a rare secondary roll in Umvuthana Grove chest loot (weight 1 out of 51 each, 3 to 8 rolls per chest, so low single-digit percent per chest, but it stacks with repeated looting).

## Bosses'Rise
- Dragon Bone: a guaranteed drop (weight 1 out of 1) from the Infernal Dragon (Ashlord), 8 to 19 per kill, the only source.
- Dragon Bones armor (Boots, Chestplate, Leggings, no helmet exists): craft each piece from 4 Dragon Bone in a 2×2. It's cheap once you have the bones, but each piece needs its own set of about 4 to 6 bones from repeat Infernal Dragon kills.
- Kraken Tooth: a guaranteed drop (weight 1 out of 1) from the Kraken boss, 6 to 8 per kill. Kraken Trident: craft from 3 Kraken Tooth and 2 wooden rods, comes pre-enchanted with Loyalty I baked into the recipe.
- Knight armor (Helmet, Chestplate, Leggings, Boots): not a direct boss drop, found in Dragon Tower chest loot (all 4 pieces present) and the Underworld Arena "rare" chest loot (all 4 pieces, each weight 1 in their pool).
- Underworld Arena Key: a guaranteed 100% drop from both the Soul Knight Wither Skeleton and the Soul Skeleton (the arena's guard mobs), also spawner-tied guaranteed loot within the arena itself, plus a rare bonus roll in the Underworld Arena Vault chest (weight 2904 out of 29109, about 10%, 0 to 4 count). In practice, just fight through the arena's wither/soul skeletons, since the key drops are guaranteed, not RNG-gated.
- Ancient Trial Key: guaranteed (weight 1 out of 1) from a dedicated spawner loot table, tied to defeating or looting whatever guardian spawner gates the Ancient Trial entrance (structure-specific, kill the trial's guard spawn to get it, not a general mob drop).

## The Graveyard
- Bone Dagger: craft from 3 bone in a vertical column, a trivial early weapon.
- Corruption (a key mid-tier reagent): drops from Crypt-biome hostiles, the Acolyte, Corrupted Pillager and Corrupted Vindicator (all weight 1 out of 1, guaranteed 1 per kill with a Looting bonus), and the Ghoul (weight 1 out of 2, 0 to 1). Also Crypt chest loot (crypt_loot weight 10 out of 60, 2 rolls; crypt_urn_loot weight 10 out of 171).
- Dark Iron Ingot: a shapeless craft of 1 Corruption and 1 Iron Ingot, a cheap "infuse iron with Corruption" recipe, not a separate ore. Also found directly as Crypt chest loot (weight 10 out of 93, 1 to 4 count).
- Ominous Bone Staff Fragments (Lower, Middle, Upper): these are placed blocks found in the world, not items with a craft or mob-drop path. They self-drop when mined (each block's own loot table, weight 1 out of 1) as part of exploring the Lich Prison structure, and collecting them is the stated prerequisite for engaging the Lich boss (per the Boss Compendium).
- Staff of Zatraq'gul / Nortoq'gul / Azrata'gul / Mynakta'gul / Xaphnok'gul (the 5 colored Bone Staves): these are the Lich boss's actual weapon reward, 1 random staff per kill (each weight 1 out of 5, guaranteed exactly one per kill, repeat kills for a specific color).
- Vial of Blood: has its own advancement, but no crafting recipe or loot table entry was found anywhere in the extracted 3.1 data. It's obtained through an in-world or code-side mechanic not visible in JSON (consistent with the Boss Compendium's note on "villager blood farming" as Lich-fight prep), so treat this as uncertain and worth confirming from a wiki or in-book source.

## Alex's Caves
The Alex's Caves section already has crafting grids for the Dreadbow, Raygun, Hazmat set, Extinction Spear, Totem of Possession, Hood/Cloak of Darkness, Rainbounce Boots, Candy Cane Hook, Resistor Shield and Galena Gauntlet. This section just fills in the deeper sub-ingredient sources.
- Polymer Plate (Hazmat, Raygun and Remote Detonator material): a shapeless craft of Toxic Paste, Radon Bottle, Sulfur Dust and Iron Ingot, making 4 plates. Fully automatable through Create: Alex's Caves Compat sequenced assembly (an iron sheet, then deploy Radon Bottle, then deploy Toxic Paste, then deploy Sulfur Dust, then press).
- Uranium Rod (Raygun ammo, Nuclear Bomb, Nuclear Furnace fuel): craft from 4 raw Uranium, 4 Iron Ingot and 1 Blaze Rod. Also drops directly as block loot (mine a world-generated rod, weight 1 out of 1) and as Toxic Ruins chest loot (weight 3 out of 237, 8 to 10 count when it rolls).
- Fissile Core (Raygun and Nuclear Bomb): not directly player-craftable, it drops from the Nucleeper mob (weight 1 out of 1, 0 to 1 per kill, boosted by Looting), or gets automated through Create Oppenheimered's superheated Compacting recipe (5 Uranium plus 1 Iron Ingot makes 1 Fissile Core plus acid fluid, with a 10% chance of a bonus core).
- Telecore (Galena Gauntlet, Holocoder): drops from the Teletor mob (weight 1 out of 1) or Magnetic Ruins chest loot (weight 10 out of 206, 8 to 10 rolls, 1 to 2 count). It's automatable through Create sequenced assembly off a Scarlet Neodymium Ingot (90% success, with a 5%/5% byproduct split into either color of Neodymium ingot).
- Pure Darkness (Dreadbow core ingredient): a 100% guaranteed drop (7 to 12 per kill) from the Forsaken mini-boss, which only exists after 3 or more Underzealots successfully ritual-sacrifice a captured Vesper (see the Alex's Caves section for the full ritual/fight writeup). This is the actual gate on Dreadbow access, not a craft or loot shortcut.
- Heart of Iron (Resistor Shield): a Magnetron mob drop.

## Alex's Mobs
More on these in the creatures and mounts sections.
- Tarantula Hawk Elytra: craft from 2 Tarantula Hawk Wing and 1 vanilla Elytra. Wing: a Tarantula Hawk drop (0 to 1 per kill, a Desert biome hostile).
- Transmutation Table: craft from 1 Nether Star, 1 Farseer Arm and 3 Obsidian. Farseer Arm: a Farseer mob drop (0 to 1 per kill; the Farseer spawns rarely at the edge of the explored overworld, a "world border defender" mob). The table's GUI converts materials at an XP cost.
- Capsid: a 100% drop from the Enderiophage (a flying hostile in the End), not crafted. It's used as an item-transform station: a Crimson Mosquito Larva becomes a Mysterious Worm (120 ticks), Cod becomes Cosmic Cod, and a Dimensional Carver becomes a Shattered Dimensional Carver.
- Mysterious Worm (the Void Worm boss summon item): Capsid-process 1 Mosquito Larva for 120 ticks. Mosquito Larva: a shapeless craft of a Maggot and a Mosquito Proboscis, both sub-drops from common insect mobs (Maggot from rats/compost-adjacent mobs, Proboscis from Crimson Mosquito), or bought from a Trader Elephant (weight 11 in its trade-chest pool).
- Void Worm summon: throw a Mysterious Worm into the void of the End, past the main island. The config confirms it's only summonable in the End, and its natural spawn weight is 0, so it's summon-only.
- Dimensional Carver: craft from 2 Void Worm Mandible, 1 Void Worm Eye and 2 Netherite Ingot. Both the Mandible (×2) and the Eye (×1) are guaranteed Void Worm kill drops, so one boss kill exactly supplies one Carver, plus the Netherite Ingots.

## Northstar (space travel)
The rest of the space stuff is in the space section. These are the suit and navigator recipes.
- Iron Space Suit Chestpiece: craft from 4 iron plates, 1 vanilla Iron Chestplate (upgrades a vanilla chestplate), 2 Durable Fabric and 1 Copper Backtank (the oxygen source, a Create item). The helmet, leggings and boots follow the same "upgrade the vanilla iron piece with plates and fabric" pattern, no backtank needed on those. Durable Fabric is a basic string-and-wool craft. Copper Backtank is a Create item that needs filling with air or oxygen.
- Martian Steel Space Suit Chestpiece: the identical pattern, but using a Martian Steel Chestplate and Martian Steel plates instead of iron, i.e. a straight material-tier upgrade of the same recipe, gated behind already having a full Martian Steel armor piece (Martian Steel itself comes from Create compacting, heated: Titanium Ingot, Raw Martian Iron Ore and Volcanic Ash make 2 Martian Steel).
- Interplanetary Navigator (needed for Mars, Mercury and Venus, not the Moon): a 6-row Create mechanical crafting recipe using 6 Titanium Plate, 6 Polished Lunar Sapphire, 4 Circuit, 2 Titanium Ingot and 1 Targeting Computer (the pack's own recipe notes briefly list 6 targeting computers before correcting that to 1). Polished Lunar Sapphire has to be farmed on the Moon, so the Moon trip is a mandatory prerequisite before any other planet.
- Oxygen Sealer: a Create mechanical crafting recipe using Titanium Plates, an Encased Fan, an Oxygen Separator, a Circuit, a Fluid Tank and a Shaft. It oxygenates a sealed area; combine it with a Large Fan (needs an adjacent powered Encased Chain Drive) to extend the range.

## Create (utility and survival items)
- Copper Backtank: craft from 2 Andesite Alloy, 1 Create Shaft, 1 copper storage block and 3 Copper Ingot.
- Netherite Backtank: smith a Copper Backtank with a Netherite Ingot at the vanilla Netherite Upgrade Smithing Template, the same upgrade path as netherite armor (a mirror recipe also lets you upgrade from a Netherite Chestplate plus a Copper Backtank, same result).
- Copper Diving Helmet: craft from 5 Copper Ingot and 1 glass. Copper Diving Boots: craft from 4 Copper Ingot and 2 Andesite Alloy. Both upgrade to Netherite Diving Helmet/Boots the same smithing-table way as the Backtank (Netherite Ingot plus template, or the netherite helmet/boots plus the copper piece).
- Extendo Grip: a 5-row Create mechanical crafting recipe using 1 Brass Ingot, 1 Precision Mechanism, 6 wooden rods and 1 Brass Hand.
- Potato Cannon: a Create mechanical crafting recipe using 1 Andesite Alloy, 1 Precision Mechanism, 3 Fluid Pipe and 2 Copper Ingot.
- Charm of Undying: not actually a separate craftable item, the Charm of Undying mod tags a vanilla Totem of Undying directly into the Curios "Charm" trinket slot. Just wear a normal Totem of Undying in your Charm slot for the same death-save, hands free.
- Totem of Undying, extra sources in this pack (beyond the vanilla 100% Evoker-kill drop): Frozen Chest (Aquamirae, weight 2 out of 10, 1 to 2), Underworld Arena Vault (Bosses'Rise, weight 500 out of 29109, about 6.9%, 3 to 4 rolls), Evoker Chest room (Dungeons & Taverns illager mansion, weight 4 out of 10, 2 rolls), several DungeonsArise "Heavenly" airship-structure loot tables (Challenger/Conqueror/Rider, small weights but numerous), Moog's Soaring Structures rare loot (weight 1 out of 94, 3 to 7 count if it hits), and Moog's Temples Reimagined desert-temple crypt/pillar chests (weight 4 out of 44 and weight 3 out of 45). Worth farming these specifically since both Divine Armor (Immersive Armors) and full space-suit-tier endgame builds compete for the same limited totem supply.
- Elytra: only the standard vanilla source, End City ship chests, no pack-specific alternate recipe was found. BetterEnd's Crystalite/Aeternium Elytra upgrades (see the BetterEnd section above) both consume a vanilla Elytra as their base ingredient. With Lootr, every player can take their own Elytra from the same End Ship.

## Shield Expansion
All standard tiers are a plain craft: 8 tier material in a ring around 1 stick in the center. Wooden uses planks, Golden uses Gold Ingot, Iron uses Iron Ingot, Diamond uses diamonds. Netherite Shield: smith a Diamond Shield with a Netherite Ingot at the vanilla Netherite Upgrade Smithing Template, the same pattern as netherite armor and tools.
- Paragon Shield and Griefer Shield: both recipes are in the pack but are conditionally gated behind mods that aren't installed in this pack (they check for two other mods that aren't present). These two shields are confirmed unobtainable in BSCraft, dead recipes, don't send players hunting for them.
- The exact per-tier block and parry-window numbers are Java-coded, not in JSON or config. The pack's config only confirms lenient parry is off (strict parry timing), general cooldown and stashing cooldown are enabled, and blocking slows your movement.

## Magic gear (Ars Nouveau, Iron's Spells and addons)
- **Ars Nouveau robes**: gold, iron or diamond armor plus 4 Magebloom Fiber at the Enchanting Apparatus, then two upgrades (Blaze Rods and 2,500 Source, then Ender Pearls, Chorus Fruit and 5,000 Source).
- **Ars Elemental armor**: a tier 3 robe piece plus a Mark of Mastery, a netherite ingot and 2 matching essences, 7,000 Source.
- **Adam's Ars Plus armor**: a tier 3 robe piece plus 4 of that Archmage's Souls and 4 Mana Diamonds, 7,000 Source. The Awakened version adds 4 netherite ingots and 4 combined souls (8,500 Source).
- **Enchanter's Sword, Bow, Crossbow and Shield**: Enchanting Apparatus, from the diamond or vanilla version plus gold and Source Gem blocks.
- **Iron's robes**: Wandering Magician is leather armor plus Arcane Essence. Wizard robes are Arcane Cloth. School robes are Wizard robes plus that school's rune and Arcane Essence. Netherite Battlemage is Wizard robes smithed with netherite.
- **Iron's best pieces**: the Lightbringer chestplate, Boots of Speed and Infernal Sorcerer chestplate all need a Divine Soulshard from Echo of Tyros, plus Mithril Weave.
- **Iron's weapons**: the Boreal Blade, Twilight Gale, Spellbreaker and Amethyst Rapier are crafted with Weapon Parts (and mithril for the first three). The Keeper's Flamberge comes from Citadel chests and upgrades to the Legionnaire Flamberge; the Decrepit Scythe drops from Echo of Tyros and upgrades to Hellrazor. The Blood Staff and Necronomicon only drop from the Dead King.
- **Iron's spellbooks**: copper, iron, gold and diamond books are crafted. The Ancient Codex needs a netherite ingot, a Ruined Book (Ancient City loot) and a Bottle o' Lightning.
- **Mithril**: an ore below Y -38 that's never exposed to air, and it needs a netherite pickaxe.
- **Dread Robes**: Iron's Cultist robes smithed with Pure Darkness (from the Forsaken) and Shadow Silk.
- **Magic From The East, Twilight Spellbooks and Refined Mod weapons**: crafted from their own materials (Crystallized Souls, Refined Jade, Twilight Ink, Arcane Ingots). Check JEI for the exact recipe.

## Upgrade ladder by slot (early to endgame, the fastest realistic order in this pack)
- Weapon: vanilla iron/diamond sword, then BetterNether Nether Ruby or Cincinnasite (cheap crafts), then Flaming Ruby or Cincinnasite-Diamond (needs a Sculk Catalyst or Diamond upgrade, template from Wither Tower/Library loot), then a Simply Swords Runic-tier weapon (any eligible chest, about 0.7% per chest), then Twilight Fiery/Knightmetal weapons or the Minotaur Axe (100% Minoshroom drop), then Aquamirae Divider/Whisper of the Abyss (Eel-farmed echoes plus Netherite), then Simply Swords Unique-tier (Sunfire/Harbinger/Awakened Lichblade chains, need a Wither and Dragon kill or a Sculk Catalyst), then BetterEnd Aeternium weapon/hammer (smith on a Terminite base), then Alex's Caves Dreadbow/Raygun (Forsaken ritual-kill or Nucleeper plus Create automation), then Bosses'Rise Kraken Trident/Helvar's Sword.
- Helmet: leather/iron, then Immersive Armors Bone/Wooden/Prismarine (cheap crafts), then Thallasium (End ore), then BetterNether Flaming Ruby, then Twilight Fiery/Yeti/Phantom, then Mowzie's Wrought Helm/Sol Visage (boss drop, unbreakable), then Aquamirae Abyssal Heaume (block-and-slow AoE) or Tiara (blind-and-slow), then Armor of the Ages Exalted Aurum/Holy (consumes a Netherite Chestplate), then BetterEnd Aeternium/Crystalite Helmet, then Immersive Armors Divine Helmet (burns a Totem of Undying).
- Chestplate: same early curve, then Terminite/Thallasium (BetterEnd) or the Terrible Chestplate (Aquamirae, cheap), then Yeti/Fiery (Twilight), then the Abyssal Brigantine (needs Astral Dust from a Frozen Chest, the hardest single piece in the pack to get), then Aeternium/Crystalite Chestplate, then Immersive Armors Divine (a Totem sink), then Northstar Martian Steel Space Suit chestpiece (the only piece that also works as space gear).
- Leggings/Boots: mirror the chest curve. Yeti Boots' Feather Falling IV and the Aquamirae Terrible half-set's swim-speed buff are notable early-to-mid picks, and Rainbounce Boots (Alex's Caves, negates fall damage) is a good universal utility boot alternative to armor progression.
- Offhand: Shield Expansion Wooden to Iron to Diamond to Netherite (straight material-tier crafts), then the Resistor Shield (Alex's Caves, Heart of Iron from Magnetron) for its magnetic slam. Extendo Grip/Potato Cannon (Create) are also strong non-shield offhand utility picks mid-game.
- Curio slots: Charm of Undying (just wear a vanilla Totem of Undying in the Charm slot, no craft needed), then Charm of Life I/II and Charm of Keeping I/II/III (Twilight Forest chest loot, then shapeless-combine upward), then Ore Magnet/Moonworm Queen (rare Hollow Hill loot), then Aquamirae Maze Rose/Poisoned Chakra (Abyssal Amethyst-tier), then the Northstar Space Walk enchant (a Curios oxygen/heat alternative to a full space suit), then Simply Swords Contained Remnant (melt down a spare Unique weapon, then carry it into the End with a Runic Tablet for its own further transform).
- Magic: Wandering Magician robes and a copper spellbook, then Wizard or Ars Arcanist robes, then a school set or Battlemage's Robes, then Ars Elemental armor or the Netherite Battlemage set, then the Adam's Ars Plus Archmage armor from Cade up to Adam. For books, Novice to Apprentice to Archmage on the Ars side, and copper up to the Ancient Codex on the Iron's side.

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

# Magic

BSCraft runs two big magic systems side by side, and they talk to each other. Ars Nouveau has you build your own spells out of glyphs, and it leans hard into automation: rituals, familiars, and helper creatures that do things for you. Iron's Spells 'n Spellbooks is closer to a normal RPG magic system: you find spells by exploring and killing bosses, then cast them from a spellbook. You don't have to pick one, most players end up using both.

## How each one feels
Ars Nouveau is a toolkit. You chain Forms, Effects and Augments together into a spell, and the result depends entirely on what you put together. It rewards experimenting, and it comes with a lot of automation on top: Source generation, familiars that farm or fetch for you, rituals that terraform or summon things.

Iron's Spells 'n Spellbooks plays more like a normal RPG caster. Spells are learned or found as items (scrolls, spellbooks), each has a level, a cooldown and a mana cost, and you cast from a spellbook or a spell wheel instead of building anything yourself.

## Getting started in each
**Ars Nouveau**: craft a Novice Spell Book, shapeless, 1 Book + Iron Shovel + Iron Pickaxe + Iron Axe + Iron Sword. Every new player also just gets a starter book on first login. You already know Projectile, Touch, Self, Harm and Break from the start, enough to put together Projectile → Harm as a basic attack spell right away. From there, Archwood trees (small chance in any biome) give you the wood for a wand, amethyst or lapis in an Imbuement Chamber makes Source Gems, and a Scribe's Table lets you learn new glyphs with XP and items.

**Iron's Spells 'n Spellbooks**: you pick up spells as scrolls or in spellbooks, mostly by exploring structures and killing the bosses tied to each addon's school, then cast them straight from your spellbook.

## Casting keybinds
- Open your Ars Nouveau spell book: `N`
- Next / previous spell slot: `X` / `Shift+X`
- Cast from your head curio slot: `Shift+N`
- Open Ars Elemental's trinket pouch: `J`
- Channel an Ars Zero circlet: `H`
- Open the Ars Zero circlet spell radial: `P`
- Iron's Spells spell wheel: `R`
- Cast from an Iron's spellbook: `V`
- Iron's spell bar modifier: `LAlt`
- Browse spell lore pages (GTBC's spell mods): `Left arrow` / `Right arrow`

A few of these double up with other systems: `H` is also Immersive Machinery's horn, `R` is also aircraft dismount and the Power Grid tool, and `V` is also ship down. If one doesn't fire, check Options → Controls.

## The shared mana bar
Ars 'n Spells is the mod that wires Ars Nouveau and Iron's Spells together, and this pack runs its default mode, ISS Primary. That means Iron's Spells mana is the one pool that matters: casting an Ars Nouveau glyph drains your Iron's mana bar instead of Ars' own Source/Mana system. In practice you only watch one mana number, and Source stays a separate resource for automation and machines rather than for casting.

On top of the shared bar, Ars spells scale off Iron's spell power attributes: the base Spell Power attribute boosts every Ars cast, and if the spell lines up with a specific element the matching Iron's elemental spell power stacks on top of that too. Casting also builds a shared per-school affinity between the two mods, so grinding one mod's spells raises your standing for the other mod's spells of the same school as well. There's also a Resonance bonus: keep your mana above 95% and you get a spell damage bonus, on both Ars and Iron's casts.

## Magic addons in the pack
Ars Nouveau side:
- **Ars Elemental**: Fire/Water/Air/Earth school foci, elemental bangle curios, and 4 full elemental armor sets.
- **Ars Zero**: multi-phase staff casting (Begin/Tick/End), plus the Blight Forest biome with the Necropolis dungeon under it (Acolytes, Necromancers, Bone Golems and a Lich).
- **Adam's Ars Plus**: 6 Archmage bosses, one per school, each with its own armor line, a Domain Expansion glyph system, and a shikigami-taming minigame.
- **Ars Trinkets**: tiered mana rings, lotuses and monocles, Arcane Runes, and the Immortal Thearch Crown.
- **Ars Artifice**: curios and gems that auto-cast a stored spell off a trigger, or carry their own mana pool.
- **Ars Additions**: rechargeable protection charms, storage and logistics gear, and 3 new structures.
- **Ars Controle**: a Remote item for retargeting spells, and a warp-enabled Spell Prism.
- **Not Enough Glyphs**: a few new glyphs plus a second castable item, the Spell Binder.
- **Too Many Glyphs**: targeting filters, a longer-range Ray form, and stronger tiers of Amplify.
- **Extended Glyphs** (Magestone): 19 new utility glyphs and the whole Magestone gear line.
- **Starbunclemania**: gives Starbuncles and Wixies new jobs, like hauling fluids or FE power.
- **Ars Creo**: lets Spell Turrets and Source Jars ride on Create contraptions, plus a Starbuncle-powered water wheel.
- **Ars Technica**: a bigger Create crossover, 10 new glyphs, and the Technomancer armor set.
- **Ars Delight** (Ars Nouveau's Flavors & Delight): a Farmer's Delight crossover food chain built from Wilden drops and Archwood fruit, plus the Enchanter's Knife.

Iron's Spells side:
- **Magic From The East**: the Symmetry and Spirit schools, jade gear, and a Jade Executioner mini-boss.
- **Wind's Spellbooks**: a Wind school with 7 spells (Tornado is disabled, see below), the Aeromancer mob, and a Windmill structure.
- **Apprentice's Codex**: the biggest of the bunch, 84 spells plus a long list of summoned turrets, companions and utility items.
- **Geomancy Plus**: the Geo school, plus 2 extra spells that only drop from beating Mowzie's Mobs' Umvuthi, the Sunbird.
- **Twilight Spellbooks**: no new spells, just 8 themed spellbooks and matching gear that drop from Twilight Forest bosses.
- **Alex's Caves Spellbooks**: 2 summon spells and 2 armor sets built from Vallumraptor hides.
- **Refined Mod**: 9 Elden Ring-flavored weapon-scaling spells plus 3 unique weapons.
- **Farmer's Spell**: the Gluttony school, a big food and drink menu, and the friendly Foodgeist NPC.
- **Iron's Spellbooks Tweaks**: balance and quality-of-life tweaks to the base mod, check in game for specifics.
- **Magic Circles**: purely visual, draws a glowing circle under you when you cast (this pack uses the NEON style).
- **Dark Doppelganger**: a single endgame boss you summon in The End that copies your own gear and spells back at you.

## Magic vs weapons
In Ars Nouveau, most spell damage is only tagged as magic damage, not armor-piercing, so a high-armor target reduces it the same way it reduces melee damage. The two exceptions are Crush and Wind Shear: those two glyphs use a damage type that ignores armor entirely. Against tough targets, a caster's real edge over melee usually isn't "ignores armor", it's utility (Snare, Freeze, Gravity), safety at range, and stacking flat damage bonuses like the Spell Power thread or Wilden food buffs.

On the Iron's Spells side, spell damage scales with your spell power and, once a spell resolves to a specific school, that school's own spell power on top of it. Gear across both systems (armor threads, boss armor sets, elemental foci) mostly buffs one or both of these numbers, so stacking spell power and matching school power is the main way to hit harder as a caster.

## Known pitfalls
- Ars Additions' Locate Structure ritual can hang when it's checking against YUNG's-generated structures. There's no toggle for it in this pack's config.
- Ritual of Arcane Permanence, the chunkloading ritual from Ars Additions, is disabled in this pack's recipes.
- Tornado, from Wind's Spellbooks, is disabled pack-wide because it crashed dedicated servers. If you find a Tornado scroll it just won't cast.
- The Immortal Thearch Crown (Ars Trinkets) looks like a top chase item, huge multipliers to everything, but it's hardcoded to one specific player and nobody on this server can equip it. Don't chase it.

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

# Ars Nouveau

## Getting started
1. Craft a Novice Spell Book: shapeless, Book + Iron Shovel + Iron Pickaxe + Iron Axe + Iron Sword. You already know Projectile, Touch, Self, Harm and Break, enough for a basic Projectile → Harm spell right away. Every new player also just gets a starter book on first login.
2. Find Archwood (small chance in any biome) and use it to make a Wand.
3. Get an Amethyst Shard or Lapis into an Imbuement Chamber with 500 Source for a Source Gem. An Amethyst Block with 2000 Source makes a Source Gem Block, worth 4 gems.
4. Build a Scribe's Table to learn new glyphs with XP and items.
5. Upgrade your book when you're ready. Apprentice Spell Book (tier 2 glyphs) = Novice book + Obsidian + 3 Diamond + 2 Quartz Block + 2 Blaze Rod, crafted at a book-upgrade pedestal setup. Archmage Spell Book (tier 3 glyphs) = Apprentice book + Nether Star + Wilden Tribute + 2 Emerald + 3 Ender Pearl + Totem of the Undying. Wilden Tribute only drops from the Wilden Chimera boss, so that fight gates Archmage tier.
6. Essences (Fire/Earth/Water/Air/Abjuration/Conjuration/Manipulation) each cost 1 Source Gem + 3 school-flavored pedestal items + 2000 Source. Fire needs Flint & Steel + Torch + Gunpowder, Air needs Feather + Wilden Wing + Arrows.

## How spells are built
A spell chain has to start with a Form (Projectile, Touch, Self, Underfoot, plus the tier 3 forms Orbit/Burst/Linger/Wall/Rune). Effects resolve left to right at the hit location. Augments only modify the glyph right before them, so you chain multiple Augments to stack them. A spell slot in the book caps at 10 glyphs, but a Wand bypasses that cap since its spell is kept separate from the book.

Amplify is additive, not a percentage: each glyph has its own fixed amount added per Amplify (Harm +2 damage, Crush +1, Flare +3, Cold Snap +2.5, and so on). Amplify itself costs 20 mana at tier 1, and some effects cap how many times you can stack it (Harm defaults to a max of 2).

A spell's total mana cost is the sum of every glyph's own mana cost, minus any discounts from rings, the Enchanter's Mirror, threads or armor. Learning a new glyph also permanently adds a small flat bonus to your max mana and regen, and that bonus scales with your book tier.

### Forms
- **Touch**: tier 1, 5 mana
- **Underfoot**: tier 1, 5 mana
- **Projectile**: tier 1, 10 mana
- **Self**: tier 1, 10 mana

### Augments
- **Randomize**: tier 1, 0 mana
- **Sensitive**: tier 1, 10 mana, changes targeting rules
- **Amplify**: tier 1, 20 mana, adds power, the amount depends on the glyph
- **Dampen**: tier 2, 0 mana, reduces power
- **Decelerate**: tier 2, 5 mana
- **Accelerate**: tier 2, 10 mana
- **Extend Time**: tier 2, 10 mana
- **Duration Down**: tier 2, 15 mana
- **Extract**: tier 2, 30 mana, silk touch on Break, no-destroy on Explosion
- **Pierce**: tier 2, 40 mana
- **AOE**: tier 2, 35 mana
- **Luck (Fortune)**: tier 2, 80 mana
- **Split**: tier 3, 20 mana

### Tier 1 effects
- **Redstone Signal**: 0 mana, Manipulation, base power 10
- **Cut**: 0 mana, Manipulation, 1.0 damage, +1.0 per Amplify
- **Item Pickup**: 10 mana, Manipulation
- **Place Block**: 10 mana, Manipulation
- **Rotate**: 10 mana, Manipulation
- **Toss**: 10 mana, Manipulation
- **Break**: 10 mana, Elemental Earth
- **Harvest**: 10 mana, Elemental Earth
- **Interact**: 10 mana, Manipulation
- **Freeze**: 15 mana, Elemental Water, 10s base, +5s per Extend Time
- **Harm**: 15 mana, Elemental Earth, 5.0 damage +2.0 per Amplify, poison 5s base +5s per Extend
- **Ignite**: 15 mana, Elemental Fire, 3s base burn, +2s per Extend
- **Knockback** (Gust): 15 mana, Elemental Air, +1 per Amplify
- **Pull**: 15 mana, Elemental Air, +0.5 per Amplify
- **Conjure Mageblock** (Phantom Block): 5 mana, Conjuration
- **Conjure Magelight** (Light): 25 mana, Conjuration, 30s base +8s per Extend
- **Leap**: 25 mana, Elemental Air, +1.0 per Amplify
- **Launch**: 30 mana, Elemental Air, +0.25 per Amplify
- **Rune**: 30 mana, Manipulation
- **Dispel**: 30 mana, Abjuration
- **Wololo**: 30 mana
- **Craft**: 50 mana, Manipulation
- **Evaporate**: 50 mana, Manipulation
- **Bounce**: 50 mana, Abjuration, 30s base +8s per Extend
- **Snare**: 100 mana, Elemental Earth, 8s base +1s per Extend
- **Summon Steed**: 100 mana, Conjuration, +120s per Extend
- **Summon Wolves**: 100 mana, Conjuration, +60s per Extend
- **Fell**: 150 mana, Elemental Earth
- **Reset**: 0 mana

### Tier 2 effects
- **Infuse**: 30 mana
- **Name**: 25 mana, Manipulation
- **Gravity**: 15 mana, Elemental Air, 30s base +8s per Extend
- **Crush**: 30 mana, Elemental Earth, 3.0 damage +1.0 per Amplify. Ignores armor.
- **Cold Snap**: 30 mana, Elemental Water, 6.0 damage +2.5 per Amplify, +1s per Extend
- **Invisibility**: 30 mana, Abjuration, 30s base +8s per Extend
- **Slowfall**: 30 mana, Elemental Air, 30s base +8s per Extend
- **Wind Shear**: 50 mana, Elemental Air, 5.0 damage +2.5 per Amplify. Ignores armor.
- **Exchange**: 50 mana, Manipulation
- **Access Ender Inventory**: 50 mana, Manipulation
- **Sense Magic**: 50 mana, Abjuration, 60s base +15s per Extend
- **Heal**: 50 mana, Abjuration, +3.0 per Amplify
- **Firework**: 50 mana, Elemental Fire
- **Flare**: 40 mana, Elemental Fire, 7.0 damage +3.0 per Amplify, +1s per Extend
- **Conjure Water**: 80 mana, Elemental Water, 20s base +10s per Extend
- **Grow**: 70 mana, Elemental Earth
- **Smelt**: 100 mana, Elemental Fire
- **Explosion**: 200 mana, Elemental Fire, 6.0 damage +0.5 per Amplify (size)
- **Animate Block**: 200 mana, Manipulation/Conjuration, +60s per Extend

### Tier 3 effects
- **Orbit**: 50 mana
- **Blink**: 50 mana, Manipulation, +3.0 per Amplify (distance)
- **Fangs**: 35 mana, Conjuration, 6.0 damage +3.0 per Amplify
- **Intangible**: 30 mana, Manipulation, +1s per Extend (the game files list this glyph at both tier 2 and tier 3, treat it as tier 3)
- **Rewind**: 100 mana, Manipulation, +20 ticks per Extend, -10 per Duration Down
- **Lightning**: 100 mana, Elemental Air, 5.0 damage +3.0 per Amplify
- **Hex**: 100 mana, Abjuration, 30s base +8s per Extend
- **Wither**: 100 mana, Abjuration, 30s base +8s per Extend
- **Glide**: 100 mana, Elemental Air, 180s base +120s per Extend
- **Summon Undead**: 150 mana, Conjuration, +10s per Extend
- **Summon Vex**: 150 mana, Conjuration, +10s per Extend
- **Summon Decoy**: 200 mana, Conjuration, +15s per Extend
- **Burst**: 500 mana
- **Linger**: 500 mana, Manipulation
- **Wall**: 500 mana, Manipulation

### Addon glyphs
**Not Enough Glyphs**:
- **Plow**: simulates a Hoe on blocks
- **Echoing Projectile** (Trail): fires a projectile that re-resolves its spell repeatedly along its flight path
- **Propagate Plane**: resolves the rest of the spell as an expanding plane from the hit block
- **Propagate Orbit**: converts the rest of the spell into an Orbit spell around the hit target

**Too Many Glyphs**:
- **Filter** (Block/Entity/Living/Player/Monster/Animal/Item/Baby/Mature/Living-Not-Player/Living-Not-Monster): tier 1, 0 mana, stops the spell unless the target matches
- **Reverse Direction**: tier 1, 0 mana, reverses a block-targeted spell across the struck face
- **Ray**: tier 1, 10 mana, an instant longer-range hit-scan form, costs mana even on a miss
- **Lay on Hands**: tier 1, 10 mana, a touch-style healing form
- **Chaining**: tier 1, 300 mana, chains the spell through grouped nearby targets
- **Amplify II**: 80 mana, a stronger Amplify
- **Amplify III**: 320 mana, the strongest Amplify

**Extended Glyphs** (Magestone):
- **Above**: retargets one block up
- **Absorption**: extra hearts, more with Amplify
- **Atrophy**: applies Weakness
- **Burrow**: buries the target 2 blocks, deeper with Amplify
- **Nearest Player**: retargets within 16 blocks, AOE adds 16 range each
- **Conjure Lava**: places lava
- **Conjure Source**: generates Source into a nearby container, Amplify/Dampen adjust how much
- **Extreme**: raises the odds of a rarer or better outcome on spells it works with
- **Fertility**: speeds baby animal growth or resets a breeding cooldown
- **Repair**: repairs one item in the target's inventory
- **Return to Spawn / Set Spawn**: teleports to or sets your spawn point
- **Saturate**: hunger saturation, more with Amplify
- **Protect** (Shield): brief heavy damage resistance, extendable
- **Summon Hostile / Summon Wildlife**: spawns 3 mobs, Amplify/Dampen change the count
- **Target Self**: redirects the rest of the chain to the caster
- **Transmute**: a Source Gem becomes random loot, better with Fortune or Extreme

**Ars Additions**:
- **Mark**: stores the target in a Reliquary
- **Recall**: re-targets whatever's stored in a Reliquary and casts on it
- **Retaliate**: applies the spell to whatever last hit you, within 5 seconds

**Starbunclemania**:
- **Pickup Fluid**: fills a hotbar or nearby tank from a fluid source
- **Place Fluid**: drains a hotbar or nearby tank into a fluid source

**Ars Technica**:
- **Carve**: shapes matching items into stairs, Amplify makes walls, Dampen makes slabs
- **Pack**: condenses matching items 2x2, Amplify 3x3, Dampen 1x1
- **Polish / Press**: item variant conversions
- **Fuse**: slams items together, combine with Smelt for heated mixing
- **Superheat**: pairs with Fuse for super-heated mixing
- **Insert**: moves nearby items into nearby containers, AOE extends the range
- **Obliterate**: arcane-hammer damage, Sensitive crushes items instead of destroying them
- **Telefeast**: consumes the first edible, potion or liquid found in a targeted container or tank
- **Whirl**: centrifuges items in an area, combine with Water/Flare/Smelt/Hex to wash, smoke, blast or haunt them

**Ars Trinkets** (25+ combat glyphs, selected):
- **Sword**: ignores most defenses
- **Cursed Flame** (Sacrifice): self-damage for more damage dealt, scales with Amplify
- **Water Spear**: piercing
- **Exp Sacrifice**: spends XP levels for damage, scales with your level and Amplify
- **Shadow Veil**: a slowing darkness trap
- **Sound Boom**: an AOE explosion of sound that doesn't hurt the caster
- **Sun Flare**: bonus damage against Undead and black mages
- **Mana Bomb**: consumes all your mana for a blast that scales with how much you had
- **Devour / Devour Soul**: life steal, can backfire if your soul isn't strong enough
- **Giant Strength**: self-buff
- **Wither's Blessing**: extra armor and toughness
- **Aura**: a persistent zone-effect form (Accelerate ticks faster, AOE bigger, Sensitive targets blocks, Pierce hollow, Amplify/Dampen move the target above or below the caster)
- Also new forms and propagators: Overhead, Missile, Propagate-Missile/-Self/-Overhead/-Projectile/-Underfoot, Random 25/50/75%, Rotate 90, Swap Position, Filter Self/Not Self, Soul Inspector

**Adam's Ars Plus**:
- **Domain**: creates a sphere that applies the rest of the spell to everyone inside for a short time. AOE makes it bigger, Accelerate resolves it faster, Extend Time makes it last longer, Pierce makes a dome shape, Extract excludes the caster, Amplify refines the domain so it can override a weaker rival one. Open Barrier removes the shell.
- **Simple Domain**: a personal ward that blocks an enemy Domain's "surehit" effect
- **Ten Shadows**: summons a tamed shikigami you've earned the right to summon
- **Spell Efficiency**: cuts mana cost (new cost = old cost times 0.7 to the power of stacks, capped at 5 stacks) but causes temporary mana-regen exhaustion
- Also: Eruption, Iceburst, Soul Rime, Fracture, Divine Smite (an upgraded Lightning that consumes Shocked stacks), Summon Undead Mage, Meteor Swarm (falling height adds bonus Amplify), Annihilate (voidbreak, bypasses most defense), Swap Target, Cremation, Dismantle, Conjure Blade (replicates your held weapon), Conjure Arrow, Detonate and Propagate Detonate, plus tiered upgrades of AOE, Accelerate, Extend Time, Duration Down, Amplify and Dampen (up to III), Lesser AOE, Filter: Not Self

**Ars Zero**:
- **Conjure Voxel**: conjures a short-lived block entity. Split/Amplify/Sensitive/Extend Time change it, and composite versions make elemental voxels (Water/Fire/Wind/Stone/Ice/Lightning/Blight) out of Conjure Water/Ignite/Wind Shear/Conjure Terrain/Cold Snap/Discharge/Conjure Blight.
- **Wind Shear**: used with Conjure Voxel to make a wind voxel
- **Select**: targets something without acting on it, combos with block/entity filter augments
- **Beam**: compresses mana into a beam that resolves the rest of the spell per hit, draining mana per hit
- **Sustain**: extends a target's lifespan
- **Temporal Context**: reads the previous phase's target or position, only usable in the Tick or End phase
- **Near**: a short-range cast right in front of your gaze
- **Anchor**, **Zero Gravity**: additional Ars Zero glyphs
- 8 voxel types in total: Arcane, Fire, Water, Wind, Stone, Ice, Lightning, Blight, and they interact with each other (fire and ice voxels reportedly cancel out)

**Ars Elemental** (empowered further if you're wearing the matching school focus):
- **Ignite**: with the Fire focus, inflicts Magic Burn, which works even on fire-resistant mobs
- **Freeze**: the Water focus adds extra Freezing seconds
- **Launch / Cut**: the Air focus turns Launch + Extend Time into Levitate, and gives Cut a head or skull drop chance
- **Poison Spores / Grow / Gravity**: the Earth focus empowers these, and Gravity + Sensitive becomes a pull field
- **Charm**: the Necrotic focus boosts your chance to charm undead
- **Homing Projectile**, **Arc Projectile**: extra projectile behavior glyphs

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

## Source generation and automation
Five Sourcelinks generate Source from a different activity each, and auto-feed nearby Source Jars within 5 blocks:
- **Agronomic**: crop and tree growth within 15 blocks, with a bonus for Mageblooms, Source Berries and Archwood saplings (bonemealing gives nothing)
- **Mycelial**: nearby food value (Source Berry food is worth far more), and it myceliumizes the 3x3 below it and grows mushrooms
- **Vitalic**: mob deaths and animal breeding nearby, plus passive generation from baby animals, which it also speeds up
- **Volcanic**: burning burnable items (Archwood logs give a bonus, Blazing Archwood the best), and it heats up, turning stone into magma into lava and spawning Lava Lilies
- **Alchemical**: consuming potions from adjacent Potion Jars, scaling with the potion's complexity, level and duration

Moving Source around: **Source Relay** (30-block reach, wand-linked point to point, redstone disables it), **Collector** (auto-pulls within 5 blocks), **Depositor** (auto-pushes within 5 blocks), **Splitter** (feeds multiple jars, higher throughput), **Warper** (unlimited range, but a chance of loss past 30 blocks).

Worker helpers (summoned with a Charm, given a home with the Dominion Wand, need Source plus a nearby chest):
- **Starbuncle**: wild ones spawn in forests and are tamed with a gold nugget, dropping a charm. Moves items between inventories, picks up drops, and harvests Source Berry bushes.
- **Whirlisprig**: a forest sprite, tamed by growing a tree near it. Generates wood, crops, seeds and flowers based on how "happy" its home radius is, meaning more variety and quantity of natural blocks.
- **Wixie**: comes from using Dispel on a half-health Witch. Auto-crafts, pulling from nearby inventories, and auto-brews potions in Potion Jars from nearby items, using Source per craft.
- **Drygmy**: a rare wild spawn, tamed by throwing a Wilden Horn near it. Simulates loot drops from nearby mobs and animals without harming them, scaling with how many different kinds are nearby. Needs a Drygmy Henge (built from Mossy Cobblestone) and periodic Source to recharge.
- **Amethyst Golem**: comes from a Ritual of Awakening near Budding Amethyst. Auto-harvests Amethyst Clusters, converts Amethyst Block into Budding Amethyst, speeds up cluster growth, and collects shards.
- **Weald Walker**: comes from a Ritual of Awakening at the base of an Archwood tree. Guards a set position with school-specific attacks (Flare, Freeze plus Cold Snap, amplified Harm plus Snare, or Hex plus Wither depending on the variant) and becomes a "Weald Waddler" if it dies.

## Rituals
All cast via a Ritual Brazier and a tablet, augment the tablet before lighting it.
- **Summon Animals**: spawns biome-native wild animals
- **Awakening**: turns Archwood trees into Weald Walkers and Budding Amethyst into Amethyst Golems (augment with a Book & Quill for Bookwyrm Charms)
- **Binding**: converts a nearby eligible entity into a Bound Script, which unlocks a familiar
- **Burrowing**: digs 4 holes down to bedrock
- **Challenge**: summons an illager raid in a village (an Emerald sets max difficulty)
- **Cloudshaping**: sets the weather (Gunpowder for rain, Lapis Block for storm)
- **Conjure Island: Desert / Plains**: terraforms a 7-radius island (Source Gems add +1 radius each, Terracotta makes Badlands, Frostaya makes Snow Plains)
- **Containment**: captures a nearby entity into a Containment Jar
- **Disintegration**: destroys nearby monsters for double XP, no drops
- **Fertility**: breeds nearby animals, caps at 20 nearby
- **Flight**: grants jump-triggered flight
- **Flowering**: spreads flowers and grass (sand turns it into cactus or dead bush)
- **Forestation**: grows a 7x7 Oak or Birch forest (mushroom gives taiga or podzol, glow berries give jungle)
- **Gravity**: forces nearby players down with a Gravity debuff
- **Harvest**: harvests nearby crops periodically
- **Moonfall / Sunrise**: sets time to night or day
- **Overgrowth**: periodic bonemeal (Bone Block speeds up baby animal growth)
- **Restoration**: heals living things, harms undead, cures zombie villagers
- **Sanctuary**: denies hostile spawns in a 32-block radius (rotten flesh adds +1 radius, up to 128)
- **Scrying**: see through blocks at range (Manipulation Essence extends it to 15 minutes)
- **Warping**: teleports nearby entities to an inscribed Warp Scroll location
- **Summon Wilden**: on its own, summons a random Wilden pack. Augmented with a Wilden Spike, Wilden Horn and Wilden Wing, it summons the Wilden Chimera boss instead, and this destroys blocks around the brazier

## Familiars
