Permanent buffs, unlocked with the Ritual of Binding near the source entity, then learned from a Bound Script.
- **Starbuncle**: Speed II. Feed it a Gold Nugget for temporary Scrying for Gold Ore.
- **Whirlisprig**: Elemental Earth glyphs cost half mana, plus bonus food saturation.
- **Wixie**: your potions last longer, and it auto-applies debuffs to enemies you're fighting.
- **Drygmy**: +2 Earth spell damage, plus a chance at bonus looting.
- **Bookwyrm**: auto-picks up items and XP within 5 blocks, toggle with the familiar hotkey.
- **Amethyst Golem**: halves knockback you take and knocks back attackers. Feed it an Amethyst Shard for 3 minutes of Shielding.

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

## Armor
Three magic armor sets, each upgradeable 3 times through the Enchanting Apparatus. Every piece repairs from mana with a Thread, and all three sets share 30 enchantability.
- **Sorcerer's Robes** (light): Boots/Legs/Chest/Helm armor 1/3/5/2 (11 total), durability 325/375/400/275, toughness 0
- **Arcanist's Robes** (medium): Boots/Legs/Chest/Helm armor 2/5/6/2 (15 total), durability 325/375/400/275, toughness 0
- **Battlemage's Robes** (heavy): Boots/Legs/Chest/Helm armor 3/6/8/3 (20 total), durability 429/495/528/363, toughness 2.0

Each one is a vanilla armor piece plus 4 Magebloom Fiber at the Enchanting Apparatus: gold for Sorcerer's, iron for Arcanist's, diamond for Battlemage's.

Mana and regen per full set, by tier: tier 1 gives +120 max mana / +4 regen, tier 2 gives +240 / +8, tier 3 gives +360 / +12 (each piece grants 30 mana and 1 regen per tier). Upgrading to tier 2 costs 2 Blaze Rod on pedestals plus 2500 Source; tier 3 costs 2 Ender Pearl + Chorus Fruit plus 5000 Source, both at the Enchanting Apparatus.

Threads (armor perks from the Alteration Table, each applies once per full set):
- **Spell Power**: +2 spell damage per level
- **Warding**: +2 flat magic damage reduction per level
- **Magic Capacity**: +10% max mana per level
- **Life Drain**: heals 20% of spell damage dealt, per level
- **Kindling**: +5 seconds of burn on damaging hits, per level
- **Chilling**: 10 seconds of Freezing per level on damaging hits, Freezing II at slot 3 and up
- **Repairing**: passively repairs any nearby magic armor or Enchanter item using mana
- **Undying**: negates death once per sleep cycle, needs a tier 3 slot
- **Gliding**: elytra-like glide, needs a tier 3 slot
- **Heights / High Step / Feather**: jump height and fall damage, step height, or fall damage percent
- **Depths**: infinite air and fast swimming at slot 3 and up
- **Amethyst Golem**: +15% knockback resistance per level
- **The Drygmy**: +1 looting level
- **The Starbuncle**: +20% move speed per level
- **The Whirlisprig**: +30% food saturation per level
- **The Wixie**: +15% potion duration per level

## Spell books
**Novice** unlocks tier 1 glyphs. **Apprentice** unlocks tier 2 (Novice book + Obsidian + 3 Diamond + 2 Quartz Block + 2 Blaze Rod, book-upgrade pedestal). **Archmage** unlocks tier 3 (Apprentice book + Nether Star + Wilden Tribute + 2 Emerald + 3 Ender Pearl + Totem of the Undying). Books are dyeable and carry every spell you know when you upgrade.

## Weapons and tools
- **Enchanter's Wand**: takes a single spell inscribed at the Scribe's Table, and always adds Projectile → Accelerate at the front. A wand's spell doesn't count against the book's 10-glyph cap, so it's the standard way to cast something longer than the book allows. Craft: Archwood log/stick + 4 Source Gem + 2 Gold Ingot + Air Essence + Manipulation Essence at the Enchanting Apparatus.
- **Enchanter's Bow / Crossbow**: inscribe a spell, and fired arrows become Spell Arrows that apply it on hit if you have the mana, otherwise a 0-damage arrow, or a normal arrow if you're totally out. Special Augment Arrows (Amplify, Pierce, Split) empower the inscribed spell further.
- **Enchanter's Sword**: a Netherite-tier sword, 8 attack damage / 1.6 attack speed, the same numbers as a vanilla Netherite Sword. Applies a Touch spell before the hit, and the last effect in the inscribed spell gets a free bonus Amplify.
- **Enchanter's Shield**: 500 durability, repairs from your mana. Blocking grants a brief Mana Regen and Spell Damage buff.
- **Enchanter's Mirror**: applies a self-only, form-less spell to you at a discount with bonus duration.
- **Focus of Summoning**: gives spell-summoned allies more duration, strength, speed and damage-on-death.
- **Focus of Block Shaping**: moved or created blocks deal spell-damage-scaled impact damage, and lets effects duplicate onto them. Classic combos: Conjure Mageblock → Launch → Delay → Knockback to throw a damaging block, or Conjure Water → Freeze → Launch → Delay → Knockback to throw ice.

Ars Zero staffs and circlet (3-phase casting: Begin runs once, then Tick loops while held, then End fires on release):
- **Novice Spell Staff**: tier 1 glyphs only. Craft: Archwood Rod + Iron Shovel + Iron Pickaxe + Iron Axe + Iron Sword, shapeless.
- **Mage Spell Staff**: tier 1 and 2. Upgrade a Novice staff with Obsidian + 3 Diamond + 2 Quartz Block + 2 Blaze Rod.
- **Archmage Spell Staff**: tier 1 through 3. Upgrade a Mage staff with 3 Ender Pearl + 2 Emerald + 1 Totem of Undying + 1 Nether Star + 1 Wilden Tribute.
- **Creative Spell Staff**: every glyph, creative mode only.
- **Psion's Circlet**: worn in a curio slot, same tier 1 through 3 access as the Archmage staff. Craft: upgrade a Dull Circlet (1 Source Gem over 3 Iron Ingots over an iron ring) with the exact same ingredients as the Archmage staff.

Channel a circlet with `H`, open its spell radial with `P`. A few named "signature staffs" (Staff of Telekinesis, Staff of Demonbane, Staff of Lakes, Staff of Switcheroo, Staff of Geometrize, Staff of the Explosion Arch Wizard) show up in flavor text as example builds, not confirmed craftable or lootable items. Check in game before promising one to anyone.

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

## Ars Elemental
Built around 4 elemental schools, Fire, Water, Air, Earth, plus a hidden Anima/Necromancy sub-school.

Craft a Lesser Focus of your chosen school and hold it in a spell-focus slot. While equipped it discounts and amplifies glyphs of that school (25% cheaper, plus an amplify buff) but weakens glyphs of the other three schools, so don't wear one while casting a mixed-school loadout. A Major Focus doubles the amplify bonus and adds a passive perk while a condition is met: Fire gives Spell Damage II while on fire or in lava, Water gives Mana Regen I while wet and Mana Regen II plus Dolphin's Grace while swimming, Air gives Mana Regen I above Y200 or while Static Charged, Earth gives Mana Regen I below Y0. The Focus of Necromancy corrupts the Anima/Summoning focus.

Elemental bangles (curio slot) boost that school's spell damage and add a themed on-hit effect: Fire ignites and speeds you up in hot biomes, Water freezes and speeds you up in water or rain, Earth snares and gives cactus/berry immunity plus knockback resist, Air knocks back and speeds you up, Summoner's boosts summon damage and targeting, Anima randomly heals or withers on hit. Caster Tomes (Fire/Water/Air/Earth/Anima/Manipulation) mimic a school focus's passive without using your focus slot.

**Elemental armor** (Fire/Water/Air/Earth, all 4 sets share the same base stats): Boots 3 / Leggings 6 / Chestplate 8 / Helmet 3 (20 total, same spread as netherite), toughness 2.0, knockback resistance 0 except Earth at 0.02, durability multiplier 33 (netherite-tier), enchantability 40. Each piece gives +100 max mana and +4 mana regen, so a full 4-piece set is +400 max mana / +16 regen on top of your Tier-3 robe base, and each piece amplifies and discounts matching-school glyphs. It's a Tier-3 (Archmage) robe upgrade, so you need the base Archmage garb and a Mark of Mastery first: at the Enchanting Apparatus, pedestal 1 Mark of Mastery + 1 Netherite Ingot + 2 matching Elemental Essence, 7000 Source.

Other Ars Elemental gear: the **Enchanter's Horn** (an inscribable item that casts on you and nearby entities, charges range while held, and grants a Spell Damage buff at max charge), a **Trinkets Pouch** (needs Magebloom Fiber) for small curios and potions, and a stronger **Spellcaster Bag** that also holds casters, this mod's armor, the arrow and the shield (open either with `J`). Prism Lenses (Arc/Homing/Rainbow/Acceleration/Deceleration/Piercing) modify the Advanced Spell Prism's redirected projectiles and cost Source per redirect; the Pierce lens caps at 10 redirects.

Mermaids and Sirens (10 HP) can be tamed with a sea pickle; use a Siren Charm on Prismarine to build a Siren Shrine, which slowly converts Source into fishing loot. The **Flarecannon** ("Firenando", 40 HP, 20 armor) is a wandering sentry turret familiar that fires homing flares, patrols an area you set with a Dominion Wand, and can be reactivated with blaze powder or magma cream if destroyed; in this pack it drops its charm on death by its owner instead of just deactivating. Rogue elemental mages (Fire/Water/Air/Earth, 50 HP) roam and will attack you unless you're wearing the matching school's focus. Flashing Archwood is a 5th, gold-colored Archwood tied to Air and lightning; this pack doesn't generate it as its own biome, but the tree still shows up scattered in existing Archwood forests, and lightning strikes near it often. Infused Turrets (Fire/Water/Air/Earth/Manipulation) are enchanted turrets imbued with a focus, and get a 65% discount when their spell contains a matching-school glyph.

## Ars Nouveau Trinkets
Three parallel curio lines, Mana Rings, Mana Lotuses, Monocles, each tiered 1 to 10 through a shared essence chain (copper, iron, silver, gold, amethyst/crystal, green, red, white, yellow, purple).
- **Monocle** tier 3: +5 mana / +0 regen / +10 attack damage. Tier 4: +10 / +5 / +15. Tier 5: +15 / +10 / +20. Tier 6: +25 / +15 / +25. Tier 7: +40 / +25 / +30. Tier 8: +60 / +50 / +35. Tier 9: +80 / +75 / +40. Tier 10: +100 / +100 / +50. (Tiers 1 and 2 only give +2 and +5 attack damage, no mana.)
- **Lotus** tier 3: +0 mana / +5 regen. Tier 4: +10 / +10. Tier 5: +15 / +25. Tier 6: +25 / +50. Tier 7: +50 / +75. Tier 8: +75 / +100. Tier 9: +100 / +150. Tier 10: +250 / +250.
- **Ring** tier 3: +25 mana / -10 regen. Tier 4: +50 / -15. Tier 5: +100 / -20. Tier 6: +150 / -25. Tier 7: +250 / -50. Tier 8: +500 / -100. Tier 9: +1000 / -150. Tier 10: +2000 / -200.

Rings give by far the biggest flat mana boost but cost you regen, so pairing a maxed Ring with a maxed Lotus (+250 mana / +250 regen) offsets the loss. Monocles are the only line that also boosts attack damage, making them a melee/caster hybrid pick. Together, a tier-10 Ring and a tier-10 Lotus net +2250 max mana and +50 net regen from two curio slots.

**Mana Cores** (Human's Heart, Mage's Heart, Archmage's Source Heart) are eaten to permanently raise your caster level. Core 1: 4 Yellow Essence + reagent Nether Star. Core 2: 8 Purple Essence + reagent Core 1. Core 3: 4 Core 1 + reagent Core 2. All at 0 Source cost through the Enchanting Apparatus.

**Arcane Runes** come in 4 lines, Life, Death, Warrior, Mage, each going Lesser (Uncommon) → Normal (Rare) → Greater (Epic). All four Greater runes plus 4 Mana Core 3 combine into a single Eternal Rune (reagent Crying Obsidian). Runes give curio bonuses across armor, health, damage, luck, toughness and percentage mana/regen/attack-speed boosts, roughly +15 to +50 flat or +25% to +100% depending on the line and tier. Oddly, `mage_rune_lesser` needs a maxed tier-10 Monocle, Ring and Lotus as ingredients, so despite the name it's a late-game item.

The **Immortal Thearch Crown** is a helm-slot curio with huge multipliers, attack speed x20, attack damage x200, max health x500, luck x100, armor x250, armor toughness x250, spell damage x5, max mana x100, mana regen x100, but it can only be equipped by one specific hardcoded player, not anyone on this server. Don't chase it.

## Ars Artifice
Craft an **Artificer's Workbench** (Archwood Slab - Scribe's Table - Archwood Slab over Gold Nugget - blank - Gold Nugget over Iron Ingot - blank - Iron Ingot, shaped) to program trigger-based curios.

Workflow: take a base Ars Nouveau curio (Dull Trinket, Mundane Belt, or Ring of Potential) and upgrade it at the Enchanting Apparatus (3 Diamond + 4 Source Gem + 1 Observer, 0 extra Source) into an Amulet, Belt or Ring of Spell Storing. At the Artificer's Workbench, pick which trigger glyph it reacts to. At a Scribe's Table, inscribe the spell onto it. Equip it and it auto-casts on the trigger.

Trigger glyphs (a stored spell must start with one of these):
- **Anguish**: fires when you take damage above a threshold (Amplify raises it, Dampen lowers it)
- **Falling**: fires once you've fallen a set number of blocks (Amplify/Dampen adjust the count)
- **Landing**: fires when you land after falling that far (Amplify raises the fall-distance requirement)
- **Interval**: fires on a repeating timer (Amplify/Dampen adjust the loop length)

**Spell Gems** are a separate consumable curio with their own mana pool, so you can cast without touching your own mana bar. Tier 1: Source Gem reagent + 1 Spell Parchment pedestal, 1000 Source. Tier 2: upgrade T1 with 3 Source storage blocks, 2500 Source. Tier 3: same upgrade on T2 with 3 more Source storage blocks, 5000 Source. Higher tiers hold more mana before the gem breaks.

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

## Adam's Ars Plus
The pack's Ars-side boss and endgame layer: 6 Archmage bosses, one per school, each with its own dungeon, armor progression and drops.

Getting there: craft an Eye for the school you want (Enchanting Apparatus: an Ender Eye + 1 matching school essence, 500 Source), which points to the nearest matching structure. Craft the Mage Summon ritual tablet (1 Blue Archwood Log + 2 Mage Cloth + 1 Mana Diamond, shapeless): used without an Eye it summons a random, weak Mysterious Mage; used inside the correct structure while holding the matching Eye, it summons that structure's Archmage boss instead.

Boss structures and eyes:
- **Ryan**, the Flame Archmage: Eye of Flame (plus Fire Essence) finds the Infernal Prison
- **Cade**, the Frost Archmage: Eye of Frost (plus Water Essence, presumed) finds the Frozen Library
- **Nick**, the Earth Archmage: Eye of Earth (plus Earth Essence, presumed) finds the Overgrown Barracks
- **Cam(eron)**, the Lightning Archmage: Eye of Lightning finds the Nimbostratic Ruins
- **Matt(hew)**, the Holy Archmage: Eye of Holy finds the Holy Mausoleum
- **Adam**, the Void Archmage: Eye of Void finds the Void Fortress
- **Josh**, the Manipulation mage: no eye or structure. Use the Mage Summon ritual with a Manipulation Essence instead of an Eye.

Boss stats (HP / attack / armor / toughness):
- **Cade** (Frost): 325 HP, 6 attack, 12 armor, 5 toughness. Drops 8 to 18 Frost Soul.
- **Ryan** (Flame): 500 HP, 12 attack, 15 armor, 10 toughness. Drops 8 to 16 Flame Soul.
- **Nick** (Earth): 900 HP, 15 attack, 17 armor, 10 toughness. Drops 8 to 16 Earth Soul.
- **Cam** (Lightning): 925 HP, 15 attack, 20 armor, 12 toughness. Drops 8 to 18 Lightning Soul.
- **Matt** (Holy): 1225 HP, 20 attack, 23 armor, 15 toughness. Drops 8 to 16 Hero (Holy) Soul.
- **Josh** (Manipulation): 1800 HP, 20 attack, 23 armor, 15 toughness. Drops 4 to 8 Advanced Clockwork.
- **Adam** (Void): 2000 HP, 20 attack, 23 armor, 15 toughness. Drops 16 to 24 Void Soul.

Mage Knight and lesser Mage variants also roam near these structures, generally 30 to 100 HP depending on the tier, and drop 0 to 1 Mage Cloth.

Armor lines (per-piece Boot/Legs/Chest/Helm armor, all 30 enchantability):
- **Cade** (Frost): 2/5/6/3, 16 total, toughness 2.0, durability multiplier 80
- **Ryan** (Flame): 3/6/8/3, 20 total, toughness 3.0, durability multiplier 80
- **Nick** (Earth): 3/7/10/4, 24 total, toughness 4.0, durability multiplier 80
- **Cam** (Lightning): 3/7/10/4, 24 total, toughness 4.0, durability multiplier 120
- **Matt** (Holy): 5/8/11/6, 30 total, toughness 5.0, durability multiplier 120
- **Adam** (Void): 6/9/12/7, 34 total, toughness 6.0, durability multiplier 160

Toughness here is per piece, like every armor number in this guide. Adam's Void armor is the best raw armor in the pack: 34 armor and 24 toughness for the full set, against Aeternium's 24 and 14 and netherite's 20 and 12. The pack raises the usual 30 armor and 20 toughness caps, so all of it counts. In practice a 30-damage hit leaves you taking 6 in full Void armor, 7.7 in Aeternium and 13.2 in netherite. A 100-damage hit is 20 against 76.7 and 84. Nick's and Cam's tier (24 armor, 4 toughness per piece) already edges out Aeternium. The Armor compared section has the full list.

Magic stats per piece, multiply by 4 for a full set: Cade +40 mana/+2.0 regen/+1 spell damage, Ryan +35/+1.5/+1, Nick +30/+1.0/+1, Cam +38/+2.5/+1, Matt +30/+1.0/+1/+1 warding, Adam +50/+4.0/+1/+1 warding.

Crafting pattern: normal tier reagent is the matching AN Tier-3 robe piece, pedestal is 4 of that boss's Soul + 4 Mana Diamond, 7000 Source. Awakened tier upgrades the normal piece with 4 Netherite Ingot + 4 of a combined-soul item, 8500 Source. Cade/Ryan/Nick need 4 Elemental Soul (itself: pedestal Flame + Frost + Earth Soul, reagent Mana Diamond, 10000 Source). Matt/Cam need 4 (Awakened) True Elemental Soul (pedestal Flame + Frost + Earth + Hero(Holy) + Lightning Soul, reagent Mana Diamond, 10000 Source). Adam just needs 4 Void Soul directly. Mana Diamond itself: Enchanting Apparatus, 8 Source Gem + 1 Diamond reagent, 500 Source.

Getting to Adam's Awakened Void armor is a long grind: Cade (easiest, at 325 HP) up through Ryan, Nick, Cam, Matt, to Adam (2000 HP, the hardest fight in this group), and each boss also gates the next armor tier's crafting materials.

Threads (crafted with a `blank_thread` reagent from Ars Nouveau Trinkets):
- **Six Eyes**: superior vision plus "legendary mana efficiency". Pedestal: 2 Void Soul + 2 End Crystal + 2 Netherite Block.
- **Cloud Steps**: grants flight. Pedestal: 2 Lightning Soul + Elytra + 3 Air Essence.
- **Immortality**: regenerating absorption hearts, drawn from mana. Pedestal: 2 Hero(Holy) Soul + Enchanted Golden Apple + 3 Ghast Tear.
- **Invincibility**: additional armor. Pedestal: 2 Earth Soul + Anvil + 3 Netherite Scrap.
- **Adrenaline**: a mana-regen burst on taking damage, scaling with missing HP. Pedestal: 2 Frost Soul + Pufferfish + Heart of the Sea + Powder Snow Bucket.
- **Draconic Hex**: curses your spell targets when you land a damaging spell. Pedestal: 2 Flame Soul + Wither Skeleton Skull + 2 Blaze Powder.

All 6 Threads cost 0 extra Source beyond their ingredients.

Utility items: the **Enchanter's Stopwatch** (3 Gold Ingot + 2 Repeater + 1 Mana Diamond + 2 Advanced Clockwork, reagent Clock, 7000 Source) repeatedly casts a scribed spell on an interval you can adjust. The **General's Wheel** (4 Gold Ingot + 4 Mana Diamond, reagent AN Summon Focus, 2500 Source) speeds up summoning-sickness removal, up to 50% faster the more Ten Shadows shikigami you control. **Mage Tome** is a limited-use spell item that sometimes drops from a Mage Knight with its known spell inscribed, repaired with Mage Cloth.

Random overworld Mysterious Mages can break blocks in this pack, and they can spawn in any dimension including your base's.

## The Wilden and the Wilden Chimera
Base Wilden mobs, all found in Wilden Dens, forests far from spawn and civilization:
- **Wilden Stalker**: 15 HP, 2.5 attack, 0 armor. Flies, dives, negates fall damage entirely, and on Hard difficulty its melee applies 40 ticks of Weakness. Drops Wilden Wing.
- **Wilden Hunter**: 20 HP, 4.5 attack, 2.0 armor. Fast and aggressive, can summon allied wolves. Drops Wilden Horn.
- **Wilden Guardian** (Defender): 25 HP, 4.5 attack, 2.0 armor. Only spawns in cold biomes, slow but a heavy hitter with a ranged attack. When hit it goes armored for 100 ticks, stops moving, takes 25% less non-bypass damage and reflects 3.0 damage to melee attackers, then fires a volley of projectiles in every direction when the armor wears off. Drops Wilden Spike.

**Wilden Chimera** (boss): 1000 HP, 8.0 attack, 6.0 armor. Summon it with the Ritual of Summon Wilden, augmented with a Wilden Spike, Wilden Horn and Wilden Wing thrown onto a lit brazier. The ritual first spawns waves of regular Wilden before the boss appears; summoning the boss does controlled, "safe" block clearing, but this pack does let its dive attack break blocks. It has 4 combat phases that switch at 75/50/25% remaining HP, each with a brief invulnerable phase-swap and more attack options unlocked: Ram (a 25-tick wind-up charge that breaks blocks and knocks back anything it hits), Dive (hovers 60 ticks then dives at 4.0 speed, impact triggers a level-4.5 explosion and spikes if it has spikes active), Rage (phase 2 and up: clears its own debuffs, buffs itself with Regeneration and Speed, and casts Launch → Pull → Amplify → Gravity), plus separate Melee and Summon goals (calls in more Wilden) that turn off while it's ramming, diving, raging or phase-swapping. It resists cold damage. On death it drops Wilden Tribute, the ingredient for the Archmage spell book.

## Archwood forests and Ars structures
Archwood trees come in 4 decorative colors and have a small chance to spawn in any biome; their fruit brews into Potions of Blasting, Recovery, Freezing or Defence. Archwood Forest is a rare biome (weight 2 in this pack) with extra gold ore and a much higher spawn chance for Starbuncles, Whirlisprigs, Drygmys and Archwood Treants. Magic decorative blocks (Mirrorweave, Falseweave, Ghostweave, Skyweave) camouflage themselves and only get revealed or hidden through Sense Magic, Invisibility or Dispel.

Ars Additions adds 3 structures, all left enabled in this pack's config: the **Arcane Library** (rare, found in Archwood Forests, Wilden-guarded loot), **Nexus Tower** (site of a Warp Nexus, a 9-destination interdimensional travel hub, free to use at the tower itself, 1000 Source per teleport once relocated), and the **Ruined Warp Portal** (a chest with a free-use Explorer's Warp Scroll).

## Create and Farmer's Delight crossovers
**Ars Creo**: Spell Turrets and Source Jars work on Create contraptions. A Basic Turret fires on interact, a Timer Turret fires on an interval, an Enchanted Turret fires per new block it visits, and a Source Jar on the contraption can feed turrets riding with it. The Starbuncle Wheel (a Starbuncle Charm plus a Create Water Wheel) makes Create rotational force, 16 RPM base and up to 24 RPM with a gold block in front, 16 SU stress capacity.

**Ars Technica**: 10 new glyphs, listed above, and the Technomancer armor set, an upgrade path from Tier-3 magic robes that needs a Mark of Mastery, Netherite Ingot, Brass Ingot and a Create Goggles, 7000 Source. Per this pack's config it gives +100 max mana and +4 mana regen per piece, reduces Create-machinery damage, and speeds up nearby Schematic Cannons (8-block range) when the full set is worn. Also adds the Precise Relay (a tunable-cooldown Source relay, config-bounded between 5 and 600 ticks or 40 to 6000 depending on setting), the Source Motor (turns Source into Create rotation), a Runic Spanner, Calibrated Precision Mechanism, Focus of Transmutation (doubles chance-outcomes on Obliterate and Whirl, doubles Obliterate damage, doubles Press/Polish/Whirl speed), Spy Monocle (a zoom spyglass), and Thread of Pressure (a high-pressure-air thread that scales with level).

**Ars Delight** (Ars Nouveau's Flavors & Delight): the **Enchanter's Knife** (6 damage, 2.0 speed, netherite durability; Farmer's Delight Diamond Knife + Diamond + Gold Block + Source Gem Block), same Touch-before-hit behavior as the Enchanter's Sword but without the free bonus Amplify.

New food effects, all stacking per level:
- **Wilden**: +max mana, +mana regen, +spell damage, 20% each per level
- **Blast Resistance**: 20% explosion damage reduction per level, immune at level 5
- **Flourishing**: restores mana on heal, same percentage as HP restored, doubling per level
- **Synchronized Shield**: grants Absorption equal to the healing amount, up to a cap that doubles per level (base cap 8.0)
- **Freezing Spell**: your spell damage also applies Freezing, matching your own Freezing Spell buff's level and duration
- **Lightning Curse**: a debuff you can inflict that lures lightning to your spell's targets

New ingredients: Archwood Barks (cut from Archwood logs, boosts or converts pod-fruit effects), Wilden Meat (a knife-kill on a Wilden Hunter, early Wilden-effect food), Chimera Meat (a knife-kill on the Chimera, bulk food with a strong Wilden effect when cooked), Chimera Horn (an axe-kill on the Chimera, a reusable food container that boosts the food effect). Archwood Pod Jelly blocks bounce falling entities and apply a jelly effect, and spell projectiles pass through them. With an Arcane Pedestal next to a Drygmy Henge holding a weapon, the Drygmy can simulate tool-based scavenging drops from Farmer's Delight and its addons. Casting spells at a cutting board simulates the matching tool: Cut acts like a Knife or Shears, Cut plus Amplify acts like an Axe or Knife, Cut plus Extract only works like Shears, Break acts like a Pickaxe, Crush like a Shovel, and Fell like an Axe. AOE cancels this and falls back to the plain spell effect.

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

# Iron's Spells 'n Spellbooks

Iron's Spells 'n Spellbooks is one of the pack's two big magic mods, next to Ars Nouveau. It gives you 9 schools of magic, over 100 spells you can level up, spell books, staves, school-themed armor sets, magic curios, and 9 structures with 2 bosses of its own. Iron's Lib is just a shared code library with nothing to look at, and Iron's Spellbooks Tweaks adds background server config plus a hidden unlock system tied to advancements and kills that isn't explained anywhere in game.

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

## Getting started

1. Craft an Inscription Table: a book in the top middle slot, 3 wooden slabs across the middle row, and 2 wooden fences in the bottom corners.
2. Craft a Flimsy Journal, the starter copper spell book: a copper ingot in each corner, string in the left middle slot, and paper filling the rest of the 3x3 grid.
3. Find a Scroll. They turn up in almost any vanilla or magic chest, and spellcasting mobs drop them too. Take a scroll to the Inscription Table to inscribe it into your book, that's how a spell gets equipped. You can also cast a scroll straight from your hand for no mana and no cooldown, but it's consumed when you do.
4. Pick up Arcane Essence whenever you see it (it shows up in vanilla structures too, not just magic ones). It's the mod's main crafting currency, used for Arcane Cloth, Arcane Ingots, runes, and the early Wandering Magician armor (leather armor + Arcane Essence, shapeless, per piece), which is your first source of bonus max mana.

Casting keys:
- Open the spell wheel: `R`
- Cast your selected spell: `V`
- Scroll through your spell bar: hold `Left Alt` and scroll

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

## Progression

Early game: loot scrolls and Arcane Essence from vanilla structures and the mod's own magic towers and huts, inscribe scrolls into a craftable book, and wear Wandering Magician armor for the early mana boost.

Mid game (Nether tier): build a Scroll Forge (polished deepslate across the top row, crying obsidian across the bottom row plus one more in the middle) to craft your own scrolls from ink, paper and a school Focus item. Ink sets the rarity and level ceiling, the Focus sets the school. Craft Gold and Diamond spell books (Diamond needs Hogskin, dropped by Hoglins), and Arcane Cloth (8 Arcane Essence + 1 wool, 3x3) for Wizard and Scarecrow (Pumpkin) armor. Runestones start dropping from magic structures and mage kills around this point, every class of mage always drops a rune of its own school.

Late game (Netherite tier): build the Arcane Anvil, the mod's master crafting station: 3 amethyst blocks across the top, a diamond, a normal anvil, and 2 polished deepslate in the bottom corners. At the Anvil you can level up scrolls (combine a scroll with next-rarity ink), upgrade armor and spell books with Upgrade Orbs, and imbue weapons with spells. Craft Netherite and Dragonskin spell books here (Dragonskin needs a Dragonskin item dropped by the Ender Dragon, both need a Ruined Book found in Ancient Cities), and craft school armor. Cryomancer or Pyromancer helmets, for example, are a wizard base helmet + that school's rune + Arcane Essence, shapeless, or via the Smithing Table using the rune as a template.

Ink tiers: Common ink only makes common-rarity scrolls. Uncommon, Rare, Epic and Legendary ink unlock progressively higher spell levels. Rare ink and above shows up regularly in magic treasure chests and from spellcasting mobs. Some spells have extra Legendary levels you can only reach through the Arcane Anvil.

Ink drop odds by chest loot table:
- Poor ink: 78% common, 20% uncommon, 2% rare
- Decent ink: 40% common, 54% uncommon, 4% rare, 2% epic
- Good ink: 29% common, 49% uncommon, 15% rare, 7% epic
- Great ink: 9% uncommon, 31% rare, 42% epic, 18% legendary

Upgrade Orbs: craft one from an Arcane Ingot, Cinder Essence and a Mithril Ingot (3x3, ingot/cinder/ingot on the top and bottom rows, cinder/orb-base/cinder in the middle), then surround it with 8 of one school's rune to get that school's Upgrade Orb. Mithril ore generates below Y=-38 with no air exposure and needs a netherite pickaxe to mine. Upgrades cap at 3 per item.

Alchemist Cauldron: 2 iron ingots, a cauldron, Arcane Essence and Cinder Essence. Use it to recycle scrolls you don't want into other magic items, and, heated with a campfire, to render mobs down into Blood Vials.

Other stations: the Pedestal is a stonecutter recipe from stone bricks, a display and ritual block. The Portal Frame (2 mithril, an Ender Pearl, 4 Arcane Ingots) links to the Portal spell to build a permanent two-way portal, and there's also a Pocket Dimension Portal Frame for the Pocket Dimension spell.

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

## How spell damage scales

Final spell damage comes from two things multiplying together: the spell's own level (set by the ink tier or scroll quality you used to make it) and your Spell Power. Spell Power splits into a global multiplier (gear commonly gives +5% or +10% per piece) and a per-school multiplier (Fire Spell Power, Ice Spell Power, and so on, typically +10% per matching-school armor piece). These stack multiplicatively with each other and with the level-based base damage, so a high-level spell cast by someone in full matching school armor hits a lot harder than the same spell at level 1 with no gear. The target's own armor then reduces the hit normally. Iron's Spellbooks Tweaks has a further global spell power multiplier config, but it's left at the default 1.0 in this pack, so it changes nothing.

Exact per-hit damage numbers aren't in the data files, spells just have a level, mana cost and cooldown that scale together. Check the in-game tooltip for the real number on a specific spell at a specific level.

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

## Schools and spells

9 core schools, each built around a crafting Focus item that sets which school a scroll or spell book belongs to: Fire (Blaze Rod), Ice (Frozen Bone), Lightning (Bottle o' Lightning, made from an empty bottle + charged creeper), Holy (Divine Pearl), Ender (Ender Pearl), Blood (Blood Vial), Evocation (Emerald), Nature (Poisonous Potato), and Eldritch, which isn't craftable or castable at all until you find and piece together an Eldritch Manuscript with an Echo Shard, after which that spell is unlocked for good.

Ranges below run from level 1 to max level. A "/s" mana cost means it drains that much per second while you hold the cast (Continuous spells).

### Fire
- **Firebolt** (levels 1-10, Common to Legendary): 10 to 28 mana, 1s cooldown. Small fire projectile, ignites on hit.
- **Fireball** (levels 1-5, Rare to Legendary): 60 to 120 mana, 25s cooldown. Thrown fireball, big explosion.
- **Fire Breath** (levels 1-10, Common to Legendary): 5 to 14 mana/s, 12s cooldown. Cone of fire.
- **Burning Dash** (levels 1-10, Common to Legendary): 20 to 38 mana, 10s cooldown. Dash that sears creatures you pass through.
- **Blaze Storm** (levels 1-10, Common to Legendary): 5 to 14 mana/s, 20s cooldown. Rapid, wild barrage of blaze fireballs.
- **Flaming Barrage** (levels 1-5, Rare to Legendary): 80 to 100 mana, 15s cooldown. Repeat-cast homing small fireballs.
- **Flaming Strike** (levels 1-5, Common to Legendary): 30 to 90 mana, 15s cooldown. Uninterruptible melee sweep, scales off your held weapon.
- **Fire Arrow** (levels 1-10, Rare to Legendary): 40 to 85 mana, 8s cooldown. Fire arrow that explodes on impact for half damage.
- **Magma Bomb** (levels 1-8, Uncommon to Legendary): 30 to 65 mana, 12s cooldown. Bomb that leaves a fire field.
- **Raise Hell** (levels 1-5, Legendary only): 90 to 270 mana, 25s cooldown. Ground slam, fiery shockwave, scales off your weapon.
- **Scorch** (levels 1-10, Uncommon to Legendary): 50 to 95 mana, 12s cooldown. Area fire eruption plus a lingering fire field.
- **Wall of Fire** (levels 1-5, Common to Legendary): 30 to 50 mana, 30s cooldown. Recast to place anchors that build into a fire wall.
- **Heat Surge** (levels 1-6, Common to Legendary): 50 to 100 mana, 45s cooldown. Self AoE ignite plus Rend, which cuts armor.

### Ice
- **Icicle** (levels 1-10, Common to Legendary): 10 to 28 mana, 1s cooldown. Piercing frost bolt.
- **Ray of Frost** (levels 1-5, Common to Legendary): 25 to 85 mana, 15s cooldown. Low-damage beam that applies Freeze.
- **Cone of Cold** (levels 1-10, Common to Legendary): 5 to 14 mana/s, 12s cooldown. Freezing cone.
- **Ice Spikes** (levels 1-10, Common to Legendary): 30 to 120 mana, 15s cooldown. Cascading ground spikes.
- **Snowball** (levels 1-5, Uncommon to Legendary): 40 to 48 mana, 12s cooldown. Thrown snowball, applies Chill in a radius.
- **Frostwave** (levels 1-8, Common to Legendary): 50 to 85 mana, 45s cooldown. Radius Chill wave.
- **Frost Step** (levels 1-8, Rare to Legendary): 15 to 50 mana, 12s cooldown. Teleport that leaves behind an aggro-drawing ice shadow.
- **Ice Block** (levels 1-6, Rare to Legendary): 40 to 90 mana, 15s cooldown. Falling ice chunk that explodes.
- **Ice Tomb** (levels 1-8, Uncommon to Legendary): 30 to 135 mana, 30s cooldown. Encase yourself, heal, and block one hit.
- **Blizzard** (levels 1-8, Rare to Legendary): 40 to 110 mana, 22s cooldown. Slow vortex that pulls creatures in.
- **Frostbite** (levels 1-5, Epic to Legendary): 80 to 120 mana, 60s cooldown. Kills on frozen targets spawn Ice Shadows.
- **Summon Polar Bear** (levels 1-10, Rare to Legendary): 50 to 140 mana, 180s cooldown. Mount/companion whose stats scale with your spell power.

### Lightning
- **Electrocute** (levels 1-10, Common to Legendary): 3 to 12 mana/s, 12s cooldown. Electrical cone.
- **Ball Lightning** (levels 1-10, Common to Legendary): 20 to 56 mana, 1s cooldown. Slow-moving orb that bounces off blocks.
- **Volt Strike** (levels 1-10, Common to Legendary): 30 to 75 mana, 10s cooldown. Spinning leap attack plus a blast.
- **Lightning Lance** (levels 1-10, Uncommon to Legendary): 50 to 140 mana, 8s cooldown. Charge and throw a spear, high single-target damage.
- **Lightning Bolt** (levels 1-10, Epic to Legendary): 75 to 210 mana, 25s cooldown. Calls down a sky lightning bolt.
- **Chain Lightning** (levels 1-10, Uncommon to Legendary): 25 to 88 mana, 20s cooldown. Cascades between nearby targets.
- **Ascension** (levels 1-10, Rare to Legendary): 50 to 59 mana, 15s cooldown. Self lightning strike that launches you upward.
- **Charge** (levels 1-3, Rare to Legendary): 50 to 100 mana, 40s cooldown. Self buff: extra damage, spell power and speed per level.
- **Shockwave** (levels 1-8, Common to Legendary): 70 to 105 mana, 30s cooldown. Electrical blast.
- **Thunderstorm** (levels 1-8, Rare to Legendary): 70 to 140 mana, 120s cooldown. AoE storm that strikes every 2 seconds for its duration.

### Holy
- **Heal** (levels 1-8, Uncommon to Legendary): 30 to 135 mana, 30s cooldown. Self heal.
- **Greater Heal** (level 1 only, Rare): 100 mana, 45s cooldown. Full self heal, slow cast.
- **Blessing of Life** (levels 1-10, Common to Legendary): 10 to 55 mana, 10s cooldown. Heal a creature you target.
- **Healing Circle** (levels 1-10, Common to Legendary): 40 to 130 mana, 25s cooldown. Stationary group heal zone.
- **Cloud of Regeneration**: numbers not pulled from the data, check in game. Hold cast to slowly heal allies in a radius.
- **Cleanse** (level 1 only, Epic): 100 mana, 60s cooldown. Removes debuffs from yourself and allies.
- **Fortify** (levels 1-10, Common to Legendary): 80 to 170 mana, 180s cooldown. Grants absorption HP to nearby allies.
- **Wisp** (levels 1-10, Common to Legendary): 15 to 33 mana, 3s cooldown. Homing holy projectile.
- **Sunbeam** (levels 1-10, Uncommon to Legendary): 40 to 130 mana, 20s cooldown. Beam smite from above.
- **Guiding Bolt** (levels 1-10, Common to Legendary): 20 to 65 mana, 8s cooldown. Marks a target so nearby projectiles home onto it.
- **Divine Smite** (levels 1-5, Common to Legendary): 30 to 90 mana, 15s cooldown. Uninterruptible melee AoE, scales off your weapon.
- **Angel Wings** (levels 1-5, Legendary only): 80 to 160 mana, 120s cooldown. Temporary elytra.
- **Haste** (levels 1-4, Epic to Legendary): 50 to 80 mana, 80s cooldown. Gives the Hastened buff to yourself or a target.
- **Wololo** (level 1 only, Legendary): 10 mana, 10s cooldown. Converts a raid illager. Also listed under the Evocation school in the mod's own files, it's the same spell.

### Ender
- **Magic Missile** (levels 1-10, Common to Legendary): 10 to 28 mana, 1s cooldown. Basic arcane bolt.
- **Teleport** (levels 1-5, Uncommon to Legendary): 20 to 40 mana, 6s cooldown. Teleport to your crosshair.
- **Shadow Slash** (levels 1-5, Common to Legendary): 30 to 90 mana, 15s cooldown. Dash slash that deflects projectiles, scales off your weapon.
- **Thunderstep**: numbers not pulled from the data, check in game. Short teleport with a lightning-wake hit.
- **Magic Arrow** (levels 1-10, Rare to Legendary): 40 to 85 mana, 8s cooldown. No-gravity piercing arrow that travels through blocks.
- **Shield** (levels 1-10, Common to Legendary): 35 to 80 mana, 8s cooldown. Stationary energy wall, blocks projectiles and creatures.
- **Counterspell** (level 1 only, Rare): 50 mana, 10s cooldown. Interrupts casting mobs and their projectiles.
- **Evasion** (levels 1-5, Epic to Legendary): 40 to 120 mana, 180s cooldown. Dodge a number of hits via teleport.
- **Portal** (levels 1-3, Uncommon to Legendary): 200 to 220 mana, 180s cooldown. Places linked two-way portals.
- **Recall** (level 1 only, Uncommon): 100 mana, 300s cooldown. Warp back to spawn.
- **Black Hole** (levels 1-6, Legendary only): 300 to 800 mana, 120s cooldown. Void fissure that sucks in and crushes targets.
- **Gravity Fissure** (levels 1-5, Epic to Legendary): 150 to 250 mana, 45s cooldown. Moving mini black hole, no damage, pure crowd control.
- **Echoing Strikes** (levels 1-5, Rare to Legendary): 50 to 90 mana, 60s cooldown. Melee hits spawn spectral echo attacks.
- **Arcane Shackle** (levels 1-8, Rare to Legendary): 40 to 96 mana, 45s cooldown. Chains bind and tether the target.
- **Summon Swords** (levels 1-5, Rare to Legendary): 80 to 140 mana, 150s cooldown. Summons 3 spectral weapon companions.
- **Summon Ender Chest** (level 1 only, Rare): 25 mana, 5s cooldown. Pulls up your Ender Chest anywhere.
- **Void Tentacles**: numbers not pulled from the data, check in game. Erupting tentacles, damage plus blind.

### Eldritch
The mod's own wiki actually groups these under a separate Eldritch page rather than Ender, and this pack's own files tag Eldritch as its own 9th school, so treat these as Eldritch, unlocked only by finding an Eldritch Manuscript and an Echo Shard.
- **Telekinesis** (levels 1-5): 25 mana, 35s cooldown. Grip and pull a creature with kinetic damage.
- **Sonic Boom** (levels 1-3): 110 to 210 mana, 25s cooldown. Passes through blocks, hits everything in its path.
- **Planar Sight** (levels 1-3): 150 to 250 mana, 200s cooldown. See through terrain.
- **Pocket Dimension** (level 1 only): 300 mana, 60s cooldown. Personal pocket-dimension retreat.
- **Abyssal Shroud** (levels 1-3): 300 to 340 mana, 200s cooldown. Full attack evasion window.
- **Eldritch Blast** (levels 1-5): 90 to 150 mana, 15s cooldown. Devastating energy rays.
- **Sculk Tentacles** (levels 1-4): 150 to 300 mana, 30s cooldown. Sculk tentacles, damage plus blind.

### Blood
- **Blood Slash** (levels 1-5, Rare to Legendary): 25 to 45 mana, 10s cooldown. Blood blade, heals you 15% of the damage dealt.
- **Blood Needles** (levels 1-10, Uncommon to Legendary): 25 to 70 mana, 10s cooldown. Ring of needles, heals 25% of damage dealt.
- **Acupuncture** (levels 1-10, Rare to Legendary): 25 to 70 mana, 20s cooldown. Needle field around the target, heals 25% of damage dealt.
- **Blood Step** (levels 1-5, Uncommon to Legendary): 30 to 70 mana, 12s cooldown. Teleport behind the target with brief invisibility.
- **Ray of Siphoning** (levels 1-10, Common to Legendary): 8 to 17 mana/s, 15s cooldown. Lifedrain beam, heals 100% of damage dealt.
- **Devour** (levels 1-10, Uncommon to Legendary): 25 to 61 mana, 20s cooldown. Pull and bite, 15% lifesteal, temporary max HP on a kill.
- **Heartstop** (levels 1-5, Rare to Legendary): 100 to 140 mana, 120s cooldown. Invulnerable now, take back 50% of that damage later.
- **Raise Dead** (levels 1-6, Uncommon to Legendary): 50 to 100 mana, 150s cooldown. Summons undead whose gear scales with your spell power.
- **Wither Skull** (levels 1-10, Uncommon to Legendary): 20 to 38 mana, 1s cooldown. Wither projectile that explodes.
- **Sacrifice** (levels 1-5, Rare to Legendary): 25 to 45 mana, 1s cooldown. Detonates your own summon for big AoE damage.

### Evocation
- **Fang Strike** (levels 1-10, Common to Legendary): 30 to 57 mana, 5s cooldown. Line of evoker fangs.
- **Fang Ward** (levels 1-8, Common to Legendary): 45 to 80 mana, 15s cooldown. Protective fang rings around you.
- **Fang Swirl** (levels 1-8, Epic to Legendary): 55 to 111 mana, 28s cooldown. Spiral fang zone.
- **Summon Vex** (levels 1-5, Rare to Legendary): 50 to 90 mana, 150s cooldown. Summons vex companions.
- **Summon Horse** (levels 1-5, Common to Legendary): 50 to 58 mana, 20s cooldown. Spectral mount, stats scale with spell power.
- **Spectral Hammer** (levels 1-5, Uncommon to Legendary): 15 to 35 mana, 2s cooldown. Mines a cube of stone-type blocks.
- **Firecracker** (levels 1-10, Common to Legendary): 20 to 38 mana, 1s cooldown. Small firework explosion.
- **Lob Creeper** (levels 1-10, Uncommon to Legendary): 20 to 38 mana, 2s cooldown. Throws a creeper head that explodes.
- **Chain Creeper** (levels 1-6, Uncommon to Legendary): 40 to 90 mana, 15s cooldown. Chaining ring of creeper-head explosions.
- **Arrow Volley** (levels 1-6, Uncommon to Legendary): 40 to 90 mana, 15s cooldown. Falling arrow barrage.
- **Gust** (levels 1-10, Uncommon to Legendary): 30 to 75 mana, 12s cooldown. Cone knockback, wall-impact damage.
- **Slow** (levels 1-4, Epic to Legendary): 50 to 80 mana, 80s cooldown. Applies Slowed to the target.
- **Invisibility** (levels 1-6, Rare to Legendary): 35 to 75 mana, 45s cooldown. True invisibility until you attack.
- **Scapegoat** (levels 1-3, Rare to Legendary): 30 to 60 mana, 45s cooldown. Decoy goat that taunts hostiles.
- **Throw** (levels 1-5, Common to Legendary): 10 to 30 mana, 8s cooldown. Throw your held item as a weapon.

### Nature
- **Poison Spray** (levels 1-10, Common to Legendary): 5 to 14 mana/s, 12s cooldown. Poison cone breath.
- **Poison Arrow** (levels 1-10, Common to Legendary): 40 to 85 mana, 15s cooldown. Poison cloud on impact.
- **Poison Splash** (levels 1-10, Uncommon to Legendary): 40 to 130 mana, 20s cooldown. Poison wave plus a lingering cloud.
- **Acid Spit** (levels 1-8, Common to Legendary): 40 to 110 mana, 15s cooldown. Acid orb splash that rends armor.
- **Aspect of the Spider** (levels 1-8, Rare to Legendary): 35 to 70 mana, 90s cooldown. Bonus damage against poisoned targets.
- **Root** (levels 1-10, Uncommon to Legendary): 45 to 72 mana, 35s cooldown. Entangles, blocks non-magic movement.
- **Blight** (levels 1-8, Rare to Legendary): 60 to 200 mana, 90s cooldown. Reduces the target's outgoing damage and incoming healing.
- **Oakskin** (levels 1-8, Common to Legendary): 25 to 95 mana, 90s cooldown. Self damage-reduction buff.
- **Earthquake** (levels 1-10, Uncommon to Legendary): 50 to 140 mana, 16s cooldown. AoE tremor damage plus slow.
- **Stomp** (levels 1-5, Uncommon to Legendary): 50 to 90 mana, 16s cooldown. Ground-tremor line attack.
- **Firefly Swarm** (levels 1-10, Uncommon to Legendary): 40 to 130 mana, 20s cooldown. Homing swarm that follows the target.
- **Gluttony** (levels 1-5, Common to Legendary): 35 mana, 90s cooldown. Turns eaten food into mana.
- **Touch Dig** (levels 1-3, Rare to Legendary): 15 mana, no cooldown. Magic block-break.

That's roughly 100+ spells across the 9 core schools, matching the mod's own "over 100 upgradable spells" claim. Higher spell level scales damage, healing or duration, and higher Spell Power from gear scales the output further on top of that (see the math above).

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

## Spell books

- **Flimsy Journal** (copper, Common): base slots. Craft: copper ingot + string + paper.
- **Ironbound Tome** (iron, Common): extra slots over the Flimsy Journal. Craft: chain + leather + paper.
- **Apprentice's Spell Book** (gold, Common): +15% Cast Time Reduction, +50 max mana. Craft: gold ingot + Arcane Essence + Hogskin.
- **Enchanted Spell Book** (diamond, Uncommon): +100 max mana. Craft: diamond + Hogskin + Arcane Cloth + an enchanted book.
- **Ancient Codex** (netherite, Legendary): +20% Cooldown Reduction, +200 max mana. Craft: netherite ingot + Arcane Cloth + Blood Vial + Ruined Book + Bottle o' Lightning.
- **Dragonskin Spell Book** (Uncommon): +10% Ender Spell Power, +200 max mana. Craft: Dragonskin (Ender Dragon drop) + obsidian + Ruined Book.
- **Druidic Tome** (Uncommon): +10% Nature Spell Power, +200 max mana. Craft: Rotten Spell Book + spore blossom + honey bottle + amethyst cluster + glow ink sac + Arcane Cloth.
- **Rotten Spell Book** (Rare): -15% Spell Resistance, +100 max mana. World loot, found in "rotten places" (ruins).
- **Villager Bible** (Uncommon): +10% Holy Spell Power, +10% Cast Time Reduction, +200 max mana. From trading with a Priest, or freeing captive villagers at an Evoker Fort.
- **Blaze Instruction Manual** (Rare): +10% Fire Spell Power, +200 max mana. Rare drop from a Blaze.
- **Grimoire of Evokation** (Unique): comes with Fang Strike level 6, Fang Ward level 4 and Summon Vex level 4 already inscribed, plus +10% Evocation Power and +200 max mana. Dropped by an Evoker or Archevoker, roughly 1.25% plus looting.
- **Necronomicon** (Unique): comes with Blood Slash level 5, Blood Step level 5, Ray of Siphoning level 5 and Blaze Storm level 5 inscribed, plus +2 levels to Raise Dead and +200 max mana. Dropped by the Dead King, 50% base chance scaled by looting.
- **Vampiric Spell Book** (cursed doll, Uncommon): +1 level to Blood Slash and Blood Step, +10% Blood Power, +10% Spell Resistance, +200 max mana. Craft: Arcane Ingot + Bloody Vellum + Ruined Book.

A Unique book's pre-inscribed spells can't be edited or removed, but you can still level them up at the Arcane Anvil by combining the book with a higher-level scroll of the same spell.

## Casting implements and staves

- **Graybeard Staff**: starter implement. Craft: wooden shovel + iron ingot + Arcane Essence.
- **Artificer's Cane**: a style upgrade in the same tier. Craft: crimson planks + gold ingot + amethyst.
- **Ice Staff**: craft (shapeless) from a Frosted Helve + Permafrost Shard, both sourced from the Cryomancer's Mountain Tower.
- **Pyrium Staff**: craft: magic cloth + netherite ingot + pyrium ingot (pyrium is a byproduct of superheated smelting).
- **Blood Staff / Improved Blood Staff**: the strongest summoning implement in the mod. Not craftable, only drops from the Dead King.
- **Rod o' Lightning**: made from a Lightning Rod + an Energized Core, which you get during a thunderstorm.
- **Hither-Thither Wand**: a teleport utility item.

## Weapons

Damage and speed below are what the tooltip shows. For comparison, a vanilla netherite sword is 8 damage at 1.6 speed, 12.8 damage per second.

- **Boreal Blade**: 16 damage, 0.9 speed (14.4 per second), 2,031 durability. The hardest single hit of the Iron's weapons. Craft: mithril + ice + Ice Venom Vial + Weapon Parts.
- **Hellrazor**: 13 damage, 1.4 speed (18.2 per second), 2,031 durability. Smithing-upgrade a Decrepit Scythe with Timeless Slurry + pyrium ingot.
- **Twilight Gale**: 13 damage, 1.4 speed (18.2 per second), 2,031 durability. Craft: mithril + Lightning Bottle + Weapon Parts + a Twilight Gale Crystal from the Twilight Forest.
- **Amethyst Rapier**: 8 damage, 2.3 speed (18.4 per second), 2,031 durability. Cheap and fast. Craft: amethyst shard + chain + Weapon Parts.
- **Spellbreaker**: 10 damage, 1.8 speed (18.0 per second), 2,031 durability, +15% cooldown reduction while held. Craft: mithril + diamond + Weapon Parts.
- **Legionnaire Flamberge**: 11 damage, 1.5 speed (16.5 per second), 2,031 durability, +4 armor while held. Smithing-upgrade a Keeper's Flamberge with Timeless Slurry + pyrium ingot.
- **Keeper's Flamberge** (Decrepit Flamberge): 11 damage, 1.3 speed, 1,000 durability, +4 armor while held. Citadel Rampart Chest, about 25%.
- **Decrepit Scythe**: 11 damage, 1.4 speed, 1,000 durability. Echo of Tyros drop, 55% plus looting.

The mod's code also has Truthseeker, Misery, Magehunter, a Claymore and the Autoloader Crossbow, but nothing in this pack drops or crafts them. The Autoloader only comes from Trial Chambers, which don't exist in 1.20.1. Don't go looking for them.

All of these take sword enchantments, and a few Iron's spells (Flaming Strike, Divine Smite, Shadow Slash, Raise Hell) hit harder the stronger your held weapon is, so a good sword also makes those spells better.

## Armor sets

Iron's armor has 0 built-in Armor Toughness on nearly everything except Netherite Battlemage (+3 per piece) and Paladin/Lightbringer (+4 per piece plus 0.4 Knockback Resistance per piece), compared to vanilla Diamond (2 toughness/piece) and Netherite (3 toughness/piece, 0.1 KBR/piece). Most of the school sets trade toughness for mana and spell power and lean on spell resistance instead of raw damage reduction. All bonuses below apply per equipped piece and stack across a full 4-piece set.

- **Wandering Magician**: 15 total armor (iron-equivalent), 0 toughness, +25 max mana per piece. Craft: leather armor + Arcane Essence.
- **Scarecrow** (Pumpkin): 20 total armor (diamond-equivalent), 0 toughness, +75 max mana per piece. Craft: Arcane Cloth + leather helmet + carved pumpkin.
- **Wizard**: 20 total armor, 0 toughness, +125 max mana and +5% Spell Power per piece. Craft: Arcane Cloth for the hood/robe/leggings/boots.
- **Pyromancer**: 20 total armor, 0 toughness, +125 max mana, +5% Spell Power and +10% Fire Power per piece. Wizard base + Fire Rune + Arcane Essence, craft or Smithing Table.
- **Cryomancer**: same as Pyromancer but +10% Ice Power per piece, using an Ice Rune.
- **Electromancer**: same pattern, +10% Lightning Power per piece, Lightning Rune.
- **Priest**: same pattern, +10% Holy Power per piece, Holy Rune.
- **Shadow-Walker**: same pattern, +10% Ender Power per piece, Ender Rune.
- **Cultist**: same pattern, +10% Blood Power per piece, Blood Rune.
- **Archevoker**: same pattern, +10% Evocation Power per piece, Evocation Rune.
- **Plagued**: same pattern, +10% Nature Power per piece, Nature Rune.
- **Netherite Battlemage**: 20 total armor, +3 toughness per piece, +125 max mana and +5% Spell Power per piece. Smithing Table: Wizard armor + netherite upgrade template.
- **Boots of Speed**: +150 max mana, +10% Spell Power, +25% Movement Speed and +60% Casting Move Speed. Craft: Mithril Weave + feathers + Archevoker Boots + Divine Soulshard.
- **Lightbringer** (Paladin) chestplate: +4 toughness, +150 max mana, +10% Spell Power, +40% Knockback Resistance. Craft: gold + Divine Soulshard + Mithril Weave + Priest Chestplate.
- **Infernal Sorcerer** chestplate: +150 max mana, +10% Spell Power, builds Immolation stacks on Fire spell hits that explode at 3 stacks. Craft: pyrium ingots + Mithril Weave + Pyromancer Chestplate + Divine Soulshard.
- **Tarnished Crown** (helmet only, unique): +150 max mana, +25% Mana Regen, -15% Attack Damage, can be Imbued at the Arcane Anvil. Unique find.

A full matching school set, 4 pieces of the same school, works out to +500 max mana, +20% Spell Power and +40% school power on top of the base 20 armor, with no toughness. Mixing pieces (say Netherite Battlemage boots with a Lightbringer chestplate) works fine since the bonuses are per piece, not tied to a full matching set.

## Curios

Rings, amulets and talismans, all requiring the Curios mod to equip (curio slots, key `G`).

- **Silver Ring**: +25 max mana. Chest loot.
- **Ring of Mana**: +100 max mana. Craft: diamond + 4 Arcane Ingot.
- **Ring of Expediency**: +15% Cast Time Reduction. Craft: amethyst shard + Mithril Scrap.
- **Ring of Recovery**: +15% Cooldown Reduction. Craft: 4 copper ingot + Mithril Scrap.
- **Emerald Stoneplate Ring**: +25% XP from kills. Craft: bottle o' enchanting + Mithril Scrap.
- **Fireward / Frostward / Poisonward Ring**: immunity to that element. Craft: Cinder Essence / Permafrost Shard / Nature Rune + Mithril Scrap.
- **Ring of Expulsion**: passive knockback burst when you're hit. Chest loot only.
- **Ring of Invisibility / Visibility**: invisibility, or the ability to see invisible things. Invisibility is chest loot; Visibility is crafted from a spyglass + Mithril Scrap.
- **Signet of the Betrayer**: +10% Eldritch Spell Power, bonus damage scaled to the target's max mana. Echo of Tyros drop.
- **Wicked Bone Ring**: +1 spell projectile ricochet. Chest loot.
- **Affinity Ring** (any school): boosts one chosen spell's level by 1. Craft: bucket + Mithril Scrap, school is randomized.
- **Amethyst Resonance Charm**: +15% Mana Regen. Craft: 4 leather + amethyst shard.
- **Amulet of Concentration**: long casts become uninterruptible. Craft: 3 chain + Mithril Scrap/ingot.
- **Amulet of Teleportation**: grants the Evasion buff after you teleport. Chest loot.
- **Amulet of Warding** (Enchanted Ward Amulet): protects against the Lost Citadel's magical ward.
- **Heavy Chain** (necklace): +15% Spell Resistance. Craft: 4 chain + Mithril Scrap.
- **Conjurer's Talisman / Greater Conjurer's Talisman**: +15% Summon Damage; the Greater version also lets timed-out summons skip their cooldown. Craft: 3 string + Mithril Scrap + skeleton skull, or + Bloody Vellum + Arcane Rune for the Greater version.

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

## Mobs and bosses

- **Dead King** (Catacombs boss): 500 HP, 10 melee damage plus spells, 15 armor, 80% knockback resistance. There's also an "ominous" version with 1,000 HP, more damage and more armor, and its own loot table. Found deep underground via a Wayward Compass. Drops 28 to 45 Arcane Essence plus looting, 2 rolls of rare-quality scroll or blank rune, 3 reward-ink rolls, and a 50% (plus looting) chance at either the Blood Staff or the Necronomicon.
- **Echo of Tyros, First Flamebearer** ("Fire Boss"): 1000 HP, 10 melee damage plus its Fire spells, 15 armor, 80% knockback resistance. Lives in the Citadel in the Nether, summoned with a Cinderous Soulcaller. It's scaled up 1.75x with 1.25x spell power, 1.25x spell resist and 1.5x fire magic resist, and changes phase at 75%, 50% and 33% HP. Drops a guaranteed Divine Soulshard, a 55% (plus looting) chance at the Decrepit Scythe or the Betrayer Signet, 2 rolls of 28 to 45 Arcane Essence, and 8 to 16 Cinder Essence.
- **Keeper** ("Ancient Knight"): 60 HP, 10 attack. Reanimated from armor piles at the Ancient Battleground in the Nether. Drops Cinder Essence plus a 25% chance of netherite scrap. A strong elite, not a full boss.
- **Necromancer**: 25 HP, 3 attack. A Blood-school caster mob.
- **Pyromancer**: 60 HP, 3 attack. Found at the Pyromancer Tower, will trade with you.
- **Cryomancer**: 60 HP, 3 attack. Found at the Mountain Tower.
- **Archevoker**: 60 HP, 3 attack. Found at the Evoker Fort, where captive villagers can be freed.
- **Priest**: 60 HP, 3 attack. Lives in Priest Houses in Repurposed Structures villages, trades the Villager Bible.
- **Cultist**: 60 HP, 3 attack. A Blood-school caster mob.
- **Apothecarist**: 60 HP, 3 attack. Found at the Mangrove Hut.
- **Cursed Armor Stand**: 60 HP, 3 attack. An animated decoy that ambushes you.
- **Ice Spider**: 50 HP, 8 attack, 20 armor. Found in the Ice Spider Den, unusually high armor for its size.
- **Wisp**: 20 HP, 3 attack. A passive Holy-themed mob, similar to the summon version.

Summon creatures have their own base stats: Summoned Horse 15 HP, Summoned Polar Bear 30 HP/6 attack, and the Summon Swords trio (Rapier 15 HP/3 attack, Sword 25 HP/5 attack, Claymore 40 HP/8 attack).

### Dark Doppelganger

A single dangerous endgame boss, added by the DarkDoppelganger addon, that copies your own build: your armor, your spells, and a 1.25x spell power multiplier on top of it. Genuinely a mirror match.

How to summon it:
1. Craft a Shadow Altar: 2 obsidian, 2 ender eyes, 2 crying obsidian and a lodestone.
2. Craft a Shadow Orb: an Echo Shard, a Nether Star, Dragon's Breath, a Ghast Tear and blackstone.
3. Place the altar. It only activates in the End.
4. Place the Shadow Orb on top of the altar, the tooltip reads "The altar copies the one who offers it." The ritual begins, and if it fails the orb is returned to you.
5. The Dark Doppelganger spawns, mirroring whatever gear and spells you're using.

Boss stats: **6000 HP**, 20 attack damage (capped at 150 damage per single hit so it can't be one-shot cheesed), 0.2 movement speed, 0.6 knockback resistance, 20 armor, 64-block follow range, and that 1.25x spell power multiplier once it's copied you. Hard mode is not enabled in this pack, so its hard-mode-only final phase spells (Eldritch Blast, Shadow Slash, Oakskin, plus some Tidal Grasp/Spectral Blink/Shadowed Miasma spells from another addon) never come up. It won't copy a Cataclysm Cursium armor set even if you're wearing one.

Its minions: the ones spawned alongside the boss itself are 100 HP/8 attack/5 armor/0.28 speed. The player-usable Summon Minion spell it can drop instead makes a 200 HP/8 attack/0 armor minion. Minions use Devour as a barrage attack plus a shuffled pool of Spectral Blink, Burning Dash, Charge, Oakskin, Shadow Slash and Invisibility.

Drops: one of Doppelganger Ring, Elder's Necklace or Summoner Necklace (evenly weighted), a guaranteed Nether Star, 3 Echo Shards, 3 Diamond Blocks and an Iron's Spells scroll, plus roughly a 50% chance of learning the Portal Jump or Summon Minion spell. The Doppelganger Ring itself gives +30 max health and +150 max mana.

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

## Structures

- **Catacombs**: an underground crypt under almost any land biome, and rare (one of the most widely spaced structures in the pack). Home to the Dead King; a Wayward Compass points to it. The Dead King's Vault holds golden apples, a small chance at an enchanted golden apple, Arcane Ingots, scrolls, runes, curios and a Dead King Phylactery Shard.
- **The Citadel**: a huge fortress in the Nether (the same biomes as bastions), and the rarest structure in the magic mods. Home to Echo of Tyros, summoned with a Cinderous Soulcaller. The Citadel Vault is the best magic chest in the pack: diamonds, Pyrium, Mithril Scrap, netherite and ancient debris, scrolls, runes, curios and a disc fragment. The Rampart Chest drops the Keeper's Flamberge around 25% of the time.
- **Ancient Battleground**: in the Nether, near bastion biomes, where Keepers (Ancient Knights) rise from armor piles. Grave goods and piglin camp loot.
- **Evoker Fort**: hills, forest, plains, desert or savanna, rare. An illager outpost with Pillagers and Vindicators around an Archevoker.
- **Pyromancer Tower**: hills, forest, plains or swamp, rare. A Pyromancer's home with Blaze Rods, fire ale and ink.
- **Mountain Tower**: mountains and snowy biomes. Home to a Cryomancer; the chest has Frozen Bone, a scroll and a small chance at a Furled Map (it leads to an Ice Spider Den).
- **Mangrove Hut**: swamps, the most common Iron's structure. Home to an Apothecarist, with potions, elixirs and a Netherward Tincture.
- **Ice Spider Den** ("Infested Ruin"): snowy biomes, rare. A branching underground den full of Ice Spiders with harvestable eggs. The dungeon chest has ink, scrolls, curios, runes, emeralds and diamonds.
- **Impaled Icebreaker**: a wrecked icebreaker ship in frozen oceans and ice spikes, rare. The captain's quarters have Epic Ink, a Permafrost Shard and curios.

- **Priest House**: added as a house inside Repurposed Structures' biome villages (birch, oak, dark forest, jungle, swamp, mountain, badlands, giant taiga, mushroom, cherry and bamboo versions). Vanilla villages are turned off in this pack, so those biome villages are where you'll find a Priest.

Iron's Spells also quietly adds bonus loot into a lot of vanilla structures and tables: Ancient City, End City, generic dungeons, the Nether, library/bookshelf loot, and treasure loot all get extra Arcane Essence, scrolls, ink, blank runes and curios mixed in.

This pack's own loot fix datapack corrects two bugs in the stock mod's loot tables. The Catacombs crypt chest had a typo that silently produced nothing from its 3-roll bonus ink pool, that's now fixed and gives you those 3 extra ink rolls per chest. The Citadel's 12 lore "Citadel Tome" written books used a book-writing function that's broken on this Forge build and would've generated blank books, the fix rewrites them so the lore books actually have their text in Citadel loot.

In the addon spell lists below, mana is written as base cost, then how much it adds per extra level.

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

## Magic From The East

Adds two more Iron's Spells schools built around Chinese and Japanese mythology: Symmetry (jade and qi, offense) and Spirit (souls and ghosts, summons and curses). Comes with 20 new spells, a full jade building-block set, two structures (Onmyoji Shrine, Taoist House), 3 new mobs (Jiangshi, Onmyoji, Taoist), a 200 HP mini-boss (Jade Executioner) summoned by a spell, jade tools/weapons/armor, and 5 enchantments for a new "spirit arrow" ranged weapon line.

Getting started: mine Jadestone Ore (a normal world-gen vein, size 10, spawns in regular stone) for Jadestone Shards. Craft a Yin Yang Core (3 flint + 3 quartz + 1 jade shard + 1 more flint or quartz), then the Taiji Sword (1 iron ingot + 1 gold ingot + 1 Yin Yang Core + 1 stick), a basic starter melee weapon that also works as a spellcasting focus. Craft Red String (string + Blood Vial) to unlock Copper Coins (3 copper ingot + 2 Red String) and the Coins Sword, and Refined Jade Ingot (4 emerald + 4 jade shard + 1 Cinder Essence) for the jade armor and gear line.

Runes and orbs: a Symmetry Rune is 8 Yin Yang Cores + Iron's blank rune, a Spirit Rune is 8 Bottles of Souls + blank rune. Each matching Upgrade Orb is 8 of that rune + Iron's upgrade orb, giving +5% of that school's spell power like the base game's orbs. Onmyoji Hat/Robes/Leggings/Geta (Spirit armor) and Taoist Hat/Robes/Leggings/Boots (Symmetry armor) are each a wizard base piece + the matching rune + Arcane Essence, craftable or smithing-transformed onto an existing wizard piece. A Bottle of Souls comes from filling an empty bottle on Soul Fire, or at the Alchemist Cauldron. A Soul Crystal comes from soaking an Echo Shard in an Alchemist Cauldron full of soul fluid.

Muramasa, the Repeating Crossbow and the Ritual Orihon spellbook have no crafting recipe in the data files. The game hints the Ritual Orihon comes from trading with an Onmyoji; check in game for the others.

### Symmetry spells
- **Sword Dance** (level 1-8): 30 mana +10/lvl, 90s cooldown. Summons magic swords that fly at your last-hurt target.
- **Bagua Array Circle** (level 1-8): 70 mana +10/lvl, 60s cooldown. Damages undead inside a circle zone, gives you Reversal Healing.
- **Dragon Glide** (level 1-10): 34 mana +4/lvl, 5s cooldown. Unstoppable dragon dash, can break magic shields on a chance.
- **Jade Judgement** (level 1-6): 65 mana +15/lvl, 60s cooldown. A giant Jade Dao falls for massive AoE impact damage.
- **Drapes of Reflection** (level 1-6): 35 mana +6/lvl, 30s cooldown. Conjures jade drapes that deflect projectiles.
- **Cloud Ride** (level 1-9): 20 mana +6/lvl, 90s cooldown. Summons a rideable magical cloud briefly.
- **Nephrite Slash** (level 1-5): 40 mana +20/lvl, 20s cooldown. Ground-spike jade slash ahead of you.
- **Jade Bullet** (level 1-10): 10 mana +5/lvl, 5s cooldown. Thrown jade bullet, small shockwave on impact.
- **Jiangshi Invoke** (level 1-7): 30 mana +10/lvl, 150s cooldown. Summons 3 Summoned Jiangshi at 20 HP each.
- **Punishing Heaven** (level 1-4): 250 mana +100/lvl, 320s cooldown. Summons a Jade Executioner (200 HP) that beheads enemies.

### Spirit spells
- **Underworld Aid** ("Impermanence's Verdict") (level 1-4): 180 mana +90/lvl, 160s cooldown. 4 spirits judge a circle: slowness, blindness, darkness plus damage over time scaling up to 40% of the target's max HP.
- **Soul Catalyst** (level 1-8): 35 mana +5/lvl, 60s cooldown. Barrage of soul skulls, applies Soulburn.
- **Soul Burst** (level 1-10): 40 mana +10/lvl, 30s cooldown. Soul-fire explosion at your position, burns souls in range.
- **Spirit Challenging** (level 1-5): 65 mana +20/lvl, 120s cooldown. Extracts part of the target's soul, damage to the soul transfers to you.
- **Bone Hands** (level 1-7): 40 mana +10/lvl, 60s cooldown. Spirit hands erupt from the ground and slam targets in range.
- **Devastating Cut** ("Calamity Cut") (level 1-5): 30 mana +18/lvl, 25s cooldown. Melee cut that scales off your weapon, stronger weapon means a stronger cut.
- **Kitsune Pack** (level 1-10): 30 mana +15/lvl, 180s cooldown. Summons a pack of Kitsune at 15 HP each, soulburn bite.
- **Revenant of Honor** (level 1-5): 80 mana +35/lvl, 240s cooldown. Summons a loyal Spirit Samurai protector (50 HP).
- **Ashigaru Squad** (level 1-8): 60 mana +20/lvl, 180s cooldown. Summons a squad of Spirit Ashigaru at 20 HP each.
- **Phantom Charge** (level 1-6): 45 mana +15/lvl, 80s cooldown. Summons Phantom Horsemen that charge the enemy line.
- **Anchoring Kunai** (level 1-8): 20 mana +10/lvl, 90s cooldown. Throws 3 kunai, hits apply Anchored Soul, teleporting leaves a soul echo.
- **Splitting Shuriken** (level 1-10): 15 mana +3/lvl, 3s cooldown. Shuriken splits into smaller ones (half damage) on hit.

### Gear
- **Taiji Sword**: starter weapon and focus. Craft: iron + gold + Yin Yang Core + stick.
- **Spirit Crusher**: 18 damage, 0.6 speed, +20% spell resistance, -10% move speed while held. Deals soul damage as a percent of the target's current HP, plus Slowness and Weakness below 30% HP. One of the hardest single hits in the pack. Craft: Crystallized Soul + gold block + warped stem + Weapon Parts.
- **Jade Guandao**: 12 damage, 1.5 speed, +2 armor toughness while held. A polearm. Craft: Refined Jade + gold + jade + Red Shaft.
- **Muramasa**: 8 damage, 2.0 speed, +15% Blood spell power. No recipe found, likely a trade, check in game.
- **Masamune**: 7.5 damage, 2.1 speed, +15% Spirit spell power. Craft: gold + Crystallized Soul + Weapon Parts.
- **Soul Breaker**: 6 damage, 1.5 speed, +10% spell resistance. Flat bonus soul damage plus Slowness below 60% of the target's HP. Craft: Yin Yang Core + Crystallized Soul + Weapon Parts.
- **Repeating Crossbow / Ritual Orihon**: high-tier gear with no recipe, likely Onmyoji or Taoist trades, check in game.
- **Soulpiercer**: a mana-fueled crossbow that scales off Spirit Spell Power.
- **Coins Sword → Rusted Coins Sword**: upgrade with a Blood Rune and Blood Vials for +25% Jiangshi-style vampirism.
- **Taoist armor set** (Symmetry) and **Onmyoji armor set** (Spirit): wizard-tier armor via the craft/smith pattern above.
- **Jade Pagoda set** (helmet, chest, legs, boots, horse armor): a heavier non-spellbook armor line. Craft: chainmail + Refined Jade + gold + Bloody Vellum.
- **Elemental Commander Robes**: a chestplate that buffs your next spell of the same school based on Symmetry Spell Power when you land a spell hit (Fire +10% damage/speed, Nature +10% knockback resist and 4 armor, Lightning +10% move speed/mana regen, Ice +10% spell resist/cooldown reduction, Holy +10% cast time reduction and 4 max HP). Craft: Taoist Chestplate + Divine Soulshard + Mithril Weave + Bloody Vellum + Copper Coins + Yin Yang Core.
- **Jade Pendant** (curio): reflects 50% of damage taken in a radius, plus 5% more reflect per point of armor toughness. Craft: Red String + Refined Jade + Symmetry Rune.
- **Mist Boots** (curio): negates a hit and grants True Invisibility plus Mist Step for 6 seconds. Craft: Onmyoji Boots + Divine Soulshard + Mithril Weave + Phantom Membrane.
- **Soulward Ring** (curio): soulburn immunity, -80% soul damage taken. A 33% drop in the Onmyoji Shrine's hall loot room.
- **Enchantments**: Soul Flame (soulburn on hit), Ghostly Cold (freeze on hit), Spiritual Focus (boosts Spirit Spell Power scaling), Wisely Will (reduces mana per shot), Expanding (more loadable projectiles), Inner Impact (boosts Soul Breaker/Spirit Crusher scaling).

### Mobs
- **Jade Executioner**: 200 HP, 9 attack, 12 armor. A mini-boss summoned by the Punishing Heaven spell, not a wild spawn.
- **Jiangshi**: 40 HP, 3 attack, 4 armor. A wild hopping-corpse mob, 2% (times looting) drop of a Jiangshi Hat.
- **Summoned Jiangshi**: 20 HP, 4 attack, 4 armor. From the Jiangshi Invoke spell.
- **Onmyoji**: 60 HP, 3 attack. Found near or spawning at the Onmyoji Shrine, drops Spirit Rune, ink and scrolls.
- **Taoist**: 60 HP, 3 attack. Found near the Taoist House, drops Symmetry Rune, ink and scrolls.
- **Spirit Samurai**: 50 HP, 10 attack, 8 armor. Summon from Revenant of Honor.
- **Spirit Ashigaru**: 20 HP, 4 attack, 4 armor. Summon from Ashigaru Squad.
- **Summoned Kitsune**: 15 HP, 3 attack. Summon from Kitsune Pack.

### Structures
- **Onmyoji Shrine**: found in Cherry Grove/Meadow biomes. Loot rooms drop scrolls, Arcane Essence, good and poor ink, Bottle of Souls, a 33% chance at a Soulward Ring in the hall, a 10% chance at an Echo Shard, and basic curio loot.
- **Taoist House**: found in Bamboo Jungle/Sparse Jungle biomes. Its coffin loot room (15 to 21 rolls) leans bone, rotten flesh and blood vial heavy; storage rooms give copper coins, jade, Yin Yang Cores, ink and scrolls; a hidden storage room is dedicated to Rice Wine Bottle (5 to 9 rolls) and Vase of Rice Wine.
- Jadestone Ore itself isn't structure-locked, it's a normal ore vein in regular stone and deepslate.

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

## Wind's Spellbooks

A single Wind school with 7 spells (one disabled in this pack), a Wind Staff/Book of Tempests spellbook line, a Wind Rune and Upgrade Orb, an Aeromancer mob, and a Windmill structure.

Tornado is disabled in BSCraft. The pack's own config comment says it "crashed dedicated servers," so it won't cast even if you find a scroll or book with it on it, that's not a bug.

Getting started: craft a Wind Staff (1 Arcane Essence + 1 Blaze Rod) as your first casting focus, or find a Book of Tempests, a 33% drop from an Aeromancer or a guaranteed find in a Windmill structure chest (which also guarantees a Wind Staff and some scroll rolls). Craft a Wind Rune (8 wind-focus tag items + blank rune) into a Wind Upgrade Orb, and craft Aeromancer armor (wizard base pieces + Wind Rune + Arcane Essence, craft or smithing).

### Spells
- **Wind Jump** (level 1-3): 30 mana, 5s cooldown. Launches you into the air.
- **Tornado** (level 1-5, disabled in this pack): 200 mana +50/lvl, 100s cooldown. Would summon a tornado that sucks nearby mobs in and launches them.
- **Iron Slash** (level 1-5): 30 mana +15/lvl, 8s cooldown. Focus then dash-slash through enemies.
- **Aeropic** (level 1-4): 30 mana +5/lvl, 12s cooldown. Spinning leaping voltaic attack, repeatable while you keep hitting.
- **Almighty Push** (level 1-10): 40 mana +5/lvl, 15s cooldown. 360-degree wind knockback around you, wall-impact damage.
- **Wind Blade** (level 1-10): 15 mana +2/lvl, 1s cooldown. Forward blade-of-wind slash, cheap and fast to spam.
- **Tailwind** (level 1-3): 50 mana +25/lvl, 40s cooldown. Self buff, extra speed and air mobility.

### Gear
Wind Staff (starter focus), Book of Tempests (spellbook), Wind Rune, Wind Upgrade Orb, and the full Aeromancer armor set (Hat/Coat/Leggings/Boots, wizard base + Wind Rune craft/smithing). No unique weapons beyond the staff.

### Mobs and structures
- **Aeromancer**: 60 HP, 3 attack. A wild-spawning wizard mob, drops Arcane Essence, a scroll, good-tier ink, and a 33% chance at a Book of Tempests.
- **Windmill**: found in Plains/Sunflower Plains/Meadow biomes. Loot includes a guaranteed Wind Staff, a spyglass, 1 to 2 scrolls, and a 30% chance at basic curios. Note that base-pack Dungeons and Arise "Illager Windmill" loot and vanilla Create windmill bearing loot are separate tables that just happen to share the word "windmill," they're not part of this addon.

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

## Apprentice's Codex

Despite its own description calling itself a "small collection," this is the biggest addon here: 84 distinct spells plus dozens of "arcane creations," permanent constructed items like turrets, a companion chest, a healing flower, a search beacon, a permafrost totem, and overseer staves. It adds 20+ unique weapon and staff types, around 15 curios, and its own crafting stations, but no new mobs, spell schools, ores or biomes. It's pure spell and gear content layered onto the existing 9 schools.

Getting started: craft a Spellcaster Workbench, the mod's central crafting station for spellcaster items and spell scroll conversion. Cheap early picks include Shock (a 1-second-cooldown lightning bolt) and Mage Light (a free-standing torch-light spell). There's also a tiered Spellcaster Gun line: Copper, Iron, Gold, Diamond and Malignant, each a step up from the last.

Other stations: Spell Calibration Bench (spell tuning/rerolling), Atelier Station, Alchemy Brewer, Essence Smoker, Apprentice Desk, and a Magnetic Stability Anchor. There are also Iron and Silver Spell Amplifiers (gear/curio upgrades), and named crafts including the Spellstained Runic Tablet, Spell-Side Edge dagger pair, Spellcharged Greatsword, and Spellcaster's Flask.

All 84 spells below, grouped by role. Mana is written as base cost, then how much it adds per extra level.

### Summoned weapons and turrets
- **Sky Edge** (level 1-5): 80 mana +15/lvl, 8s cooldown. Wind-up weapon fired on your sightline, ignores i-frames, no knockback.
- **Archer Multiple** (level 1-5): 70 mana +30/lvl, 30s cooldown. Summons a following magic bow that auto-attacks.
- **Commence Fire** (level 1-5): 100 mana +30/lvl, 12s cooldown. Summons a following rifle, use again for a powerful shot.
- **Breaching Enemy** (level 1-5): 60 mana +15/lvl, 12s cooldown. Short-range magic shotgun, huge knockback.
- **Bullet Stream** (level 1-3): 3 mana/s +2, 12s cooldown. Sustained gatling barrage with a spin-up delay, no knockback, ignores i-frames.
- **Fly Swatter** (level 1-3): 10 mana +5/lvl, 12s cooldown. Homing-shot launcher locked to your target.
- **Auto Turret** (level 1-3): 80 mana +20/lvl, 12s cooldown. Autonomous crossbow turret, right-click to refill its ammo for mana.
- **Silent Assassin** (level 1-3): 100 mana +40/lvl, 16s cooldown. One-shot self-destructing sniper rifle, huge damage on unaware targets.
- **Lethal Assault** (level 1-5): 20 mana +5/lvl, 1.5s cooldown. Self-destructing burst rifle, no knockback, ignores i-frames.
- **Dual Acrobat** (level 1-5): 5 mana/s +5, 12s cooldown. Twin SMGs spray after a brief charge.
- **Artisan Smash** (level 1-3): 40 mana +20/lvl, 8s cooldown. Grenade-launcher shell, wide blast, doesn't destroy blocks or items.
- **Tiro Volley** (level 1-3): 13 mana +3/lvl, 20s cooldown. Summons muskets around you that auto-fire, no knockback.
- **Field Overseer** (level 1-5): 50 mana +20/lvl, 30s cooldown. Autonomous lightning staff, stores mana based on level, prioritizes the highest-HP target.
- **Servant Gaze** (level 1-5): 75 mana, 150s cooldown. Following magic-bolt staff tied to you, dispel removes it too.

### Melee and weapon-summon spells
- **Slash Blade** (level 1-10): 10 mana +3/lvl, 1.5s cooldown. Summoned katana wide slash, partially pierces blocks, uncancellable.
- **Higanbana** (level 1-5): 40 mana +20/lvl, 4s cooldown. Cursed blade flurry, no knockback, heals 50% of damage dealt.
- **Moonlight** (level 1-3): 120 mana +30/lvl, 8s cooldown. Single flash-cut that also dispels magic effects and spellcasting, uncancellable.
- **Mantis Leap** (level 1-3): 70 mana +15/lvl, 8s cooldown. Leap-shred with twin blades, uncancellable, no knockback on the leap.
- **Phalanx Charge** (level 1-4): 15 mana +5/lvl, 4s cooldown. Spear and shield guard stance, then a forward thrust, uncancellable.
- **Fujin** (level 1-10): 5 mana +1/lvl, 12s cooldown. Wind-wreathed katana flurry, cuts through groups without knockback.
- **Shiden** (level 1-5): 50 mana +15/lvl, 10s cooldown. Upward katana slash, partial block penetration.
- **Bound Sword** (level 1): 50 mana +150/lvl, 30s cooldown. Temporarily forges a lighter sword in hand, dual-wields with Better Combat.
- **Bound Bow** (level 1): 50 mana +150/lvl, 30s cooldown. Temporarily forges a bow that can fire arrows made from mana.
- **Edge Dancer** (level 1, Spell-Sided Edge only): 50 mana, 30s cooldown. Copies your main-hand blade to your offhand.
- **Anchor Blink** (level 1, Reverse Edge only): 80 mana, 30s cooldown. Throws a dagger, jump within 3 seconds to blink to it.
- **ManaSlash** (level 1, Crystal Bladed Staff only): 30 mana, no cooldown. Mana-blade projectile, creates mana-restore orbs on hit.

### Utility, support and exploration
- **Mage Light** (level 1): 20 mana, 0.5s cooldown. Free-standing magic torch.
- **Wizardlamp** (level 1): 50 mana, 0.5s cooldown. Free-standing magic lantern.
- **Personal Shelf** (level 1): 50 mana, 5s cooldown. Temporary personal vault interface.
- **Companion Trunk** (level 1): 10 mana, no cooldown. Following arcane chest with real chest capacity, auto-stashes on despawn.
- **Healing Bloom** (level 1-3): 100 mana +20/lvl, 300s (5 min) cooldown. Places a healing flower, 1 per caster, that grows Comfort Berries over time.
- **Search Beacon** (level 1-3): 100 mana +40/lvl, 60s cooldown. Offer a school-tied item to find nearby structures with colour-coded markers.
- **Totem of Permafrost** (level 1-5): 30 mana +15/lvl, 12s cooldown. Placed totem pulses cold and slows nearby enemies.
- **Auto Magnet** (level 1-5): 40 mana +10/lvl, no cooldown. Following magnet vacuums up items and XP, sneak toggles reverse mode.
- **Grind Runner** (level 1-3): 20 mana +5/lvl, 20s cooldown. Arcane grinding wheel, launch it to mow a path, integrates with Create crushing.
- **Remote Eye** (level 1-3): 80 mana +25/lvl, 30s cooldown. Detach your vision and fly a camera around with no wall-clip, you're defenseless while it's active.
