- **Sense Evil** (level 1-3): 70 mana +25/lvl, 10s cooldown. Highlights nearby unholy beings and spawners through walls.
- **Deep Sensor** (level 1-3): 60 mana +20/lvl, 100s cooldown. Sculk-sensor-like vibration sense through walls, suppresses your own noise.
- **Long Stride** (level 1, Explorer's Cane only): 12 mana -1/lvl, 1200s (60s) duration. Walk on water and lava, step up obstacles, faster stride.
- **Spectral Wing** (level 1): 150 mana, 2s cooldown. Mana-wing launch into a glide, recast for a directional surge.
- **Illuminate Stellar** (level 1, latent staff): 80 mana +80/lvl. Homes stars onto malevolent entities.
- **Unite Luna** (level 1, latent staff): 160 mana +160/lvl. Crescent-moon blast, reflects dispel attempts into more power.
- **Demicreator Wings** (level 1-3): 300 mana +100/lvl, 90s cooldown. Places a core granting creative flight while inside its radius.
- **Assist Wings** (level 1-3): 20 mana, no cooldown. Leap-flap wings, usable midair a limited number of times, negates fall damage.
- **Feather Rush** (level 1-5): 4 mana +2/lvl, 12s cooldown. Flap wings to scatter feathers, no fall damage while flapping.
- **Mirage Avoidance** (level 1): 50 mana, 3s cooldown. Brief total damage evasion plus a directional glide.
- **Otherworld Lens** (level 1): 35 mana, 5s cooldown. Conjures a reversed-perspective viewing lens, breaks on obstruction.
- **Mystic Shield** (level 1-3): 15 mana +3/lvl, 15s cooldown. Frontal barrier that releases a counter-projectile based on absorbed damage.
- **Force Field** (level 1-3): 15 mana, 15s cooldown. Omnidirectional knockback field that can repel some spells, costly per use.
- **Rift Hole** (level 1-3): 150 mana +50/lvl, 10s cooldown. Carves a temporary tunnel through a wall.
- **Linear Build** (level 1): 20 mana, 0.5s cooldown. Places held blocks in a line along the targeted face.
- **Earth Forge** (level 1): 20 mana, 1s cooldown. Flattens a dirt patch at your sightline, single block while sneaking.
- **World Flatter** (level 1-3): 10 mana +5/lvl, 20s cooldown. Drill pierces the target through armor, carves large holes into ground.
- **Treasure Divination** (level 1-3): 20 mana, 30s cooldown. Senses distance to the nearest valuable-looking thing.
- **Terra Resonance** (level 1-3): 80 mana +10/lvl, 10s cooldown. Detects gemstones like amethyst through walls.
- **Tiny Lumberjack** (level 1-3): 10 mana +5/lvl, 20s cooldown. Circular-saw weapon, fells trees on hit.
- **Harvest Moon** (level 1-4): 60 mana +20/lvl, 20s cooldown. Harvests crops around you non-destructively and pulls them to you.
- **Tamer's Pocket** (level 1): 50 mana, 4s cooldown. Stores or recalls one tamed pet in a pocket dimension.
- **Manifestation Grimoire** (level 1): 100 mana, 60s cooldown. Domain-linked grimoire usable from any summoned copy.
- **Mana Charge** (level 1-3): free, 120s cooldown. Rapid mana regen during the cast.
- **Mana Mending** (level 1-3): 25 mana, 30s cooldown. Channels mana into a damaged held item to repair it.
- **Mana Transcription** (level 1): 500 mana, 4s cooldown. Extracts one enchant from your main-hand item onto a Book and Quill.
- **Palette Shift** (level 1, latent, Pastel Staff): 50 mana, 30s cooldown. Dyes the Pastel Staff with your next spell cast.
- **Echo Cast** (level 1, latent, Multicast Echo Staff): 50 mana, no cooldown. Your next non-Echo spell triggers multiple times.
- **Call Broom** (level 1): 20 mana, 1s cooldown. Summons, mounts or recalls your carried broom.
- **Divine Possession** (level 1, latent, Maiden of Elements set): 600 mana +200/lvl, 300s (5 min) cooldown. Your strongest school bonus temporarily applies to all your other schools too.

### Damage, elemental and misc
- **Shock** (level 1-10): 15 mana +2/lvl, 1s cooldown. Lightning bolt converted from elemental energy.
- **Arcane Blast** (level 1-10): 20 mana +4/lvl, 1s cooldown. Detonates arcane power in the target, bypasses armor, weak against demons.
- **Arcane Beam** (level 1-5): 5 mana/s +5, 12s cooldown. Continuous piercing beam, bypasses armor, weak against demons, boosted by mana fed into it.
- **Magic Spear** (level 1-5): 30 mana +30/lvl, 0.5s cooldown. Thrown spear, bursts on impact hitting nearby targets.
- **Frost Rune** (level 1-5): 50 mana +10/lvl, 8s cooldown. Hidden ground rune, detonates when a non-caster touches it.
- **Inscribe Ice** (level 1-5): 30 mana +15/lvl, 2s cooldown. Fan of ice daggers, the 3rd inscription on a target bursts for big damage.
- **Blood Brand** (level 1-5): 35 mana +10/lvl, 4s cooldown. Kunai brands the target, its death triggers an AoE burst that heals you 50% of that damage.
- **Combustion Jet** (level 1-5): 30 mana +10/lvl, 8s cooldown. Heat-wave push in your look direction, no damage to fire-immune targets.
- **Catch Flame** (level 1-8): 10 mana +3/lvl, 0.5s cooldown. Close-range ignite that hits through i-frames, can light an Essence Smoker.
- **Thermal Process** (level 1-3): 10 mana +5/lvl, 20s cooldown. Flamethrower that also smelts held smeltable items.
- **Precision Jack** (level 1-4): 60 mana +30/lvl, 1s cooldown. Knife cleave with boosted drop rates, needs charge-up rotations.
- **Compound Phial** (level 1-10): 10 mana +4/lvl, 1s cooldown. Instant-brewed potion throw, bypasses armor, can hit yourself.
- **Extract** (level 1, latent, Alchemist's Flask): 30 mana +90, 0.5s cooldown. Throws the flask's extracted contents as a potion.
- **Gracedrain** ("Graced Rain") (level 1-3): 15 mana +5/lvl, 20s cooldown. Healing rain cloud that also nourishes plants.

### Gear
A large item list: elemental bows, focus staff-bows, the Pastel Staff, Revolver Cast Staff, Mithril Free-Cast Staff, Multicast Echo Staff, Charged Twinblade Staff, Crystal Bladed Staff, Zenith Staff, Smash-Cast Scepter, Mana Force Blade, Spellcharged Greatsword, Spell-Side Edge, Swing Staff, Scroll Caster Gauntlet, and a Multipurpose Staff Rifle. Plus around 15 curios: Absorption Amplify Amulet, Archivist's Grimoire, Attack Cast Ring, Autocast Amulet, Circlets, Craftsman's Delight, Ender Grimoire, Explorer's Codex, Isekai Travel Guidebook, Jumpcast Charm, Magicompressor Gadget, Mana Shield Charm, Mana Thruster, Protection Spell Supporter, Satellite Follow-Cast Amulet, Spellcaster Ammo Pouch, Spellcaster Quiver, Spellcast Parrying Ring, and the Spellstained Runic Tablet. The Spellcharged Greatsword is 8 damage at 1.1 speed with netherite durability (craft: 2 netherite ingots + 2 Arcane Ingots + Mithril Scrap). Exact stats for the rest weren't pulled from the data, so check tooltips or JEI in game.

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

## Geomancy Plus

Adds the Geo school: 14 base spells plus 2 more, Solar Beam and Solar Storm, that only come from defeating Umvuthi, the Sunbird, an existing Mowzie's Mobs boss already in the pack, not a new mob. Also adds a Geomancer (60 HP) and Geo Grandmaster (180 HP) NPC, a Geomancer Tower structure, and a rideable Seismic boulder mount.

Getting started: find a Geomancer or Geomancer Tower for a guaranteed Geo Rune drop, then craft a Geo Upgrade Orb. Good early picks are Chunker (rolling dirt block), Dripstone Bolt (fast, cheap projectile) and Fissure.

Solar Chorus, Solar Beam and Solar Storm are gated behind Mowzie's Mobs bosses specifically: Solar Chorus only comes from an Umvuthana Raptor (30% scroll drop), and Solar Beam/Solar Storm only from Umvuthi, the Sunbird itself (50% scroll drop, random between the two). These aren't craftable or found any other way.

### Spells
- **Dripstone Bolt** (level 1-10): 10 mana +2/lvl, 1s cooldown. Fast shard that shatters for 50% splash damage.
- **Fissure** (level 1-8): 15 mana +15/lvl, 15s cooldown. Traveling fissure erupts in a damaging spike.
- **Chunker** (level 1-6): 70 mana +50/lvl, 22s cooldown. Rolling dirt boulder, damage and knockback on its path.
- **Eroding Boulder** (level 1-5): 50 mana +16/lvl, 30s cooldown. Boulder explodes on contact, applies Erode, which shreds armor and resist.
- **Tremor Spike** (level 1-5): 50 mana +30/lvl, 25s cooldown. Expanding rings of spikes, damage plus knock-up.
- **Weeping Wall** (level 1-5): 20 mana +20/lvl, 45s cooldown. Obsidian pillar wall shield, releases stored damage when it breaks or expires.
- **Petrivise** (level 1-3): 60 mana +60/lvl, 25s cooldown. Two stone walls slam together, crushing and funneling targets.
- **Weeping Cluster** (level 1-4): 40 mana +40/lvl, 25s cooldown. A crying-obsidian cluster stores damage dealt and detonates it later.
- **Crystalline Conduit** (level 1-4): 30 mana +30/lvl, 60s cooldown. Amethyst cluster grants Geo Spell Power in a radius, restores mana with Pillar resonance.
- **Geo Conductor** (level 1-3): 60 mana +60/lvl, 75s cooldown. Small pillar buffs nearby allies' armor and spell resist, a resonance anchor.
- **Pillar of the Resounding Earth** (level 1-6): 70 mana +30/lvl, 75s cooldown. Big pillar, periodic shockwaves shred armor and resist, resonates with other geo entities.
- **Tremor Step** (level 1-3): 40 mana +20/lvl, 75s cooldown. Sprint-triggered shockwaves plus a move speed buff.
- **Seismic Surf** (level 1-3): 40 mana +40/lvl, 90s cooldown. Rideable rising boulder that auto-moves forward, drops you safely near the end.
- **Solar Chorus**: numbers not pulled from the data. Summons an Umvuthana Raptor plus 2 Umvuthana Cranes, only obtainable from an Umvuthana Raptor.
- **Solar Beam**: numbers not pulled from the data. Continuous solar beam line damage, only from defeating Umvuthi, the Sunbird.
- **Solar Storm**: numbers not pulled from the data. Continuous sunstrikes on nearby enemies plus ignite, only from defeating Umvuthi, the Sunbird.

### Gear
Geo Rune, Geo Upgrade Orb, Codex of Shattered Horizons (craft: Magic Cloth + Ruined Book + Geo Rune + a Mowzie's Mobs Earthrend Gauntlet + sand), The Stonecaller (craft: Emerald + Geo Rune + a Mowzie's Mobs Sculptor Staff + Bluff Rod + sand), Ring of the Resounding Earth (traded from the Geo Grandmaster, resonates Pillar shockwaves from the wearer too), Bracelet of Inversion (traded, crouch-cast inverts Weeping Cluster to absorb your own damage instead at -70% capacity), Geo Echo (rare Geomancer Tower chest find or Geo Grandmaster trade), Bluff Instruction Manual (0.1 to 0.4% drop from Mowzie's Bluff, or a Geo Grandmaster trade), Measure of Stone (found in Geomancer Tower).

### Mobs and structures
- **Geomancer**: 60 HP, 3 attack, 11 armor. A wild NPC with a guaranteed Geo Rune drop.
- **Geo Grandmaster**: 180 HP, 3 attack, 15 armor. A wild NPC that trades several unique curios and items.
- **Seismic Ride**: 800 HP. The rideable boulder from the Seismic Surf spell, not a hostile mob.
- **Geomancer Tower**: the addon's structure.

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

## Twilight Spellbooks

Adds no new spells or spell schools of its own, it's a pure boss-loot expansion for the Twilight Forest: 8 themed spellbooks and grimoires (one per boss, containing random high-tier spells of the relevant flavor), matching rings and curios, 3 unique weapons, and 3 new alchemical inks (Unusual, Royal, Twilight), plus a Knightmetal ring pair (deactivated to activated).

Drops from Twilight Forest bosses:
- **Naga**: 75% chance of Naga's Grimoire.
- **Lich**: 75% chance of Lich's Secret.
- **Minoshroom**: 75% chance of Minoshroom Fury.
- **Hydra**: 75% chance of Hydra Codex.
- **Knight Phantom**: 12.5% chance of Knight's Memories.
- **Ur-Ghast**: 75% chance of Urghast Chronicle.
- **Alpha Yeti**: 75% chance of Yeti's Scream.
- **Snow Queen**: 75% chance of Queen's Diary, plus a separate 20% roll for Queen's Crown.

Since this addon has no unique spells of its own, these spellbooks almost certainly grant randomized Iron's Spells of a themed rarity and school when they generate, rather than fixed unique spells, check in game to confirm exact contents.

### Recipes
Ironwood Ring: Ironwood Ingot + Mithril Scrap. Ironwood Tome: Ironwood Ingot + Arcane Essence + paper. Fiery Ring: 2 Fiery Tears/Blood + Ironwood Ring. Fiery Spell Book: Fiery Ingot + Arcane Ingot + Magic Cloth + paper + Fiery Blood/Tears. Fiery Halo: Royal Ink + Fiery Ingot + gold ingot + Mithril Scrap. Frozen Ring: Alpha Yeti Fur + Ice Bomb + Arctic Fur + Ironwood Ring. Ring of Thorns: diamond + Thorn Rose + Ironwood Ring + Royal Ink. Naga Chain: Naga Scale + Iron's Heavy Chain Necklace. Knightmetal Ring: Knightmetal Ingot + Mithril Scrap, then activated with Twilight Ink. Steeleaf Katana: Steeleaf Ingot + Twilight Ink + Steeleaf Sword + netherite ingot. Steeleaf Spell Book: Steeleaf Ingot + Knightmetal Ingot + Arcane Rune + Magic Cloth + Twilight Ink. Headcutter: a Netherite Minotaur Axe + Twilight Ink (the axe itself is a smithing-transform on the Twilight Forest's Diamond Minotaur Axe). Carminite Resonance Charm: Twilight Forest Carminite + Iron's Amethyst Resonance Charm. Cursed Pearl: Iron's Divine Pearl + Blood Vial. Cursed/Divine Reliquary: Cursed Pearl or Divine Pearl + Twilight Forest Red Thread. Knight's Talisman: Mithril Scrap + Unusual Ink + Iron's Conjurer's Talisman + Knightmetal Ingot.

The 3 ink types (Unusual, Royal, Twilight) each have Alchemist Cauldron brew/fill/empty recipes, a separate tiered ink system used specifically for unlocking these spellbooks' higher spell slots.

The two weapons worth knowing:
- **Headcutter**: 12 damage, 1.0 speed, +20% Blood spell power and +100 max mana while held. Made from a Netherite Minotaur Axe + Twilight Ink.
- **Steeleaf Katana**: 11 damage, 1.0 speed, +20% Nature spell power and +100 max mana while held. Steeleaf Sword + Steeleaf Ingots + netherite ingot + Twilight Ink.

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

## Alex's Caves Spellbooks

A small Blood-school addon (uses Iron's existing Blood school, no new school) that crosses over with Alex's Caves: 2 summon spells, 2 armor sets, and a direct loot-table override on the Vallumraptor.

Getting started: hunt a wild Vallumraptor for hides, then craft the Primordial Mage Helmet (Elder Vallumraptor Hide + Heavy Bone), Chestplate (6 Vallumraptor Hide + 2 Heavy Bone), and Leggings (Tough Hide + Elder Vallumraptor Hide + 2 Heavy Bone); no boots piece was found in the data, check in game. For Dread Robes, get Iron's own Cultist armor pieces first, then smithing-transform each with Alex's Caves' Pure Darkness (template) and Shadow Silk (addition).

### Spells
- **Conjure Vesper** (level 1-4): 50 mana +10/lvl, 75s cooldown. Summons a Vesper ally (28 HP, 4.5 attack).
- **Conjure Grottoceratops** (level 1-2): 280 mana +10/lvl, 75s cooldown, very costly and slow to cast. Summons a Grottoceratops ally.

The lang files also mention a "Demonic Howl" with no matching spell class, likely an armor-set passive effect on Dread Robes or Primordial Mage rather than a castable spell.

### Gear
- **Primordial Mage set** (3 pieces, no boots): 3 armor per piece (9 total), 2 toughness per piece, enchantability 25. Each piece gives +85 max mana, +5% Nature spell power and +5% summon damage.
- **Dread Robes** (4 pieces): 23 armor total, 0 toughness, enchantability 25. Each piece gives +150 max mana, +5% spell power and +20% Blood spell power, so a full set is +600 mana, +20% spell power and +80% Blood power. That's the strongest single-school caster armor in the pack, and it has more armor than netherite, just no toughness.

The addon completely overrides the Vallumraptor's loot table: regular Vallumraptors now guarantee a Vallumraptor Hide, and Elder Vallumraptors guarantee an Elder Vallumraptor Hide, replacing whatever base Alex's Caves gave.

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

## Refined Mod

A small addon with no new school: 9 weapon-scaling spells (Frost Strike, Thunder Strike, Moon Slash, Storm Blade and others), a wolf-summon spell, a hunter's-mark spell, and 3 unique craftable weapons. It depends on Alex's Caves and Citadel for their code libraries, but doesn't actually consume items from either mod in its recipes, and adds no loot tables of its own.

### Spells
- **Frost Strike** (level 1-5): 25 mana +15/lvl, 10s cooldown. Weapon-scaling ice sweep, uninterruptible, fully-Chilled targets get entombed.
- **Thunder Strike** (level 1-5): 35 mana +15/lvl, 15s cooldown. Weapon-scaling lightning bolt, uninterruptible, scales with your enchants too.
- **Moon Slash** (level 1-4): 35 mana +15/lvl, 15s cooldown. Weapon-scaling moonlight wave.
- **Storm Blade** (level 1-7): 20 mana +5/lvl, 8s cooldown. Weapon-scaling thunderous emission.
- **Tidal Wave** (level 1-7): 35 mana +15/lvl, 25s cooldown. AoE wave, knockback plus damage.
- **Glintstone Pebble** (level 1-10): 12 mana +2/lvl, 1s cooldown. Fast tracking star-projectile.
- **Hunter's Mark** (level 1-5): 50 mana +10/lvl, 60s cooldown. Self buff, non-magic hits on a marked target deal bonus damage.
- **Create Bonfire** (level 1-3): 40 mana +20/lvl, 60s cooldown. Summons a bonfire granting Regeneration and Absorption while active.
- **Summon Wolves** (level 1-10): 50 mana +10/lvl, 180s cooldown. Summons a pack of loyal wolves.

### Gear
- **Flame Tongue**: 10 damage, 1.8 speed (18 per second), netherite durability, +10% Fire spell power and +10% fire resistance, and it comes with a spell imbued. Craft: Arcane Ingot + netherite ingot + Weapon Parts.
- **Summer Rain**: 10 damage, 1.8 speed, diamond durability, +10% spell power and +10% spell resistance, also spell-imbued. Craft: Arcane Ingot + lapis + Weapon Parts.
- **Hunter's Bow**: craft: Graybeard Staff + string + Nature Rune.

Both swords are cheap for what they do, since the weapon-scaling spells in this mod get stronger with a better held weapon.

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

## Farmer's Spell

Adds the Gluttony school (a Farmer's Delight crossover): 6 spells, a large food and drink menu (30+ new dishes, several restoring a percent of max mana), a new set of stations (Arcane Cooking Pot, Cindersoul Stove, Wisdomwood Cabinet), Gluttony-themed wizard armor, and the friendly Foodgeist NPC you feed for buffs. The Foodgeist is not a boss, and Bad Apple isn't a wild boss either, it's the entity thrown by the Rotten Apple spell.

Getting started: craft an Alchemist Pot (Hogskin Slice + Arcane Essence + Arcane Ingot + Farmer's Delight Cooking Pot) to unlock Gluttony's magical recipes; some need a specific scroll in the pot's scroll slot, for example a Blaze Scroll removes the heat-source requirement. Drink Pumpkin Juice for 5% max mana, Goodberry Juice or Butterbeer for 10%, or Ink Beer for a massive 50% max mana. Craft a Foodgeist Ring (Gluttony Rune + Mithril Scrap, +75 max mana while Nourishment is active), then a Gluttony Affinity Ring (Foodgeist Ring + Foodgeist Cheese + Primal Nectar, +150 max mana while Nourished) once you're feeding Foodgeists regularly.

### Spells
- **Goodberry** (level 1): 30 mana, 10s cooldown. Conjures an emergency-food berry.
- **Rotten Apple** ("Bad Apple") (level 1-5): 50 mana, 30s cooldown. Hurls a rotten apple that enrages hostile mobs near it.
- **Grease Coating** ("Seal Coat") (level 1-5): 30 mana +5/lvl, 35s cooldown. Self buff, changes inertia and cast duration; eating butter mid-effect heals you when hit.
- **Chaotic Pastry Slash** ("Chaos Slash") (level 1-5): 8 mana +2/lvl, 1s cooldown. Blade slash that "tenderizes" targets, amplified from behind.
- **Brining Ritual** ("Preserve Circle") (level 1-10): 30 mana +2/lvl, 40s cooldown. AoE zone reduces the aggression of creatures inside it.
- **Phantom Loot** (level 1): 100 mana, 10s cooldown. A one-shot loot-burst spell on weak enemies.

### Gear and mobs
Demon-Binding Chef's Toque/Apron/Leggings/Boots (Gluttony wizard armor, craft: wizard base + Gluttony Rune + Arcane Essence), Gluttony Rune and Gluttony Upgrade Orb, Foodgeist Ring, Gluttony Affinity Ring. Wheat Tome and Tiramisu Tome (spellbooks) have no craft recipe found, likely loot or reward only, check in game. Several themed cooking-knife weapons exist too (Gospel Butter Knife, Inferno Chef's Blade, Cherrywood Ladle, Iris Flavor Fork, Harvest Sickle, Twilight Gale Blade, Boreal Paring Knife, Echo Dagger), exact stats not pulled from the data, check in game.

- **Foodgeist**: 20 HP. A friendly food-spirit NPC, feed it food to earn Foodgeist Cheese, Ham or Seasoning.
- **Rotten Apple (Bad Apple entity)**: 100 HP, 10 armor. The thrown/summoned entity from the Rotten Apple spell, not a wild boss.

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

# Creatures

Five animal mods add well over 150 creatures. Alex's Mobs (about 89 mobs) matters most, because its drops craft real gear. Naturalist adds realistic wildlife (lions, elephants, bears, deer), Spawn adds ocean and beach life, and Ecologics adds coconut crabs, penguins and squirrels. Spawn rates are the mod defaults, except that the Void Worm is summon-only. Mob Plaques (`Shift` + `J`) shows a mob's health and armor above its head, and Jade shows its name and which mod it's from.

Villagers are their own thing in this pack and have their own page.

## Alex's Mobs gear & who drops it
- **Dimensional Carver**: carves a portal home from anywhere; shatter it in a Capsid for a one-way trip 1,000,000 blocks out. Made from 2 Void Worm Mandibles, a Void Worm Eye and 2 netherite ingots. Tier S.
- **Transmutation Table**: turns junk into rarer items, 3 levels each (breaking it can explode). Made from a Nether Star, 2 Farseer Arms and obsidian. Tier A.
- **Tarantula Hawk Elytra**: an upgraded elytra, boosted by the Sunbird Blessing. Made from an Elytra and 2 Tarantula Hawk Wings. Tier A.
- **Cave Centipede Leggings**: lets you climb walls. Made from 7 Centipede Legs. Tier A.
- **Outback Leggings**: 45% chance to dodge projectiles. Made from Emu Feathers and Kangaroo Hide. Tier A.
- **Grappling Squok**: a grappling-hook crossbow. Made from Lost Tentacles (from a Giant Squid), a crossbow and copper. Tier A.
- **Unsettling Kimono**: makes most undead neutral toward you. A 10% drop from Murmurs only, no recipe. Tier A.
- **Hemolymph Blaster**: the top-tier sprayer weapon. Made from a Blood Sprayer, Warped Mosco parts and Mimicream. Tier A.
- **Echolocator**: reveals nearby caves. Made from Cachalot Whale teeth, ambergris and iron. Tier B.
- **Falconry Glove + Hood**: carry and launch a tamed Bald Eagle to scout. Made from Leather plus Bear Fur or Roadrunner feather. Tier B.
- **Rocky Chestplate**: sprint to roll like a boulder. Made from 7 Rocky Shells. Tier B.
- **Spiked Turtle Shell / Froststalker Helmet**: an upgraded turtle helmet, or Froststalkers follow you. Made from Spiked Scutes, or a Froststalker Horn plus packed ice. Tier B.
- **Tendon Whip / Vine Lasso**: a whip attack, or capture mobs. Made from Dropbear Claws and tendons, or vines, a lead and snake skin. Tier B.
- **Flying Fish Boots / Roadrunner Boots**: glide out of water, or move faster on sand. Made from Flying Fish or Roadrunner feathers. Tier C.
- **Ghostly Pickaxe**: a ghost pickaxe from the Underminer (mineshafts only). Made from the Underminer. Tier B.

## Dangerous or notable mobs
- **Skreecher** (Alex's Mobs): can summon a Warden, enabled in this pack.
- **Warped Mosco** (Alex's Mobs): a mini-boss made when a Crimson Mosquito transforms; never spawns naturally.
- **Crocodile, Anaconda, Komodo Dragon, Grizzly** (Alex's Mobs): heavy-hitting predators in swamps, jungles and forests.
- **Froststalker** (Alex's Mobs): pack hunter in snow biomes.
- **Cachalot Whale** (Alex's Mobs): smashes wooden boats and ships when angry, beaches itself in storms.
- **Mimicube / Mimic Octopus** (Alex's Mobs): copy your weapon (Mimicube only in End Cities).
- **Barbed (skeletons in water) / land Drowned** (Spawn): skeletons turn into Barbed in water; Drowned can walk on land at night.
- **Rhino, Elephant, Hippo, Lion** (Naturalist): rhinos charge through blocks, lions hunt.
- **Rotten Creatures variants** (Rotten Creatures): Frostbitten, Swampy, Burned, Mummy, Undead Miners (drop ore), Zap, Dead Beard (treasure key), Immortal (2.5%).
- **Graveyard undead** (The Graveyard): Revenants, Ghouls, Reapers, Nightmares, Skeleton Creepers around graveyards and crypts.

## Tames and mounts
How to tame and ride all of these is in the mounts and companions section.

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

# Villagers and families

Minecraft Comes Alive Reborn replaces every villager with a person: a name, a face, a gender, a personality, a mood and a family. They still take jobs at workstations and still trade the way you expect, so you can treat them as shops and ignore the rest. If you don't, there's a whole side of the pack here: befriending them, hiring them, marrying one, raising children, and keeping a village safe.

The villagers already living in the server's world were converted when this went in. Their professions, trades and levels were kept. Villager experience and any reputation discounts you'd built up were reset, so prices start fresh.

## Talking to them
- Right-click a villager to open their screen. It shows their age, mood, personality and happiness, who their parents are, and whether they're single, engaged, married or widowed.
- The buttons across that screen are: **Talk**, **Interact**, **Command**, **Work**, **Trade**, **Clothing**, **Inventory**, **Village** and **Hire**.
- **Interact**: gift, hug, kiss and picking up children. A good gift raises their hearts and a bad one doesn't, and what counts as good depends on the villager.
- **Command**: follow me, stay here, move freely, ride a mount, go home, and setting their home, workplace and hangout.
- **Work**: sets a chore, one of prospecting, hunting, cooking, fishing, chopping or harvesting. Mostly for your own family and villagers you've hired.
- Villagers you've befriended can send you mail, and a few of them keep written records of village events.

-# The pack has MCA's chat AI and text-to-speech turned off, so villagers speak in the mod's own written lines. Nothing is sent anywhere.

## Marriage and children
- Rings and the other relationship items are made at the **Jeweler Workbench** from **Rose Gold** (dust, ingots, blocks).
- A **Bouquet** starts things off. An **Engagement Ring** and then a **Wedding Ring** marry you to a villager once you have enough hearts; the rose gold versions do the same and look better.
- A **Matchmaker's Ring** marries two villagers to each other. **Divorce Papers** end a marriage.
- You can also marry another player: `/mca propose <name>`, and they answer with `/mca accept <name>`.
- A married couple can have a baby. You carry it as an item, name it, and after about 20 minutes of holding it, it's ready to grow into a child. You can't drop a baby.
- Children grow up, can be given chores, and count as family. The **Whistle** calls your family to you, and the **Family Tree** book shows how everyone is related.
- The **Villager Tracker** finds a villager by name, as long as you remember the name.

## Guards and running a village
- Roughly one in six villagers is a **guard**, carrying iron gear. Guards are a normal profession here, not a separate mob.
- The **Blueprint** manages the village you're standing in, including how many guards it has and the marriage limit.
- In this pack guards don't hunt ordinary monsters on sight. They go after the real threats to a village: zombies, pillagers, evokers, vindicators, vexes and the like.
- A village screen also lets you **execute** or **pardon** villagers, for when someone turns out to be a problem.
- Inns draw in wandering traders, adventurers and the occasional cultist, so villages aren't always just villagers.

## Death, and what you can do about it
- MCA villagers can die for good, which matters more here than in vanilla because they're the same people you married or raised.
- If a villager you were close to dies (about ten hearts or more), a gravestone appears for them. Headstones come in a dozen styles if you want to build a proper graveyard.
- The **Staff of Life** revives a dead villager at their tombstone.
- The **Scythe** catches the soul of a villager you kill. Reviving that soul brings them back undead, so it's not a kind way to do it.
- Villagers can catch an **infection**, and babies can be born infected. The mod's own book, Beware the Infection!, covers the cure.
- The **Grim Reaper** is a real entity in this mod, and there's an advancement for beating him.

## Your own character
- The first time you join you get the **Destiny** screen: it sets where your story starts and lets you adjust how you look. You only get it once.
- **Needle and Thread** changes your clothes later, and the **Comb** changes your hairstyle.

## Worth knowing
- Villagers wander more than vanilla ones. If a trader you rely on goes missing, the Villager Tracker or their home setting usually sorts it out.
- Jobless villagers happen: a villager whose workstation was taken or blocked may not pick up a new job on its own. Placing a free workstation nearby is the usual fix.
- Iron golems still spawn and still defend the village, alongside the guards.
- Keep a backup before you ever remove this mod from a world. Taking MCA out deletes every villager it created.

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

# Mounts and companions
Everything you can tame, ride or bring along, grouped by mod.

## Alex's Mobs
- **Bald Eagle**: tame with Fish Oil. Launch it from a Falconry Glove to scout and attack.
- **Cosmaw**: tame with Cosmic Cod (a regular cod put in a Capsid). Flies. Found in the End.
- **Elephant**: tame as a baby with Acacia Blossoms. Rideable and can carry a chest.
- **Grizzly Bear, Gorilla, Capuchin**: tame the bear with honey and salmon, the gorilla and capuchin with bananas. They guard you. Capuchins also steal items.
- **Tarantula Hawk, Mantis Shrimp**: tame with Spider Eyes and Tropical Fish. A flying attacker and a strong puncher.
- **Crocodile, Froststalker, Flutter**: breed them with rotten flesh, porkchops and flowers.

## Alex's Caves
- **Subterranodon**: tame by feeding it Trilocaris Tails. Flies.
- **Tremorsaurus**: stun it with the Primitive Club, then feed it Serene Salad. Rideable. 150 HP, 14 damage.
- **Vallumraptor**: feed it Serene Salad after it steals a Dinosaur Nugget. A pack hunter ally.
- **Atlatitan**: feed it Serene Salad (temporary). Rideable. A 400 HP giant that flattens terrain as it walks.
- **Gum Worm**: lure it with a Gobthumper and ride it with two Candy Cane Hooks. Burrows and leaps.
- **Candicorn, Raycat, Gummy Bear**: tame with Caramel Apples, Radgills, or by breeding with Sweetish Fish. Raycats soak up your radiation.
- **Tremorzilla**: its egg only hatches from a nuclear blast, then you tame the baby with 4 or more Waste Drums. Rideable. 500 HP and a 100-block beam.
- **Deep Ones**: win them over with Pearl offerings at an Abyssal Altar. They fight alongside you and leave gifts.
- **Any mob**: a Totem of Possession lets you control a mob remotely from up to 32 blocks away.

## Naturalist
- **Naturalist animals**: each species has its own taming food, like cake for baby elephants and melons for baby hippos. Some are rideable with chest saddles.

## Dragon Mounts
- **Dragons**: 60 HP, 8 damage, can't be knocked back. There's no dragon armor or dragon chest in this version, and each dragon can breed twice.
- **Getting an egg**: the only source is the Ender Dragon. A new egg shows up on the exit portal every time it's killed. Right-clicking the egg teleports it like in vanilla, so push it with a piston or drop it onto a torch to pick it up.
- **Breeds**: where you leave the egg while it incubates decides the breed. Lava or fire nearby makes Fire, the Nether makes Nether (+8 armor), snow or ice makes Ice, water or coral makes Water, leaves, flowers or a jungle make Forest, below Y 0 in darkness (light 3 or less) makes Ghost, above Y 200 makes Aether (fastest flier, +0.39 flying speed), and leaving it alone gives End (+75 HP).
- **Raising one**: keep the chunk loaded while it hatches (the egg's tooltip shows progress). Tame the baby with raw fish, and it grows up in about an hour. A Bone toggles sit and follow.
- **Riding**: put a Saddle on it. `Space` takes off and climbs (it needs 3 blocks of headroom), `W` flies where you look, `Z` goes down, and `F6` switches camera-steered flying.

## Magic mounts and companions
- **Summoned mounts**: Iron's Summon Horse and Summon Polar Bear spells (they get stronger with your spell power), the Ars Summon Steed glyph, Magic From The East's Cloud Ride, Geomancy Plus's Seismic Surf boulder, and Apprentice's Codex's broom. They're temporary.
- **Familiars** (Ars Nouveau): bind a Starbuncle, Whirlisprig, Wixie, Drygmy, Bookwyrm or Amethyst Golem with the Ritual of Binding and summon it as a companion that gives you a passive buff.
- **Helpers** (Ars Nouveau): Starbuncles, Whirlisprigs, Wixies, Drygmies and Amethyst Golems can also work at your base instead.
- **Shikigami** (Adam's Ars Plus): the Ten Shadows glyph summons shikigami you've earned, like Mahoraga.
- **Summoned fighters**: Iron's Raise Dead, Summon Vex and Summon Swords, Magic From The East's Spirit Samurai, Ashigaru and Kitsune, and Ars Summon Wolves, Undead and Vex.
- **Mermaids** (Ars Elemental): tame one with a sea pickle.

## Other mods
- **Flying Pig** (BetterNether): mount it directly in the Nether Jungle. Flies. Only 6 HP.
- **Wither Skeleton Horse** (Bygone Nether): found in catacombs. Rideable.
- **Tiny Bird** (Twilight Forest): tame it in the Firefly Forest. A small flying pet.
- **Umvuthana** (Mowzie's Mobs): wear the Sol Visage and up to 10 masked warriors fight for you.

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

# Bestiary

This one's long. Every regular (non-boss) creature from 16 mods. HP, damage and armor were pulled from each mod's own code for this pack, and drops from its loot tables. Blank stats mean the mob uses vanilla defaults. Use Ctrl+F to find a mob by name. Bosses are in the Bosses section.

## Alex's Mobs 1.22.9
Spawn rates are the mod defaults; none are disabled in this pack. "Where" lists the main biomes, and mobs also show up in modded look-alike biomes.
- **Alligator Snapping Turtle**: swamp/river, neutral (snaps if provoked), 18 HP, 4 damage, 8 armor built in. Strong bite, ambush predator.
- **Anaconda**: mangrove swamp, hostile constrictor, 40 HP. Not tameable, but leashable with a Vine Lasso. Coils and squeezes players; long multi-segment body, 10 HP per segment.
- **Anteater**: jungle, passive, 20 HP, 6 damage. Breed with pupae. Eats termite mounds; there's an "Aunt Anteater" breeding advancement.
- **Bald Eagle**: hills/windswept forest, neutral, 16 HP, 5 damage. Tame by feeding Fish Oil. Drops 0 to 1 Feather. The Falconry Glove and Hood (Leather + Bear Hair) let you launch a tamed eagle to attack.
- **Banana Slug**: old-growth taiga, passive, 4 HP, 1 damage. Drops 0 to 2 Banana Slug Slime. Rare spawn.
- **Bison**: meadow/plains, passive but retaliates in herd, 40 HP, 8 damage. Drops 6 to 8 Beef, 0 to 2 Bison Fur. Herding AI, good early food source.
- **Blobfish**: deep ocean, passive, 3 HP, bucketable. Drops a Blobfish item, 5% chance of Bone Meal. Decorative/food.
- **Blue Jay**: forest/taiga/cherry grove, passive, 10 HP, 1 damage. Drops 0 to 1 Feather.
- **Bone Serpent**: Nether, hostile by default, 25 HP, 5 damage. The Serpentfriend enchant on a Straddleboard makes it neutral toward the rider. Drops a Bone Serpent Tooth, 10 to 15 Bone, 1 to 4 Bone Block. Long segmented burrower, dangerous in groups.
- **Bunfungus**: overworld, rare mushroom mob, passive, 80 HP, 8 damage. Drops 0 to 2 Red Mushroom. Very tanky, basically a living mushroom mound.
- **Cachalot Whale**: deep/lukewarm ocean, passive, 160 HP, 30 damage. Biggest HP passive mob in the pack. Drops a Whale Echo item used for the Echolocator and Strange Fish Finder.
- **Caiman**: mangrove swamp, neutral, 20 HP, 3 damage, 8 armor.
- **Capuchin Monkey**: jungle/mangrove, passive, 10 HP, 2 damage. Tame with bananas. Occasionally steals items from players.
- **Catfish**: river/overworld, passive, 10 HP, bucketable. Drops 0 to 1 Raw Catfish.
- **Cave Centipede**: overworld caves, hostile, 10 HP body / 35 HP head, 8 damage, 6 armor. Multi-segment mob. Drops 0 to 2 Centipede Leg, used to craft wall-climbing Leggings.
- **Cockroach**: overworld caves, passive nuisance, 6 HP. Drops 0 to 1 Wing Fragment. Infests player bases if left alone.
- **Comb Jelly**: frozen ocean, passive, 6 HP. Drops 0 to 2 Rainbow Jelly; Rainbow Jelly plus Sponge makes Rainbow Glass.
- **Cosmaw**: the End, hostile flier, 20 HP, 1 damage. Drops 0 to 1 Chorus Fruit. Fires void-orb projectiles.
- **Cosmic Cod**: the End, passive, 4 HP, bucketable. Drops a Cosmic Cod item, 5% chance of Bone Meal. End-biome fish.
- **Crimson Mosquito**: Crimson Forest, hostile blood-drainer, 10 HP, 5 damage. Drops a Mosquito Proboscis, and a Blood Sac once fed. Feeds on the player, and is the first stage toward becoming a Warped Mosco.
- **Crocodile**: river/mangrove swamp, hostile, 30 HP, 10 damage, 8 armor. Drops 0 to 2 Crocodile Scute and an Egg; scutes craft the Crocodile Chestplate.
- **Crow**: forest/taiga/cherry grove, passive but steals shiny items, 8 HP, 1 damage. Befriend it with gifts. Drops 0 to 1 Feather. Can be sent to fetch items.
- **Devil's Hole Pupfish**: overworld, rare, passive, 2 HP, bucketable. The rarest fish in the pack; there's a "One Chunk Fish" advancement for it.
- **Dropbear**: Nether wastes, hostile ambush mob that drops from "trees," 22 HP, 2 damage. Drops 0 to 2 Dropbear Claw, used to craft the Tendon Whip grapple.
- **Elephant**: savanna, passive, 85 HP, 10 damage. Tame with Acacia Blossoms, or tame the tusked baby. Rideable with a chest saddle; huge HP pool.
- **Emu**: badlands/savanna, passive but kicks back if hit, 20 HP, 3 damage. Drops 0 to 2 Emu Feather plus a regular Feather; the Emu Feather crafts Emu Leggings that dodge projectiles.
- **Endergrade**: the End, neutral, 20 HP, 2 damage. 0 base armor but still tanky for an End mob.
- **Enderiophage**: the End, hostile flier, shoots rockets, 20 HP, 2 damage. Drops 1 Capsid.
- **Farseer**: overworld border, rare, hostile "defender of the world border," 70 HP, 4.5 damage, 6 armor. Drops 0 to 1 Farseer Arm. Found at the edge of the explored world.
- **Flutter**: lush caves, passive, 8 HP, 1 damage, 0.21 armor. Tame with any of several flower kinds. Drops 0 to 1 Spore Blossom.
- **Fly**: overworld, passive pest, 2 HP, 1 damage. Drops 0 to 2 Maggot. Spawns around rotten flesh and corpses.
- **Flying Fish**: ocean, passive, 6 HP, bucketable. Drops a Flying Fish item, 5% chance of Bone Meal. Leaps out of the water.
- **Frilled Shark**: deep ocean, hostile ambush predator, 20 HP, 3 damage.
- **Froststalker**: frozen peaks/ice spikes, hostile, 24 HP, 4.5 damage, 2 armor. Drops a Froststalker Horn. Mountain predator.
- **Gazelle**: savanna, passive, fast and skittish, 8 HP, 2 damage. Drops 0 to 1 Mutton, 0 to 1 Gazelle Horn. A prey animal that flees.
- **Gelada Monkey**: overworld plains/plateau, passive, 18 HP, 4 damage. Lives in troops.
- **Giant Squid**: deep ocean, hostile, grabs with tentacles, 38 HP, 8 damage. Drops 4 to 8 Ink Sac, plus a Lost Tentacle that crafts the Squid Grapple, a crossbow-based grapple hook.
- **Gorilla**: jungle, neutral but the silverback is aggressive, 30 HP, 7 damage. Tame with bananas. Rideable once tamed.
- **Grizzly Bear**: forest/taiga, neutral but aggressive, 55 HP, 8 damage. Tame with honey and salmon. Drops 0 to 2 Bear Fur plus Bear Dust. High-HP brawler.
- **Guster**: overworld dry/sandy biomes, neutral, throws sand, 16 HP, 1 damage. Drops a Guster Eye, 0 to 3 Sand. Sand, Red Sand and Soul Sand variants match the biome.
- **Hammerhead Shark**: warm ocean, hostile, 30 HP, 5 damage. Fast swimmer.
- **Hummingbird**: jungle/flower biomes, passive, 4 HP, 0 damage. Pollinates flowers.
- **Jerboa**: desert, passive, 4 HP. Fast hopper.
- **Kangaroo**: badlands/savanna, neutral but boxes if provoked, 22 HP, 4 damage. Drops 0 to 2 Kangaroo Hide, 1 to 2 Meat. Carries a joey in its pouch.
- **Komodo Dragon**: jungle, hostile, venomous bite, 30 HP, 4 damage. Bite applies poison damage over time.
- **Laviathan**: Nether lava, hostile, a shark that swims only in lava, 60 HP, 1 damage, 10 armor. Drops 0 to 3 Magma Block, 0 to 3 Blackstone.
- **Leafcutter Ant**: jungle, passive colony mob, 8 HP, 2 damage. The queen variant spawns Ant Pupa.
- **Lobster**: beach/stony shore, neutral, pinches, 5 HP, 2 damage, 2 armor. Drops 0 to 1 Lobster Tail.
- **Maned Wolf**: overworld/savanna, passive, 16 HP, 2 damage. Feed it an apple (there's an advancement for it). A tall-legged, fox-like canine.
- **Mantis Shrimp**: warm ocean/mangrove, hostile, punches hard enough to break blocks, 20 HP, 3 damage, 8 armor. Tame it with repeated Tropical Fish feedings. Lives on land once tamed, as long as you keep a water bucket handy.
- **Mimic Octopus**: warm ocean, neutral, disguises as other fish, 16 HP, 2 damage. Drops 0 to 1 Ink Sac.
- **Mimicube**: End Cities, hostile, disguises as blocks, 30 HP, 2 damage. Drops Mimicream. Copies End City block textures.
- **Moose**: overworld/snowy taiga, neutral but charges, 55 HP, 7.5 damage. Drops 1 to 3 Moose Ribs. High HP.
- **Mudskipper**: mangrove swamp, passive, 12 HP, 2 damage. Drops Tropical Fish. Amphibious.
- **Mungus**: overworld, rare mushroom mob, passive, 15 HP.
- **Murmur**: overworld, rare, neutral, disguises as blocks or villagers, 30 HP, 3 damage. 10% chance to drop the Unsettling Kimono (makes undead neutral), plus 0 to 2 Elastic Tendon. Not always what it looks like.
- **Orca**: cold ocean, neutral, hunts other sea mobs, 60 HP, 10 damage.
- **Platypus**: river, passive, 10 HP.
- **Potoo**: dark forest, passive, camouflages, 8 HP, 1 damage. Drops 0 to 1 Feather.
- **Raccoon**: forest/taiga, passive but steals items, 9 HP, 2 damage. Drops a Raccoon Tail.
- **Rain Frog**: desert, only during rain, passive, inflates when threatened, 6 HP.
- **Rattlesnake**: badlands/desert, hostile, venomous, 8 HP, about 2 damage plus poison. Drops 0 to 1 Rattlesnake Rattle.
- **Rhinoceros**: savanna, neutral but charges, 60 HP, 8 damage, 12 armor. Very tanky charger.
- **Roadrunner**: badlands/desert, passive, fast, 8 HP, 1 damage. Drops a Roadrunner Feather plus 0 to 2 Feather; crafts Roadrunner Boots for a desert speed boost.
- **Rocky Roller**: dripstone caves, passive, rolls into a ball to flee, 10 HP, 2 damage, 20 armor while rolled. Drops a Rocky Shell, 0 to 2 Tuff, 0 to 2 Dripstone; shell crafts the Rock Shell Chestplate for a roll-dash.
- **Sea Bear**: ocean/beach, rare, hostile, amphibious, 200 HP, 8 damage. Huge HP pool, a threat on both land and sea.
- **Seagull**: beach, passive but steals food, 8 HP, 1 damage. Drops 0 to 2 Feather.
- **Seal**: cold beach/ocean, passive, 10 HP, 2 damage.
- **Shoebill**: overworld swamp, neutral, spears fish, 10 HP, 4 damage. Drops 0 to 4 Feather.
- **Skelewag**: deep ocean near sunken ships, hostile pirate skeleton, 20 HP, 3 damage. Drops a Skelewag Sword, Novelty Hat, Fish Bones, 0 to 2 Bone. Fish Bones plus Slimeball plus an Echolocator craft the Strange Fish Finder.
- **Skreecher**: overworld, rare sculk mob, hostile, loud shrieker, 2 HP, 1 damage. Drops a Skreecher Soul, which crafts the explosive Sculk Boomer.
- **Skunk**: forest, passive, sprays if scared, 8 HP, 1 damage. Its spray crafts a Stink Bottle, then the Stink Ray dart gun.
- **Snow Leopard**: frozen peaks, hostile apex predator, 30 HP, 6 damage.
- **Soul Vulture**: Soul Sand Valley, neutral scavenger, 12 HP, 4 damage. Drops 0 to 2 Bone, 0 to 1 Coal (the heart-fed variant also drops a Soul Heart). Feed it a Soul Heart to befriend it, or use it to lure a Spectre.
- **Spectre**: the End, neutral, lured by a Soul Heart, 50 HP, 2 damage. Lead it with a leash after luring. Ghost-like, ridable via lead.
- **Straddler**: Basalt Deltas, passive mount that walks on lava, 28 HP, 2 damage, 5 armor. Feed it to tame; craft a Straddleboard from Straddlite. Drops Straddlite, 0 to 3 Basalt. Safe way to cross lava.
- **Stradpole**: Basalt Deltas, passive, the baby Straddler, 4 HP. Grows into a Straddler.
- **Sugar Glider**: birch forest, passive, glides, 8 HP, 2 damage. Tame with food; rides on your shoulder.
- **Sunbird**: mountain peaks, passive, 20 HP, 2 damage. Its Blessing and Curse potion effects tie into gear covered elsewhere in this guide.
- **Tarantula Hawk**: desert, hostile, paralyzing sting, 18 HP, 5 damage, 4 armor. Tame with Spider Eyes. Drops 0 to 1 Wing Fragment, which crafts the Tarantula Hawk Elytra upgrade.
- **Tasmanian Devil**: forest, hostile, bites hard for its size, 14 HP, 2 damage.
- **Terrapin**: river, passive, 10 HP, 10 armor (shell). Bucketable, 6 color variants plus a secret "Koopa" variant.
- **Tiger**: bamboo jungle/cherry grove, hostile apex predator, 50 HP, 12 damage. Highest raw damage of any non-boss Alex's Mobs entry.
- **Toucan**: jungle, passive, 6 HP. Drops 0 to 1 Feather.
- **Triops**: desert temporary pools, passive, 4 HP. Called a "living fossil."
- **Tusklin**: ice spikes/snowy plains, neutral, has tusks, 40 HP, 9 damage. Drops 3 to 6 Porkchop, 0 to 1 Snowball.
- **Underminer**: overworld caves, rare, neutral, mines ahead of you, 20 HP, 3 damage. Break the block it's mining to steal a Ghostly Pickaxe or reveal hidden ore.
- **Warped Mosco**: Nether Warped Forest, spawning is disabled, it only appears through the Crimson Mosquito feeding chain. Hostile mini-boss, 100 HP, 10 damage, 10 armor. Drops Warped Muscle, 1 to 5 Hemolymph Sac. Full fight notes are in section 3 of this guide.
- **Warped Toad**: Warped Forest, passive, 30 HP, 2 damage. Tame with Mosquito Larvae. Drops 0 to 1 Shroomlight, 0 to 1 Nether Wart. Eats and repels Crimson Mosquitoes.
- **Void Worm**: summon-only, doesn't spawn naturally. Hostile final boss, 160 HP (segmented), 5 damage. Drops 1 Void Worm Eye, 2 Mandible. End-game boss: craft a Mysterious Worm and throw it into the End's void to summon it. The summon ritual is covered in sections 2 and 3 of this guide.

## Mowzie's Mobs 1.8.2
Mowzie's bosses are in the Boss Compendium.
- **Foliaath**: jungle, disguised as a fern or sprout on grass, hostile ambush (grabs and eats), 10 HP (baby 1 HP), 8 damage. Drops 0 to 2 Foliaath Seed; plant a seed to grow your own. Bites hard for its size, 8 damage is a lot for something this small.
- **Naga**: Stony Shores/coastal cliffs, spawns in packs, hostile, 30 HP, 4 damage. Leash only, not tameable. Drops 0 to 1 Naga Fang (0 to 4 with Looting III). Ranged acid-ball attack that poisons, about 5 to 6 hearts per hit. The Fang crafts the Naga Fang Dagger plus a Potion of Poison Resistance.
- **Grottol**: deep underground caves/ravines, passive, 20 HP. Drops a guaranteed Diamond. A rare "crystal crustacean," and the guaranteed diamond makes it a good early target.
- **Lantern**: dark/roofed forest canopy, passive light source, 4 HP. Drops 0 to 2 Glowing Jelly. Floats in treetops, glows like a jellyfish.
- **Bluff**: mountain peaks, same biome as Tongbi, neutral/skittish, 45 HP, 18 damage. Drops 0 to 1 Bluff Rod. Highest raw damage stat in this table at 18, but it's skittish and non-aggressive unless cornered. The Rod is the entry fee for the Sculptor trial.
- **Elokosa**: savanna, around Umvuthana villages, neutral guardian spirit, 17 HP (Howler variant 20 HP), 5 to 6 damage. Drops an Elokosa Paw with moon-phase variant items. A wolf/hyena-like Umvuthana spirit guardian; Paw items track the real moon phase.
- **Umvuthana (Bliss/Faith/Fear/Fury/Misery/Rage)**: savanna, Umvuthana Grove villages (Umvuthi's worshippers), neutral villagers that turn hostile if provoked, 8 HP (Raptor variant 10, Follower-to-Player 20), 3 to 7 damage. Drops 1 Umvuthana Mask per variant, 0 to 2 Feather. 6 emotion-mask variants; the masks are cosmetic trophies.

## Naturalist 5.0pre2
All savanna/forest real-world animals; adults are tameable and rideable with a 25-slot chest saddle. No unique pack config for this mod, defaults are used.
- **Lion**: savanna, hostile predator, 24 HP, 6 damage. Drops 0 to 1 Leather, 1 to 2 Fur. Hunts herbivores, dangerous in prides.
- **Giraffe**: savanna, passive, 35 HP. Tame and ride with a chest saddle. Drops 1 to 2 Bushmeat, 0 to 1 Hay Block. Tall, good scouting mount.
- **Elephant** (Naturalist's own, separate from the Alex's Mobs elephant): savanna, passive, 80 HP, 10 damage. Tameable and rideable. Drops 3 to 5 Leather, 1 to 3 Bushmeat. Two unrelated "Elephant" mobs exist in this pack.
- **Hippo**: savanna/rivers, neutral but aggressive in water, 40 HP, 6 damage. Drops 1 to 5 Melon Slice. Territorial.
- **Rhino** (Naturalist's own, separate from Alex's Mobs Rhinoceros): savanna, neutral but charges, 40 HP, 6 damage. Drops 1 to 3 Leather, 1 to 3 Bushmeat.
- **Vulture**: savanna, passive scavenger, 10 HP, 4 damage. Drops 0 to 2 Feather, 0 to 2 Rotten Flesh. Circles corpses.
- **Boar**: forest, neutral, 14 HP, 1 damage. Drops 1 to 3 Porkchop, 0 to 2 Leather. Weak melee.
- **Bear** (Naturalist's own, separate from the Alex's Mobs Grizzly Bear): forest/taiga, neutral but aggressive, 30 HP, 6 damage. Drops 1 to 2 Fur.
- **Deer**: forest, passive, flees, 8 HP. Drops 0 to 2 Leather, 1 to 2 Venison. Good early food.
- **Moose** (Naturalist's own, separate from Alex's Mobs Moose): taiga, neutral but charges, 40 HP, 6 damage.
- **Alligator**: swamp, hostile ambush predator, 30 HP, 6 damage.
- **Snake** (Naturalist's own, separate from Alex's Mobs Rattlesnake): overworld, hostile, venomous, 10 HP, 6 damage.
- **Lizard**: overworld, passive, tail detaches, 8 HP, 2 damage. Drops a Lizard Tail, which regrows.
- **Tortoise** (Naturalist's own): overworld, passive, 20 HP, 2 damage. Drops 0 to 1 Turtle Scute. Slow, fairly tanky for a passive mob.
- **Catfish** (Naturalist's own, separate from Alex's Mobs Catfish): river, passive, 6 HP, 1 damage, bucketable. Drops a Catfish item.
- **Bluejay/Cardinal/Canary/Robin**: forest, passive, 6 to 10 HP. Drops 1 to 2 Feather. Decorative songbirds.
- **Butterfly/Caterpillar/Dragonfly/Firefly**: overworld, passive, 5 to 10 HP. Drops 0 to 1 Bone Meal, or Froglass from the Dragonfly, or Glow Goop from the Firefly. Ambient insects; the Dragonfly drops colored Froglass blocks.
- **Snail** (Naturalist's own, separate from the Spawn mod's snail): overworld, passive, 2 HP. Drops a Snail Shell, 0 to 1 Slime Ball.
- **Duck**: overworld water, passive, 4 HP, bucketable. Drops 0 to 2 Feather plus a Duck item. Food source.

## Spawn 4.0
- **Stranded** (rare hostile): beach near shipwrecks, hostile, 80 HP, 15 damage. Drops 0 to 3 Dead Sea Star, 2 to 5 Barnacle, 3 to 5 Iron Ingot. The toughest mob in this mod by far, basically a mini-threat.
- **Firekeeper**: Nether/overworld, rare, neutral, 60 HP, 8 damage, 5 armor. Drops a guaranteed Sunstone, a valuable light-source material.
- **Sea Cow**: ocean, passive, 30 HP, bucketable and milkable. Drops 0 to 2 Leather, 1 to 3 Beef. Manatee-like.
- **Flukeshroom**: overworld, rare, neutral, stats about equal to the Sea Cow's. Drops 0 to 2 Leather, 1 to 3 Beef. A mushroom-whale hybrid oddity.
- **Tuna/Herring/Bluefish/Angler Fish/Blenny/Pilot Fish**: ocean, passive, 2 to 15 HP, 0 to 3 damage, bucketable. Each drops its own fish item, some with a 5% Bone Meal chance. Food-chain fish; the Angler Fish is the anglerfish variant.
- **Seahorse**: ocean/reef, passive, 4 HP, bucketable. Decorative.
- **Octopus** (Spawn's own): ocean, passive, 20 HP.
- **Barracuda**: ocean, hostile, 16 HP, 8 damage. Aggressive fast swimmer.
- **Spider Crab / Coastal Crab / Clam**: beach/ocean floor, passive/neutral, 8 to 16 HP. Spider Crab drops 0 to 2 Spider Eye. Coastal Crab has 8 armor.
- **Booby (+ egg)**: beach cliffs, passive, 10 HP, 2 damage. Drops 0 to 2 Feather, or a Booby Egg Shard. Seabird.
- **Dodo**: overworld, rare, jungle, passive, 10 HP, 0 damage. Drops 0 to 2 Feather plus a Dodo item. An extinct-bird novelty mob with 0 attack damage.
- **Ant (colony)**: overworld, passive, 10 HP, 1 damage. Part of the ant farm decorative mechanic.
- **Hamster**: overworld, passive, 6 HP, 1 damage. Tameable as a pet. Works with the hamster wheel decoration.
- **Stickbug**: forest, passive, camouflages, 4 HP. Drops a Stick, and rarely the Music Disc "Rain."
- **Snail** (Spawn's own): overworld, passive, 12 HP. Drops a Snail Shell, 0 to 1 Slime Ball.
- **Seal** (Spawn's own, separate from the Alex's Mobs Seal): beach, passive, 16 HP, 3 damage.
- **Sunfish**: ocean, passive, 6 HP, bucketable.
- **Iguana**: overworld desert/jungle, passive, 16 HP, 4 damage.

## Ecologics 2.2.6
- **Coconut Crab**: tropical beach/coconut-tree biomes, neutral, pinches, 20 HP, 2 damage. Drops 0 to 2 Crab Claw. Crab meat is a food source.
- **Camel** (Ecologics' own): desert/savanna, passive. HP wasn't found in the code, likely around 32 like the vanilla camel. Tame and ride, 2-player saddle. Drops 0 to 2 Leather. Predates the vanilla 1.20 Camel and coexists alongside it in this pack.
- **Penguin**: snowy/ice biomes, passive, 15 HP, 2 damage. Slides on ice, cosmetic companion.
- **Squirrel**: forest (oak/walnut trees), passive, 10 HP. Climbs trees, buries items.

## Rotten Creatures 1.1.0
Dead Beard and Immortal, the rare variants, are in the Boss Compendium.
- **Burned**: Nether, spawn weight 20, hostile, 22 HP, 4 damage, 4 armor. Drops 0 to 2 Magma Rotten Flesh, plus 0 to 2 Magma Block or Magma Cream. Fire-themed zombie.
- **Frostbitten**: snowy biomes, spawn weight 80 (common), hostile, 20 HP, 3 damage, 2 armor. Drops 0 to 2 Frozen Rotten Flesh, plus Iron Ingot, Carrot, Potato or Snowball.
- **Glacial Hunter**: snowy biomes, spawn weight 20, hostile, 20 HP, 3 damage, 4 armor. Drops 0 to 2 Rotten Flesh, plus Cod and Leather.
- **Swampy**: swamp, spawn weight 80 (common), hostile, 16 HP, 3 damage. Drops 0 to 2 Rotten Flesh, plus Brown Dye and Poisonous Potato.
- **Undead Miner** (4 variants: stone/iron/gold/diamond): underground at depth 63 or below, spawn weight 20, hostile, 24 HP, 4 damage, 2 armor. Drops Rotten Flesh, Cobblestone and tier-appropriate ore (raw iron, gold or diamond, plus coal). The diamond variant is the best random loot pull.
- **Mummy**: desert, spawn weight 20, hostile, 26 HP, 3 damage, 2 armor. Drops 0 to 2 Rotten Flesh, plus Paper, Gold Nugget and Raw Gold.
- **Scarab**: desert, hostile, 10 HP, 2 damage. No unique drops found in its loot table.
- **Skeleton Lackey**: overworld, hostile, ranged, 18 HP, 2 damage, 0 armor. Drops Bone. A weak skeleton reskin.
- **Zombie Lackey**: overworld, hostile, 20 HP, 3 damage, 0 armor. Drops Rotten Flesh and Copper Ingot.
- **Zap**: overworld, possibly during storms, hostile, 22 HP, 3 damage, 2 armor. Drops 0 to 1 Rotten Flesh, plus Iron Ingot, Carrot or Potato.
- **Hunter Wolf**: overworld, hostile. HP wasn't found in the code, and there's no loot table either. A rare aggressive wolf variant.

## The Graveyard 3.1
Disabled in this pack: Acolyte, Wraith, Corrupted Pillager and Corrupted Vindicator. They can't spawn naturally.
- **Ghoul**: graveyard structures/biomes, spawn weight 25, groups of 2 to 5, hostile, about 50 HP (Ghouling class), 6.5 damage, 5 armor. Drops 1 to 2 Rotten Flesh, 0 to 1 Corruption, Bone. The most common Graveyard hostile.
- **Revenant**: graveyard structures, spawn weight 25, groups of 5 to 8 (the largest groups), hostile, 25 HP, 3.5 damage, 2 armor. Drops 0 to 2 Bone, 0 to 1 Corruption.
- **Skeleton Creeper**: graveyard structures, spawn weight 25, groups of 1 to 4, hostile, a ranged and explosive hybrid. HP isn't overridden, it uses vanilla Skeleton values, and damage is vanilla skeleton scale. Drops 0 to 2 Bone, 0 to 1 Corruption, and can drop music discs like a creeper kill.
- **Reaper**: graveyard structures, spawn weight 5 (rare), groups of 2 to 3, hostile, charges at you, 20 HP, 4 damage. Drops Soul Sand, 0 to 1 Corruption, Bone. No armor stat found.
- **Nightmare**: graveyard structures, spawn weight 10, spawns solo (group size 1), hostile, immune to sunlight burn, 45 HP, 10 damage, 3 armor. Drops 1 to 3 Corruption. The strongest regular Graveyard mob at 10 damage, and it casts the Falling Corpse spell also used by the Lich boss.
- **Acolyte** (disabled in this pack, can't spawn): 45 HP on paper (unused NamelessHangedEntity), would drop 1 Corruption if its loot table were active. The mod includes it, but the mod's config has it turned off.
- **Wraith** (disabled in this pack, can't spawn): 27.5 HP, 6.5 damage on paper, no loot table so it would drop nothing anyway.
- **Corrupted Pillager** (disabled in this pack, can't spawn): 24 HP, 3 damage on paper, would drop 1 Corruption if active.
- **Corrupted Vindicator** (disabled in this pack, can't spawn): 24 HP, 5 damage on paper, would drop 1 Corruption if active.

## Northstar 0.5.4
Planet mobs drop nothing (empty loot tables), only XP.
- **Moon Lunargrade**: Moon, all lunar biomes, spawn weight 40, groups of 1 to 4, neutral, spits acid. HP wasn't found in the code. Drops nothing. The most common Moon mob.
- **Moon Snail**: Moon, all lunar biomes, spawn weight 20, groups of 1 to 3, passive. Drops nothing.
- **Moon Eel**: Moon, all lunar biomes, spawn weight 20, groups of 2 to 5, neutral. Drops nothing.
- **Frozen Zombie**: Moon, sub-biome unconfirmed, hostile. Drops nothing. Likely the Moon's hostile night/low-light threat, but its spawn table isn't confirmed.
- **Mars Cobra**: Mars, spawn weight 20, groups of 1 to 3, hostile, venomous, 20 HP, 5 damage. Drops nothing.
- **Mars Devil Moth**: Mars, spawn weight 20, groups of 1 to 3, neutral, flies, 8 HP. Drops nothing.
- **Mars Root Toad**: Mars, spawn weight 20, groups of 3 to 6 (the largest group size on Mars), passive, 20 HP, 5 damage. Drops nothing.
- **Mars Echo Worm**: Mars, spawn weight 40 (most common), groups of 1 to 4, hostile, 20 HP, 5 damage. Drops nothing.
- **Mercurian Raptor**: Mercury, spawn weight 30, groups of 1 to 3, hostile, 20 HP, 5 damage. Drops nothing.
- **Mercury Roach**: Mercury, spawn weight 30, groups of 2 to 5, passive/neutral, 20 HP, 5 damage. Drops nothing.
- **Mercury Tortoise**: Mercury, spawn weight 30, groups of 1 to 3, passive, 20 HP, 5 damage, 20 armor. Drops nothing.
- **Venus Scorpion**: Venus, spawn weight 30 to 50 depending on biome, groups of 1 to 4, hostile, venomous, 30 HP, 5 damage. Drops nothing. The toughest Venus mob by HP.
- **Venus Vulture**: Venus, spawn weight 10 to 30, groups of 1 to 3, neutral scavenger, 8 HP. Drops nothing.
- **Venus Stone Bull**: Venus, spawn weight 20 to 30, groups of 2 to 6 (the largest Venus groups), hostile, charges, 60 HP, 10 damage. Drops nothing. The highest HP and damage Northstar mob, the planet's real threat.
- **Venus Mimic**: Venus, spawn weight 40 (most common), groups of 1 to 3, hostile, disguises itself, 20 HP, 5 damage. Drops nothing. Mimics Venus terrain and blocks.

## BetterEnd 20.0.8
Ambient mobs with no drops.
- **Cubozoa**: End biomes, water/pools, passive, 2 HP. Drops nothing. Jellyfish-like ambient swimmer.
- **Ender Dragonfly**: End biomes, passive, 8 HP. Drops nothing.
- **End Fish**: End biomes, water, passive, 2 HP, bucketable. Drops nothing.
- **End Slime**: End biomes, neutral, hops, 1 HP, 1 damage. Drops nothing. The weakest combat stat in this whole document, 1 HP and 1 damage.
- **Silk Moth**: End biomes, passive, 2 HP. Drops nothing.

## BetterNether 20.0.10
- **Naga** (BetterNether's own, unrelated to Mowzie's Naga): Nether, hostile, 10 HP, 3 damage, 2 armor. Drops 0 to 1 Coal, 0 to 2 Bone, and rarely a Wither Skeleton Skull.
- **Jungle Skeleton**: Nether jungle biome, hostile, ranged. Uses vanilla Skeleton HP and damage. Drops 0 to 2 Arrow, 0 to 2 Bone. A Nether-reskinned Skeleton.
- **Skull**: Nether, hostile, flies, 4 HP, 1 damage. Drops 0 to 1 Coal, 0 to 2 Bone, and rarely a Wither Skeleton Skull. Very low HP, dies fast but can net you a rare skull.
- **Hydrogen Jellyfish**: Nether gas caves, hostile, explosive, 0.5 HP, 20 damage. Drops nothing. Extremely dangerous per hit (20 damage) despite its tiny 0.5 HP pool; kill it from range, don't melee it.
- **Flying Pig**: Nether, passive, flies, 6 HP, 3 damage. Drops 0 to 2 Porkchop. Food source, floats around.
- **Firefly** (BetterNether's own): Nether, passive, 1 HP. Drops 0 to 2 Glowstone Dust. Light-source ambient mob.

## Bygone Nether 1.3.2
- **Corpor**: Catacombs structure, hostile, a brutish wither skeleton, slow but hits hard, 60 HP, 4 damage. Drops Coal, 0 to 2 Bone, a Wither Skeleton Skull. High-HP tank type.
- **Wraither**: Catacombs structure, hostile, fast, "possessed," releases a Wex when nearly dead, 25 HP. Drops Coal, 0 to 2 Bone, a Wither Skeleton Skull.
- **Wex**: spawned only from a dying Wraither, hostile, fast harasser, 14 HP, 4 damage. Drops nothing. A small, fast add-mob, not a natural spawn.
- **Wither Skeleton Knight**: Nether structures, hostile, armored, 60 HP, 2 armor. Drops Coal, 0 to 2 Bone, a Wither Skeleton Skull.
- **Wither Skeleton Horse**: Nether structures, the Knight's mount, neutral, 35 HP. Rideable if you tame or capture it. Drops nothing.
- **Warped Enderman**: Citadel structure, hostile, attacks on sight with no eye-contact rule unlike vanilla Endermen, 55 HP, 8.5 damage. Drops 0 to 1 Warped Ender Pearl. Can be sheared back into a normal Enderman; one of the higher-damage regular mobs in the pack.
- **Piglin Prisoner / Piglin Hunter**: Nether structures, neutral, 24 HP, 6 damage. Befriend by giving gold ingots, which also gives you potion buffs. No drops found in its loot table. A rescue-and-recruit mechanic, not a standard tame.

## Aquamirae 6.4.0
Captain Cornelia and Mother of the Maze are in the Boss Compendium.
- **Eel**: deep ocean and Ice Maze depths, hostile, evasive, area attacks, 180 HP, 8 damage, 20 armor. Drops 8 to 16 Fin, 12 to 24 Sharp Bones, 8 to 16 Spinefish, 2 to 6 Dead Sea Scroll, and a Treasure Pouch (4 to 6 rolls). One of the tankiest non-boss mobs in the whole pack.
- **Anglerfish** (Aquamirae's own): deep/frozen ocean, hostile, can trigger the Mother of the Maze encounter, 40 HP, 5 damage, 2 armor. Drops Fin (weight 70), Angler's Fang (weight 25), Ship Graveyard Echo (weight 5), and Esca.
- **Maw**: Ice Maze structure, hostile, 20 HP, 4 damage. Drops Fin (weight 100), Sharp Bones (weight 5), Slime Ball, Ship Graveyard Echo. Also has a bonus loot pool: pirate pouch, saber, food and more.
- **Tortured Soul**: overworld surface, haunted areas, hostile, turns invisible, inflicts Blindness, 30 HP, 7 damage, 4 armor. Drops 0 to 3 Rotten Flesh, Bread, 0 to 2 Bone, Ship Graveyard Echo.
- **Golden Moth**: overworld, spawns at night, peaceful, 3 HP. Drops a Gold Ingot, 1 to 3 Gold Nugget. A safe, free source of gold.
- **Abyssal Scyphoid**: Ice Maze structure, hostile, poisons and blinds on contact, 16 HP. Drops Ship Graveyard Echo (weight 5), Jellyfish Jelly.
- **Spinefish**: deep ocean, passive, bucketable. No separate HP stat, it's an item-drop fish. Drops a Spinefish item.

## Alex's Caves: Stuff & Torpedoes 1.0.4
Small Alex's Caves addon. Two entities are player-summoned constructs, not wild spawns.
- **Polarizz**: player-summoned (place a Carved Pumpkin on an Energized Galena block, golem-style), friendly companion, 10 HP, 3 damage. You summon it rather than tame it. Drops an Attractive Music Disc. Light body, can be knocked around like a ball; heal it with a Neodymium Ingot.
- **Shadow**: spawned via the addon's Shadow Casting/Brazier of Desolation mechanic, hostile, 80 HP, 10 damage. Drops Pure Darkness. The toughest entity in this addon by far at 80 HP and 10 damage, a late-game threat.
- **Bulb / Magnetic Burst / Torpedo Missile**: deployed by the player's Torpedo item, not wild spawns, utility/projectile constructs, 10 HP, 3 damage. Drops nothing. Ordnance tied to the torpedo weapon system, not real mobs to fight.

## Forgotten Ruins 0.0.13
A structure mod: ruined jungle, swamp and mangrove dungeons guarded by golems. The golem details come from the wiki and couldn't be checked against the pack files.
- **Guardian Golem(s)**: inside Forgotten Ruins structures, jungle/swamp/mangrove biomes, hostile "ancient defense systems," some described as watchful, others unstable. HP and damage are unknown; drops are unknown too, though structures often reward rare loot tied to the separate Armor of the Ages mod. We couldn't find data for this one, so check in-game before trusting any number here.

## Create Nuclear 1.3.1
Irradiated Cats, Chickens and Wolves keep vanilla stats and drop nothing. They show up around radiation from reactor meltdowns.
- **Irradiated Cat**: Irradiated Land, the post-meltdown biome around a reactor, hostile mutant, about 10 HP (vanilla Cat base), low damage. Drops nothing. Also fall-damage immune.
- **Irradiated Chicken**: Irradiated Land, hostile mutant, about 4 HP (vanilla Chicken base), low damage. Drops nothing. Also fall-damage immune.
- **Irradiated Wolf**: Irradiated Land, hostile mutant, about 8 HP (vanilla Wolf base), moderate damage. Drops nothing. Not fall-damage immune, only the cat and chicken are.

A meltdown or Irradiated Land marks "you built your reactor wrong" territory, not a destination. Wear the mod's Hazmat/Anti-Radiation gear if you're cleaning one up.

## TF Dungeons & Villages 1.2.3
Not a combat bestiary mod, it adds trader villager professions to Twilight Forest villages plus one unique roaming NPC.
- **Twilight Trader**: Twilight Forest, roaming or village-linked, passive NPC, 10 HP, 6 damage (a defensive stat, not used offensively). Drops standard villager-trade goods. A unique wandering trader themed for Twilight Forest.
- **Mycologist/Alchemist/Lumberjack/Hunter/Farmer** (professions): Twilight Forest villages, passive NPCs, 20 HP (vanilla Villager stat). Drop profession-specific trades. Reskinned vanilla villager professions selling Twilight Forest goods.

## Magic mods
- **Wilden Hunter** (Ars Nouveau): 20 HP, 4.5 damage, 2 armor. Wilden Dens in forests. Fast, and it can call wolves. Drops the Wilden Horn.
- **Wilden Stalker** (Ars Nouveau): 15 HP, 2.5 damage. Wilden Dens in forests. Flies and dives. Drops the Wilden Wing.
- **Wilden Guardian** (Ars Nouveau): 25 HP, 4.5 damage, 2 armor. Wilden Dens in cold conifer forests. Armors up when hit, reflects melee damage, then fires spikes everywhere. Drops the Wilden Spike.
- **Weald Walker** (Ars Nouveau): 60 HP, 10.5 damage. Made from an Archwood tree with the Ritual of Awakening, and it guards an area with its tree's spells.
- **Starbuncle, Whirlisprig, Drygmy, Bookwyrm** (Ars Nouveau): small passive creatures, most common in Archwood Forests. Tamed as helpers and familiars.
- **Mysterious Mages and Mage Knights** (Adam's Ars Plus): 40 to 100 HP. They roam every dimension and guard the Archmage dungeons, and they can break blocks. Drop Mage Cloth.
- **Acolyte, Necromancer, Bone Golem and Lich** (Ars Zero): undead that guard the Necropolis under the Blight Forest. The Lich drops a Wither Skeleton Skull and Necromancy Essence.
- **Pyromancer, Cryomancer, Archevoker, Priest, Cultist, Apothecarist** (Iron's Spells): 60 HP spellcasters using their school's spells. Each drops its school's rune plus scrolls and ink.
- **Necromancer** (Iron's Spells): 25 HP, a Blood caster that raises undead.
- **Keeper, the Ancient Knight** (Iron's Spells): 60 HP, 10 damage. Rises from armor piles at the Nether's Ancient Battlegrounds. Drops Cinder Essence and sometimes netherite scrap.
- **Ice Spider** (Iron's Spells): 50 HP, 8 damage, 20 armor. Ice Spider Dens in snowy biomes.
- **Cursed Armor Stand** (Iron's Spells): 60 HP. An armor stand that comes alive and ambushes you.
- **Jiangshi** (Magic From The East): 40 HP, 3 damage, 4 armor. A hopping corpse. 2% chance of a Jiangshi Hat.
- **Onmyoji and Taoist** (Magic From The East): 60 HP casters at the Onmyoji Shrine and Taoist House. They drop Spirit or Symmetry runes.
- **Aeromancer** (Wind's Spellbooks): 60 HP. Drops a Book of Tempests 33% of the time.
- **Geomancer and Geo Grandmaster** (Geomancy Plus): 60 HP and 11 armor, or 180 HP and 15 armor. The Geomancer always drops a Geo Rune; the Grandmaster trades curios.
- **Rogue elemental mages** (Ars Elemental): 50 HP. They attack you unless you wear their school's focus.
- **Mermaid and Siren** (Ars Elemental): 10 HP, tameable with a sea pickle.
- **Foodgeist** (Farmer's Spell): 20 HP and friendly. Feed it for Foodgeist Cheese, Ham and Seasoning.

The bosses from these mods (the Dead King, Echo of Tyros, the Wilden Chimera, the Archmages and the Dark Doppelganger) are in the Bosses section.

## Top 15 most dangerous regular (non-boss) mobs in this document
Ranked by a rough HP times damage threat estimate, cross-checked against notable special mechanics.
- **Sea Bear** (Alex's Mobs): 200 HP, 8 damage, amphibious ambush hunter. The biggest non-boss HP pool in the pack.
- **Eel** (Aquamirae): 180 HP, 8 damage, 20 armor. The toughest armor value of any non-boss mob here.
- **Cachalot Whale** (Alex's Mobs): 160 HP, 30 damage if provoked. The highest raw damage stat among ocean mobs, avoid melee.
- **Shadow** (Alex's Caves: Torpedoes): 80 HP, 10 damage. A late-game addon hazard.
- **Warped Mosco** (Alex's Mobs): 100 HP, 10 damage, 10 armor. Nether mini-boss.
- **Venus Stone Bull** (Northstar): 60 HP, 10 damage. The hardest-hitting planet mob.
- **Laviathan** (Alex's Mobs): 60 HP, 1 base damage but 10 armor, a lava predator. The lava itself is the real danger here.
- **Corpor / Wither Skeleton Knight** (Bygone Nether): 60 HP each, Nether structure guardians.
- **Tiger** (Alex's Mobs): 50 HP, 12 damage. The highest raw melee damage of any confirmed non-boss stat in this document.
- **Bluff** (Mowzie's Mobs): 45 HP, 18 damage. The second-highest raw damage stat overall; usually non-aggressive but devastating if it does hit.
- **Nightmare** (The Graveyard): 45 HP, 10 damage. The strongest currently enabled Graveyard mob.
- **Warped Enderman** (Bygone Nether): 55 HP, 8.5 damage. Attacks without the vanilla eye-contact rule.
- **Rhinoceros / Moose / Grizzly Bear** (Alex's Mobs): about 55 to 60 HP, 7.5 to 8 damage. Dangerous if you provoke a charge.
- **Hydrogen Jellyfish** (BetterNether): only 0.5 HP but 20 damage per hit. The highest damage-per-encounter ratio in this document; never melee it.
- **Stranded** (Spawn): 80 HP, 15 damage. This mod's one real combat threat.

## Most useful mob drops
- **Void Worm Eye / Mandible** (Alex's Mobs): crafts the end-game Dimensional Carver, a portable-home teleport pick.
- **Unsettling Kimono** (Murmur, 10% drop): makes undead mobs neutral, excellent late-game quality of life.
- **Ghostly Pickaxe** (Underminer, break its target block): reveals hidden ore.
- **Whale Echo + Fish Bones + Slimeball** (Cachalot Whale + Skelewag): craft the Echolocator and Strange Fish Finder.
- **Tarantula Hawk Wing Fragment**: upgrades a vanilla Elytra into the Tarantula Hawk Elytra.
- **Rocky Shell** (Rocky Roller): crafts the Rock Shell Chestplate for roll-dash mobility.
- **Dropbear Claw**: crafts the Tendon Whip grapple tool.
- **Wither Skeleton Skull** (BetterNether Skull/Naga, Bygone Nether undead): several easy sources beyond vanilla Wither Skeletons.
- **Diamond** (Mowzie's Mobs Grottol, guaranteed): a reliable early diamond source if you can find one.
- **Sunstone** (Firekeeper, Spawn mod): a strong utility light item outside the boss list.

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

# Create and power

Create is the backbone of the tech side in this pack. Rockets, aircraft, ships, nuclear power and most addons all need Create parts. Everything runs on rotation: speed in RPM, capacity in SU (stress units). A few addons convert rotation into FE (electricity) for RFTools, Pipez energy and batteries.

> Create's per-world server config (stress values, train speeds) isn't shipped with the pack, so the numbers here are mod defaults. Your server admin could have changed them.

## The Create material ladder

### Tier 1: Andesite Alloy
Andesite plus an iron or zinc nugget (2x2 crafting, or a Mixer). This unlocks shafts, cogs, water wheels, presses, mixers, fans, crushing wheels (ore doubling), deployers and mechanical crafters. Zinc ore spawns from Y -63 to 70.

### Tier 2: Brass
Copper and zinc in a heated Mixer (a Blaze Burner underneath does it: craft an Empty Blaze Burner from 4 iron sheets and netherrack, then right-click a Blaze or a blaze spawner with it). Unlocks brass casings, mechanical arms, steam engines, and most addon machines (Alternator, pumpjack, Power Grid devices).

### Tier 3: Precision Mechanism
Sequenced assembly on a golden sheet: 5 loops of cogwheel to large cogwheel to iron nugget. 80% success, 20% scrap. This gates reactor cores, factory gauges, electro weapons, rockets and aircraft.

### Tier 4: Trains and logistics
Sturdy Sheet (powdered obsidian, fill with 500 mB lava, press x2) becomes Railway Casing, which gets you track, stations and train controls. Steam 'n' Rails adds Conductors (robot drivers made from a Conductor's Cap on andesite casing), more gauges and couplers. Create 6's package logistics (Packager, Frogport, Stock Ticker, Factory Gauge) automates whole factories.

### Tier 5: Electricity and big power
Bridge rotation into FE with the Crafts & Additions Alternator or New Age Generator Coils, then scale up with diesel engines, Create Nuclear, or Bigger Reactors.

## Every power source compared
- **Water Wheel** (Create): 8 RPM, 256 SU. Needs flowing water, free forever. Early stage.
- **Large Water Wheel** (Create): 4 RPM, 512 SU. Needs flowing water. Early stage.
- **Windmill** (Create): +RPM per 8 sails, 512 SU per RPM. Needs a Windmill Bearing plus sails, more sails is faster. Early to mid stage.
- **Steam Engine** (Create): up to 16,384 SU per engine. Needs a fluid-tank boiler, water and heat (Blaze Burner or New Age heaters). Boiler level (max 18) sets how many engines it runs at full. Mid stage.
- **Diesel Engine** (Diesel Generators): 6,144 SU at 96 RPM on diesel. Needs liquid fuel, 0.05 mB per tick. Mid stage.
- **Modular Diesel Engine** (Diesel Generators): 8,192 SU at 96 RPM. Needs chained engines on one fuel line. Mid to late stage.
- **Huge Diesel Engine** (Diesel Generators): 16,384 SU at 224 RPM on diesel. Biggest single-block engine. Late stage.
- **New Age Motors** (New Age): 512, 2,048 or 8,192 SU (Basic, Advanced, Reinforced). Needs FE in. Mid to late stage.
- **Alternator**, SU to FE (Crafts & Additions): 480 FE/t at 256 RPM. Takes up to 16,384 SU in, 75% efficiency, 5,000 FE buffer. Mid stage.
- **Generator Coil**, SU to FE (New Age): about the same rate as the Alternator. Needs magnets and carbon brushes, tuned to match Crafts & Additions. Mid stage.
- **Power Grid generator** (Power Grid): output scales with your rotor/coil build. Needs a realistic volts/amps circuit, and a Device Connector bridges it to FE. Mid to late stage.
- **Create Nuclear Reactor** (Create Nuclear): 2,000,000+ SU at scale, from rotation. Needs uranium rods, graphite rods and coolers (blue ice). A meltdown is a size-10 explosion after 30 seconds. Late stage.
- **Bigger Reactors** (Bigger Reactors): up to about 2,000,000 RF/t. Needs uranium ingots, can go up to 128x128x192, add a Turbine for efficiency. Late stage.

Fuel ranking for Diesel Generators, straight from the mod's files: Diesel beats Gasoline and Biodiesel (tied), which beat Ethanol, which beats Plant Oil. Get crude oil from a Pumpjack over an oil deposit (find one with the Oil Scanner), then run it through a Distillation Tower to split it into diesel and gasoline. Ethanol comes from the Bulk Fermenter.

## The cheap end: cranks, burners and heat
The table above starts at water wheels, but there's a whole tier below and beside it that costs almost nothing to set up.

- **Hand Crank** (Create): right-click it and it turns. Fine for opening a door or running one machine for a few seconds, useless for anything continuous.
- **Blaze Burner** (Create): the standard heat source. Feed it a blaze, then keep it fed with fuel. It heats basins for mixing and boilers for steam.
- **Solid Burner** and **Liquid Burner** (Create: Propulsion): burn solid fuel like coal, or liquid fuel, to make heat for whatever sits directly above them. The liquid one puts out twice the heat of the solid one, and both have a thermostat so they don't cook the block above.
- **Stirling Engine** (Create: Propulsion and Create: New Age): sits on a heat source and turns. More heat means faster rotation. This is the "burn coal, get rotation" answer, and it's the simplest engine in the pack.
- **Burner** (Diesel Generators): burns a flammable liquid to heat a basin. A valve sets the burn rate, and a redstone signal makes it burn faster.
- **Heating Plates** (New Age): make heat from daylight, so a roof of them runs a Stirling Engine for free in the day. There's also a **Boiler Heater**, which behaves like a Blaze Burner but eats heat instead of fuel, plus heat pipes and pumps to move heat where you want it. Push too much heat into a block and it turns into lava, so watch the pipes.
- **Coal Burner**, **Combustion Engine** and **Gas Engine** (Clockwork): small engines meant for ships built with Valkyrien Skies rather than for base power.

## Rotation, electricity, and getting between them
Two currencies run side by side. Create machines want **rotation**, measured in RPM and SU. RFTools, Pipez energy pipes, the Builder, teleporters and anything electric want **FE**.

- **Rotation to FE**: the **Alternator** (Crafts & Additions) needs at least 32 RPM to do anything. **Generator Coils** (New Age) spin inside a ring of magnets and hand their output to **Carbon Brushes**; stronger magnets convert a bigger share of the SU but eat more of it, and one set of brushes reads up to 8 coils. Coils only make electricity, never rotation.
- **FE to rotation**: the **Electric Motor** (Crafts & Additions), where scrolling the back panel sets the RPM, or New Age's **Motors**, where a wrench sets speed and redstone switches them off.
- The two mods are tuned to match each other in this pack, so it doesn't matter much which side you build.
- **Wires and connectors** (Crafts & Additions, New Age, Power Grid) carry FE between blocks, and Pipez energy pipes do the same job with less fuss.

If you only ever build Create machines you can ignore FE completely. The moment you want an RFTools quarry, a teleporter or a dimension, you need one of the converters above, or a Bigger Reactor, which makes FE directly.

## Create Nuclear
Fuel chain: raw uranium gets crushed into uranium powder, mixed into a liquid, compacted into yellowcake, fan-enriched into enriched yellowcake, then mechanical-crafted into a Uranium Rod. Graphite rods are graphene (pressed coal dust) plus steel (coal dust and iron).

The reactor needs a Reactor Casing (steel on brass casing), a Frame, a Core (16 casings plus 4 precision mechanisms), a Cooler (blue ice), Input/Output, and a Controller (16 casings, 4 netherite ingots, a nether star, a content observer, electron tubes, an item vault). Rod layout comes from a Reactor Blueprint.

Heat caps at 1,000. Each rod adds 30 per tick, and rods last 3 minutes, so over-build your coolers.

Uranium and unshielded reactors inflict Radiation. A full Anti-Radiation suit (dyeable, 16 colours) blocks it. Irradiated cats, chickens and wolves spawn near radiation.

## Tech weapons and gadgets
- **Potato Cannon** (Create): fires food as ammo, dual-wield for about 50% faster fire. With a filled Backtank it uses air instead of durability.
- **Extendo Grip** (Create): +4 block reach and extra knockback. Runs on Backtank air.
- **Netherite Backtank + Diving gear** (Create): underwater breathing, fireproof, and it also counts as space armor for Northstar.
- **Chemical Sprayer / Turret** (Diesel Generators): sprays any fluid at mobs. Add a Lighter plus diesel or gasoline and it's a flamethrower. The turret is redstone-triggered and you can filter its targets.
- **Electro-Baton / Electro-Zapper** (Power Grid): electric melee and ranged weapons. Both need a worn, charged Portable Battery.
