- **Tesla Coil** (Crafts & Additions): base defense block, 3 hearts to mobs (2 to players) in a 3-block radius, 1,000 FE per zap, 1 second cooldown. Also charges items.
- **Barbed Wire** (Crafts & Additions): 2 damage on contact.

## Automating enchanting (Create: Enchantment Industry)
Create: Enchantment Industry turns experience into a fluid you can pump, so enchanting becomes a production line. The pieces, roughly in the order you meet them:
- **Liquid Experience**: experience as a fluid. A **Grindstone Drain** under a **Mechanical Grindstone** catches the experience from disenchanting, and an **Experience Hatch** collects what mobs and furnaces give off.
- **Blaze Enchanter**: a blaze burner that enchants items fed to it, drinking liquid experience. Set the level, and feed it either a direct enchantment or a template.
- **Printer**: copies written books, enchanted books and maps, using ink and experience.
- **Blaze Forger**: an automatic anvil. It repairs and combines gear, and a Spout with liquid experience will top up Mending gear on a belt.
- **Cake o' Enchanting** and **Bucket o' Enchanting** store experience in a form you can carry.

The mod also ships a Gem Cutter, Infuser, Brass Bookshelf, Affix Augmentor and the apotheotic essences. Those all need Apotheosis, which this pack does not have, so they are dead weight here. The working half is the loop above: experience in, enchanted gear out.

It needs **Create: Dragons Plus**, which also adds fan processing of its own: blow air through sand for bulk sanding, through powder snow for freezing, through a dragon head or dragon's breath for ending, and through golden aercloud for bulk enchanting. It brings a large set of dyes with it too.

## Automation extras
- **CC: Tweaked + CC:C Bridge**: Lua computers and mining turtles. The bridge lets computers read Create Display Links (stress, stock, train ETAs) and write to displays.
- **Slice & Dice / Delightful Creators**: automate Farmer's Delight cutting and cooking with Create; the Sprinkler fertilises crops.
- **Create: Oppenheimered**: recipes only, about 80 of them. It makes Alex's Caves Fissile Cores craftable with Create, so nukes and rayguns can be mass-produced.
- **Wireless Redstone**: transmitters and receivers that send a redstone signal without wires on a shared frequency, plus a handheld Remote.
- **Pipez**: item, fluid, energy and gas pipes, with 5 upgrade tiers up to Infinity. Alternate Current makes redstone lag-free, but its update order isn't vanilla, so some BUD and piston tutorials won't work.

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

# RFTools and Bigger Reactors

RFTools runs on FE, so you'll need an Alternator, Generator Coils, or a Bigger Reactor first. The two key materials are the Machine Frame (2 iron + gold nugget + blue dye, shaped `ili / g g / ili`) and Dimensional Shards, which you mine from rare Dimensional Shard Ore in the Overworld, Nether and End.

## The powers RFTools gives you
- **Environmental Controller**: a player buff field around your base. Modules include Flight, Regeneration/+, Speed/+, Haste/+, Saturation/+, Featherfalling/+, Peaceful (no hostile spawns), Waterbreathing and Night Vision. Several modules can share one controller, and FE cost grows with radius. Pair Flight with Featherfalling so you don't drop at the edge.
- **Teleporter / Matter Transmitter**: linked teleport pads, FE per jump.
- **Builder + Shape Cards**: quarry, Fortune Quarry, Silk Quarry, area clearing, pumping, voiding, structure copying.
- **Shield Projectors (T1 to T4)**: force-field walls for bases.
- **Crafter T1 to T3**: auto-crafters.
- **Screens**: live monitors for energy, inventory, fluids, redstone and machines.
- **RFTools Dimensions**: research dimlets at the Dimlet Workbench (fed with Lost Knowledge), combine them, then build your own dimension in the Dimension Builder. Categories: terrain, biome, structures, sky, time, blocks, fluids, features and more. 7 rarity tiers. Dimensions draw FE continuously, even while you're offline. Dimension "Blob" mobs have 30, 250 or 5,000 HP.

## What each RFTools module gives you
- **Matter Transmitter and Dialing Device** (Utility): stand on the transmitter, dial a receiver, and you teleport. It is one way, so a return trip needs a second pair.
- **Builder and shape cards** (Builder): the Builder does the digging and the building. A Quarry card mines an area and backfills with dirt, a Clearing card leaves air, and there are Silk Touch and Fortune versions. A Void card throws away the junk instead of filling your chests with cobble.
- **Dimension Builder** (Dimensions): makes your own dimension from Dimlets. It costs a lot to create and then keeps costing power to stay alive, and the fancier the dimension the higher that upkeep.
- **Screens and Powercells** (Utility): wall screens that show energy, fluid levels, item counts or plain text, and Powercells that store energy and can be linked so several of them share one pool.
- **Crafter** (Utility): simple autocrafting that can chain several recipes, useful long before you build anything bigger.

-# Exact RF numbers for the Builder and dimensions vary by version. Check the tooltips in game rather than trusting a wiki figure.

## Computers (CC: Tweaked)
Create ships ComputerCraft support out of the box: a computer can read a Speedometer or Stressometer, set a Rotation Speed Controller, drive a Sequenced Gearshift, and run Train Stations and schedules. CC:C Bridge adds more peripherals on top. In practice people use it for timed factory sequences, automatic train dispatch and status displays, the things redstone alone times badly.

## Bigger Reactors
A multiblock of reactor casing and glass around fuel rods, fuelled with uranium ingots, with a Power Tap for FE output. The passive reactor is simple. An active reactor feeding a Turbine (up to 32x32x192) is the most efficient FE setup in the pack. Pack multipliers are the defaults (1.0). Recycle waste in the Cyanite Reprocessor.

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

# How the bigger systems work
Step-by-step for the bigger systems.

## Travel network
- **Waystones** (stone bricks, a Warp Stone and obsidian, or found in the world and in villages): right-click one to register it, then warp between known stones for free.
- **Sharestones** (one per color): everyone with the same color shares the network. Great for a server hub.
- **Warp Stone / Scroll**: warp to a waystone from anywhere (30-second cooldown). Alex's Mobs' Dimensional Carver takes you home from anywhere.

## Sophisticated Backpacks
Tiers go leather, copper, iron, gold, diamond, then netherite (a smithing upgrade, 120 slots, 7 upgrade slots). Shift-right-click to wear it (`B` opens it). Upgrades:
- **Pickup / Magnet (+ Advanced)**: collects nearby items automatically, or from range.
- **Void / Filter**: deletes junk you pick up, or filters items going in and out.
- **Feeding (+ Advanced)**: feeds you from the pack.
- **Refill / Restock / Deposit**: keeps a hotbar stack topped up, and pulls from or dumps into a chest you sneak-click.
- **Compacting**: auto-crafts 9 items into a block.
- **Stack (up to Omega)**: much bigger stacks per slot.
- **Smelting / Smoking / Blasting (+ Auto)**: a working furnace inside the pack.
- **Crafting / Stonecutter / Anvil / Smithing**: those stations in a tab.
- **Tool Swapper (+ Advanced)**: auto-equips the right tool for what you hit.
- **Alchemy (+ Advanced)**: drinks potions and milk automatically when needed.
- **Battery / Tank / Pump / XP Pump**: portable energy, fluids and XP.
- **Inception / Everlasting**: backpacks inside backpacks, and a pack that can't be destroyed.
- **Jukebox / Chipped station upgrades**: music, and Chipped workbenches in a tab.

## Trains (Create + Steam 'n' Rails)
1. Make track (sequenced assembly on slabs), 2 Stations (railway casing plus compass), and Train Controls.
2. Place bogeys on the track, build the carriage on them, and add controls. Right-click the station to assemble.
3. Give the train a Schedule (stops plus conditions like "wait until cargo is full").
4. Craft a Conductor (Conductor's Cap on andesite casing) so it drives the schedule with nobody aboard.

## Robots (CC: Tweaked)
1. Craft a Computer (stone, redstone and a glass pane) or a Turtle (7 iron plus a computer plus a chest).
2. Write Lua on its screen, for example `turtle.dig()` or `turtle.forward()`, or run the built-in `excavate` program.
3. Add a Wireless or Ender Modem to network machines. CC:C Bridge lets code read Create data (stress, stock, train ETAs) and write to Create displays.

## Supplementaries gadgets
- **Quiver** (`V` cycles arrows), **Slingshot** (throw items; the Stasis enchant makes shots fly straight), **Cannon** (mount it on a boat or raft, fires whatever you load).
- **Rope** plus **Rope Arrows** (shoot a climbable rope), **Sack** (bulk storage, heavy), **Safe** (locks to your key), **Jar** (holds mobs, fluids or items), **Faucet**, **Globe**.
- **Lumisene** fuel powers two special enchants: Lumisene Fire Aspect and Lumisene Flame.

## Villagers, taverns and misc
- Cartographers and wandering traders sell Underground Cabin maps (Alex's Caves). Taverns from Dungeons and Taverns make safe rest stops.
- Drink Beer bartending: a Bartending Table, recipe boards, kegs, and Call Bells to summon named barkeeps.
- Comforts: the sleeping bag skips the night and the hammock skips the day, without moving your spawn. Chunk loaders keep farms running (they switch off after 7 days offline).
- Graveyard altar ritual: see the Lich in Bosses. Dark Iron (corruption plus iron) is a decorative metal.

## Magic trees and the Uncrafting Table (Twilight Forest)
- Magic saplings grow in the Twilight Forest. Put the matching core into a grown magic tree: Time speeds up growth nearby, Transformation turns the area into Twilight biome blocks, Mining mines ores around it, and Sorting sorts nearby chests. They run on their own.
- Uncrafting Table: put a crafted item in to break it back into its ingredients. Good for salvaging rare materials from gear you don't use.
- Quest Ram (Enchanted Forest): bring it every color of wool for the Crumble Horn and a loot bundle.

## Transmutation Table and Capsid (Alex's Mobs)
- Transmutation Table: needs a Nether Star, 2 Farseer Arms and 3 obsidian. Converts items into rarer ones over 3 levels each. Breaking it can cause an explosion.
- Capsid (dropped by End Enderiophages) is a display case that pushes items upward and transforms certain items placed in it: a Crimson Mosquito Larva becomes a Mysterious Worm (which summons the Void Worm), a Cod becomes a Cosmic Cod (tames Cosmaws), and a Dimensional Carver becomes a Shattered Carver, a one-way trip 1,000,000 blocks out.

## Change a biome (Alex's Caves)
1. Get a Biome Treat (a Candy Cavity item, check JEI for its recipe). Eat it while starving in the biome you want, and it binds to that biome.
2. Feed the Conversion Crucible that biome's blocks, the treat, and a rare item (diamond, netherite, or a shulker shell). It converts a small area into that biome.
3. Bonus: Gingerbread Men hoard 9 stolen candies in a Gingerbarrel, which assembles Gingerbread Armor. The more worn it is, the faster you move.

## Deep One trading (Alex's Caves)
1. Reach the Abyssal Chasm in Diving armor or a Submarine.
2. Craft an Abyssal Altar (a Pearl on top of Abyssmarine) and place Pearls on it near Deep Ones. Each offering raises their opinion of you, and a Gazing Pearl shows their mood.
3. Friendly Deep Ones stop fleeing, help you fight, and leave gifts on the altar: Ortholance, Magic Conch (from Knights, about 4% each), Sea Staff (from Mages, about 4%), tridents, Bottles of Enchanting, nautilus shells. These can't be crafted anywhere else.

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

# Food and drink

In this pack, food works like potions. Farmer's Delight meals give two core buffs, and drinks, wines and Twilight dishes add real combat effects. AppleSkin shows how much hunger and saturation a food gives before you eat it.
- **Nourishment**: your hunger bar stops draining, except when saturation heals you. Comes from Farmer's Delight plate meals; in this pack, vanilla stews and soups give it too.
- **Comfort**: natural regeneration at any hunger level. Comes from Farmer's Delight bowl meals (soups, stews).
- **Strength III + Regen II (5 minutes)**: a big combat buff. Comes from the Plate of Fiery Snakes (Twilight Delight).
- **Strength V (30 seconds)**: a burst buff. Comes from Chocolate 113 (Twilight Delight).
- **Fire / Frozen / Poison Range**: an aura that hurts, slows or poisons nearby mobs. Comes from Twilight Delight dishes.
- **Raging**: +5% attack speed per hit, stacks up to 4 times. Comes from Brewin' & Chewin' drinks.
- **Sweet Heart**: extra saturation healing at any hunger level. Comes from Brewin' & Chewin'.
- **Tipsy / Intoxication**: wobbling, scrambled text, no saturation gain. Comes from drinking too much alcohol.
- **Sugar Rush**: slows time around you. Comes from Alex's Caves sweets, best from the Sundae.

## Vinery wines (they get stronger with age)
Ferment grapes in a barrel, then store the wine in a rack. A wine gains 1 effect level every 6 in-game years, up to level 5 at 30 years. Duration grows too, from 30 minutes up to about 4 hours.
- **Jellie Wine**: Absorption and Regeneration, people say it beats a golden apple. Key ingredient: Apple plus Chenet and Bolvar wine.
- **Aegis Wine**: extra armor and toughness. Key ingredient: Kelp plus iron.
- **Eiswein**: Frosty Armor, +4 armor, +2 damage, -5% speed. Key ingredient: Snowballs.
- **Apple Cider**: Strength. Key ingredient: Sugar.
- **Apple Wine**: Resistance. Key ingredient: Apple juice.
- **Solaris / Stal Wine**: Health Boost, +4 max HP. Key ingredient: Honey, berries, cocoa.
- **Clark Wine**: double jump. Key ingredient: Sugar.
- **Bolvar Wine**: walk on lava. Key ingredient: Honey plus cherry.
- **Kelp Cider**: walk on water. Key ingredient: Kelp.
- **Chenet Wine**: wall climbing. Key ingredient: Spider eye plus honey.
- **Cristel Wine**: water breathing. Key ingredient: Feather plus blaze rod.
- **Magnetic Wine**: pulls items to you. Key ingredient: Iron.
- **Mead / Noir / Red Wine**: Haste, Jump Boost, Slow Falling. Key ingredient: Honey, berries, sugar.
- **Cherry / Strad Wine**: Invisibility, Night Vision. Key ingredient: Cherries, cocoa.
- **Mojang Noir**: +25% XP per level. Key ingredient: Honey, cherry, red wine.
- **Mellohi Wine**: Instant Health, no aging needed. Key ingredient: Glowstone.
- **Lilitu Wine**: your hits launch damaging fireworks. Key ingredient: Honey plus cherry.
- **Creeper's Crush / Villager's Fright**: you explode after 5 seconds, or get Bad Omen, which starts a raid. Key ingredient: Gunpowder, arrow.

Ingredients come from the pack's own recipes. The effect names come from the Vinery wiki.

## What the buffs actually do
- **Farmer's Delight** is where the useful effects are. Soups and stews give **Comfort**, which regenerates health quietly over time. The big plated meals give **Nourishment**, which stops running, jumping and fighting from draining your hunger. The bigger the meal, the longer it lasts.
- **Croptopia** is the opposite: hundreds of dishes, all of them pure food and saturation with no effects. Use it for feeding yourself cheaply and for ingredients.
- **Delightful**, **More Delight** and **Croptopia Delight** are bridges. They let Croptopia's crops go into Farmer's Delight cutting boards and cooking pots, and add extra dishes to both.

## Drinks and smokes
- **Vinery**: gather wild grapes with a Basket, press them in a Grapevine Pot for juice, then ferment that in a Fermenting Barrel. Wine keeps ageing in the barrel, so older is better. An Applepress does cider. It also brings a full vineyard building set and a wine-themed armour set.
- **Brewin' and Chewin'**: a **Keg** with four ingredient slots, a liquid slot and a container slot, for fermenting liquors, cheese and fudge, each recipe wanting its own time. It also talks to Create, so a Spout can fill and empty kegs as part of a line.
- **Drink Beer Refill**: nine base beers, and mixing them with spices turns up new flavours to find, plus tavern decoration to go with them.
- **Tobacconist**: growing, curing, fermenting and blending tobacco into cigars, cigarettes, pipes and hookahs. It has optional Create integration if you want it industrial.

## Magic food and drink
- **Farmer's Spell**: drinks that restore mana. Ink Beer gives back 50% of your max mana, Evasion Latte 15%, Goodberry Juice and Butterbeer 10%, and Pumpkin Juice 5%. Its Alchemist Pot does the magic cooking, and the Gluttony rings add up to +150 max mana while you have Nourishment.
- **Ars Nouveau's Flavors & Delight**: Wilden and Chimera meat dishes give the Wilden effect (+20% max mana, mana regen and spell damage per level). It also has Archwood fruit pies and jellies, and foods with Blast Resistance, Flourishing (you regain mana when you heal), Synchronized Shield or Freezing Spell. Wilden Hunter and Chimera meat drop when you kill them with a knife.

## Other kitchens
- **Drink Beer**: Miner Pale Ale gives Haste for 1:00, Blaze Stout and Milk Stout give Fire Resistance for 1:30 and 2:00, Apple Lambic and Sweet Berry Kriek give Regen, Night Howl Kvass gives Night Vision (longer at a full moon), and Frothy Pink Eggnog gives Absorption. Drinking too much can kill you.
- **Brewin' & Chewin'**: keg fermentation at set temperatures, put hot or cold blocks next to the keg, about 8 minutes per batch. Makes beer, mead, rice wine, vodka, cheeses, kimchi, jerky, pizza.
- **Croptopia**: about 60 crops and about 30 fruit/nut trees; seeds come from grass, traders and wild crops. The top-end meal is the Transcendental Breakfast, a Nether Star Cake plus a Dragon Egg Omelette (this uses up the dragon egg) plus more.
- **Farmer's Delight feasts** (placed, multi-serving): Roast Chicken, Stuffed Pumpkin, Shepherd's Pie, Honey Glazed Ham, Rice Roll Medley.
- **Tobacconist**: grow 6 tobacco types, cure them with air, sun, fire or flue, then ferment in barrels. Crop quality depends on light, heat and rain.
- **More Delight and Croptopia Delight**: more Farmer's Delight dishes (toasts, sandwiches, burgers, rice and pasta plates, stone and wooden knives), and Croptopia's crops cooked in Farmer's Delight cookware.
- **Automation**: Slice & Dice runs cutting-board recipes with Create; Delightful Creators turns soups into fluids you can bottle with spouts.

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

# Building and gadgets


## Handy gadgets (Supplementaries & friends)
- **Supplementaries**: Quiver (`V` to switch arrows), Slingshot, Bombs, a boat-mounted Cannon, Rope (climb and pulley), Sack, Safe, Jar (holds mobs, fluids or items), Globe, Flute, Faucets, Signposts, Notice Boards, Clock blocks, Lumisene, and lots of vanilla-style decor.
- **Sophisticated Backpacks**: leather to copper to iron to gold to diamond to netherite. Top upgrades: Magnet, Void, Stack (Omega), Restock, Feeding (auto-eat), Compacting, Crafting, Tank, Battery, and Inception (backpacks inside backpacks).
- **Iron Chests**: goes up to Crystal (see-through) and Obsidian (blast-proof). Chunk Loaders run from radius 1 to 4 and switch off after 7 days offline.
- **Waystones**: free travel between stones, plus warp stones and scrolls. Comforts adds sleeping bags and hammocks.

- **Sit** on any stair or slab by right-clicking it.

## Decoration mods
- **Chipped**: turns almost any vanilla block into hundreds of variants. This is the one that needs its own benches: the Carpenter's Table, Mason's Table, Alchemy Bench, Mechanist's Workbench and the rest, each handling its own material.
- **Macaw's Furniture** (over 650 recipes) and **Macaw's Lights and Lamps**: couches, wardrobes, desks, sinks, counters, then street lamps, chandeliers, paper lamps and wall lanterns. Plain crafting table.
- **Handcrafted**: chairs, tables, benches, desks, cushions and crockery in every wood type. Plain crafting table.
- **Dawn of Time**: over 400 recipes of Japanese, Mayan, Roman and French building blocks, for when vanilla blocks run out of looks. Plain crafting table.
- **Decorative Blocks**: rustic pieces like palisades, braziers, bonfires, trellises, chandeliers and seats. Craftable normally, and many also cut from a woodcutter.
- **Supplementaries**: the vanilla-plus one. Jars, sconces, signposts, faucets, planters, pedestals and the rest, mixed in with the gadgets above.
- **Immersive Furniture**: build your own furniture from an in-game editor rather than fixed recipes.
- **Curious Lanterns**: more lantern styles to build with, and any of them can hang off your belt for light.
- **Create: Bells & Whistles** for dressing up train stations and factories, and **Gauges and Switches** for industrial-looking redstone panels, dials and alarm lights.

Resource packs include Enhanced Audio, Visual Titles and Pretty Pipez.

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

# All 256 mods

## Dimensions & adventure (11)
- **Alex's Caves**: six rare underground biomes (magnetic, primordial, toxic, abyssal, forlorn, candy) with unique mobs, gear and a nuke.
- **Alex's Caves: Stuff & Torpedoes**: Alex's Caves add-on: torpedoes, submarine-hunting gear and armor for the Abyssal Chasm.
- **Better End**: overhauls the End: dozens of biomes, new ores/metals, Eternal Portal, infusion rituals, gear.
- **Better Nether**: Nether biomes, flora, mobs and Cincinnasite/Nether Ruby gear.
- **Bygone Nether**: ancient nether remnants: new mobs, structures and gilded gear.
- **Create: Alex's Caves Compat**: Create processing recipes for Alex's Caves materials.
- **The Twilight Forest**: a boss-by-boss progression world with its own gear tiers.
- **True Ending: Ender Dragon Overhaul**: a dramatic Ender Dragon overhaul with new attacks.
- **Twilight Forest - Dungeons & Villages**: new villages and dungeons inside the Twilight Forest.
- **Twilight's Flavor & Delight**: Twilight Forest foods and cooking in Farmer's Delight style (plus a few weapons).
- **YUNG's Better End Island**: YUNG's redesign of the main End island and dragon arena.

## Structures & exploration (28)
- **Bartering Station**: automates Piglin bartering.
- **Dungeons and Taverns**: taverns, dungeons and themed structures.
- **Dungeons and Taverns Ancient City Overhaul**: overhauled Ancient Cities.
- **Epic Structures: Villages**: sprawling medieval cities and towns.
- **FixMySpawnR**: mob spawners shut off after a set time (bypass with redstone).
- **Forgotten Ruins**: NPC-civilization ruins, ancient golems and lost scrolls.
- **Lootr**: per-player loot chests, every player gets their own loot on the server.
- **MoogsEndStructures**: Moog's End Structures.
- **MoogsNetherStructures**: Moog's Nether Structures.
- **MoogsSoaringStructures**: Moog's Soaring (sky) Structures.
- **MoogsTemplesReimagined**: Moog's Temples Reimagined.
- **MoogsVoyagerStructures**: Moog's Voyager Structures (160+ overworld builds).
- **No More Villages**: disables vanilla village generation (replaced by the village mods above).
- **Repurposed Structures**: biome variants of vanilla structures (mansions, temples, mineshafts, villages and more).
- **Tidal Towns**: floating driftwood villages on the ocean.
- **Towns and Towers**: new villages, outposts and ships.
- **Traveler's Titles**: RPG-style title cards when entering biomes/dimensions.
- **Trek**: a broad pack of new overworld structures.
- **When Dungeons Arise**: huge roguelike castles, towers, sky fortresses and ships with big loot.
- **YUNG's Better Desert Temples**: YUNG's desert temple redesign (puzzles, Pharaoh's traps).
- **YUNG's Better Dungeons**: YUNG's dungeon redesign (skeleton, spider, zombie dungeons, catacombs).
- **YUNG's Better Jungle Temples**: YUNG's jungle temple redesign.
- **YUNG's Better Mineshafts**: YUNG's mineshaft overhaul.
- **YUNG's Better Nether Fortresses**: YUNG's Nether fortress redesign.
- **YUNG's Better Ocean Monuments**: YUNG's ocean monument redesign.
- **YUNG's Better Strongholds**: YUNG's stronghold redesign.
- **YUNG's Better Witch Huts**: YUNG's witch hut redesign.
- **YUNG's Bridges**: natural bridges across rivers and ravines.

## World generation & biomes (5)
- **Oh The Biomes We've Gone**: 80+ overworld and nether biomes with new woods and plants.
- **Oh The Trees You'll Grow**: tree-template library for BWG.
- **Regions Unexplored**: 70+ new biomes.
- **TerraBlender**: lets multiple biome mods coexist.
- **YUNG's Cave Biomes**: frosted caves, lost caves (sand), marble.

## Bosses & mobs (12)
- **Alex's Mobs**: about 90 creatures with drops that craft unique gear, potions and powers; Void Worm boss.
- **Aquamirae**: Ice Maze ship-graveyard biome, Captain Cornelia, deep-sea horrors and strong gear sets.
- **Bosses'Rise**: a set of world bosses with special attacks and unique drops.
- **DarkDoppelganger**: a 6,000 HP End boss that copies your gear and spells.
- **Ecologics**: vanilla-plus biome updates with small mobs (coconut crabs, penguins, squirrels) and plants.
- **Minecraft Comes Alive Reborn**: villagers become people with names, faces, families and jobs, and villages get their own guards.
- **Mowzie's Mobs**: high-quality bosses (Ferrous Wroughtnaut, Frostmaw, Umvuthi, Sculptor and more) with signature powers.
- **Naturalist**: realistic wildlife (bears, deer, snakes, birds and more) with real behaviors.
- **Nyctophobia**: spooky, foggy, dark new biomes.
- **Rotten Creatures**: many new zombie variants (frostbitten, swampy, burned, dead beard and more).
- **Spawn**: niche animals on land and sea with personalities, drops and foods.
- **The Graveyard**: graveyards, crypts, undead mobs and the Lich boss.

## Weapons, armor & accessories (10)
- **Armor of the Ages**: fancy historic armor sets from world cultures.
- **Artifacts**: uncraftable magic accessories (curios) found in loot and mimics.
- **AttributeFix**: lifts vanilla attribute caps so high armor/damage values work.
- **Charm of Undying**: wear a Totem of Undying in a curio slot.
- **Curios API**: the accessory slot system (rings, necklaces, belts, etc.).
- **Elytra Slot**: wear an elytra in a curio slot alongside chest armor.
- **Ensorcellation**: many extra enchantments and vanilla enchantment improvements.
- **Immersive Armors**: vanilla-faithful armor sets with set effects (Wither, Divine, Warrior, Heavy and more).
- **Shield Expansion**: shield tiers and a parry mechanic.
- **Simply Swords**: the main weapon mod: 15 weapon types x 5 materials, plus dozens of unique legendary swords with abilities.

## Magic (26)
- **Adam's Ars Plus**: seven Archmage bosses with their own dungeons, boss armor stronger than netherite, Domain spells and Ten Shadows summons.
- **Alex's Caves Spellbooks**: Vesper and Grottoceratops summon spells, plus the Dread Robes and Primordial Mage armor, for Iron's Spells.
- **Apprentice's Codex**: 84 Iron's spells, spell guns, staves, turrets, companions and utility magic.
- **Ars 'n' Spells**: joins Ars Nouveau and Iron's Spells: one shared mana bar, shared spell power, and a damage bonus at full mana.
- **Ars Additions**: protection charms, warp and storage gear, a few glyphs, and 3 structures (Arcane Library, Nexus Tower, Ruined Warp Portals).
- **Ars Artifice**: curios that cast a stored spell for you on a trigger (getting hurt, falling, landing, a timer), and Spell Gems.
- **Ars Controle**: a Remote for retargeting spells and a warping Spell Prism.
- **Ars Creo**: Spell Turrets and Source Jars on Create contraptions, and a Starbuncle-powered water wheel.
- **Ars Elemental**: Fire, Water, Air and Earth schools with foci, bangles, four elemental armor sets, new glyphs and creatures.
- **Ars Nouveau**: build your own spells from glyphs, plus magical automation, rituals, familiars and helper creatures.
- **Ars Nouveau - Extended Glyphs**: 19 utility glyphs and the Magestone gear line.
- **Ars Nouveau Trinkets**: tiered mana rings, lotuses and monocles, Arcane Runes and 25+ combat glyphs.
- **Ars Nouveau's Flavors & Delight**: Farmer's Delight food from Wilden and Archwood with mana and spell buffs, plus the Enchanter's Knife.
- **Ars Technica**: Create crossover: 10 glyphs, the Technomancer armor and Source-powered machines.
- **Ars Zero**: three-phase staff casting, the Psion's Circlet, and the Blight Forest with its Necropolis dungeon.
- **Farmer's Spell**: the Gluttony school, mana-restoring food and drinks, and the Foodgeist.
- **GTBC's Geomancy Plus**: the Geo school for Iron's Spells, spells from Mowzie's Mobs bosses, and the Geomancer Tower.
- **Iron's Spellbooks Tweaks**: server settings for Iron's Spells (left at defaults here).
- **Iron's Spells 'n Spellbooks**: RPG-style spells in 9 schools, spellbooks, mage armor and weapons, wizard towers and boss dungeons.
- **ISS: Magic From The East**: the Symmetry and Spirit schools, jade gear, eastern-myth mobs, and two shrine structures.
- **Not Enough Glyphs**: extra glyphs (Plow, Echoing Projectile, propagators) and the Spell Binder.
- **Refined Mod**: weapon-scaling spells and three magic weapons (Flame Tongue, Summer Rain, Hunter's Bow).
- **Starbunclemania**: new Starbuncle and Wixie jobs, like moving fluids and power.
- **Too Many Glyphs**: targeting filters, the Ray form and stronger Amplify.
- **Twilight Spellbooks**: Twilight Forest boss spellbooks, rings and weapons for Iron's Spells.
- **Wind's Spellbooks**: the Wind school (7 spells, Tornado disabled here), the Aeromancer and the Windmill.

## Space, ships & vehicles (11)
- **Clockwork**: propellers, balloons, gyros and flaps to pilot VS2 airships with Create.
- **Create Planetary Tweaks**: recipe/progression glue for the Create plus Northstar space path.
- **Create: Propulsion**: thrusters and more for VS2 ships.
- **Do a Barrel Roll**: plane-style elytra controls (roll and banking; thrust is off in this pack).
- **Dragon Mounts: Legacy**: hatch dragon eggs into rideable, breed-specific flying dragons.
- **Immersive Aircraft**: rustic aircraft: airships, biplane, gyrodyne, quadrocopter, warship and more.
- **Immersive Machinery**: rustic vehicles/machines: tunnel digger, copperfin, bamboo bee, redstone sheep and friends.
- **Man of Many Planes**: extra Create-flavored aircraft for Immersive Aircraft.
- **Northstar Curios Compat**: oxygen tanks work from Curios slots for Northstar.
- **Northstar Redux**: Create-powered rockets, oxygen, space suits and travel to other planets.
- **Valkyrien Skies 2**: turn builds into physics ships that sail and fly.

## Tech & automation (25)
- **Alternate Current**: faster, lag-free redstone dust implementation (behavior-compatible).
- **Bigger Reactors**: multiblock yellorium fission reactors and turbines, the big FE generator.
- **CC: Tweaked**: programmable Lua computers, turtles, monitors and modems.
- **CC:C Bridge**: lets ComputerCraft read and drive Create (source/target blocks, train schedules, animatronic).
- **Chunk Loaders**: tiered chunk loaders to keep farms and machines running.
- **Create**: rotational power (SU), mechanical crafting, contraptions, trains, and fluid and item logistics.
- **Create Crafts & Additions**: converts Create rotation into FE electricity (alternator/electric motor), wires, rolling mill, tesla coil charging.
- **Create Nuclear**: full fission reactor multiblock (2M+ SU), uranium processing, radiation and anti-radiation suits.
- **Create Slice & Dice**: slicer machine that runs Farmer's Delight cutting-board recipes automatically.
- **Create: Bells & Whistles**: train cosmetics and utility: pilots, seats, doors, platform blocks.
- **Create: Diesel Generators**: crude oil, distillation, diesel/gasoline engines, plus lighters, chemical sprayer and oil pumpjacks.
- **Create: Dragons Plus**: bulk sanding, freezing, ending and enchanting with Create fans, plus a lot of dyes.
- **Create: Enchantment Industry**: automated enchanting, liquid experience, book printing and disenchanting.
- **Create: New Age**: electricity for Create: magnetite motors/generators, solar heating, energisers and a small nuclear reactor.
- **Create: Oppenheimered**: crafting bridge between Create and Alex's Caves' nuclear tech.
- **Create: Power Grid**: a real electrical network layer (generators, cables, transformers) for Create.
- **Create: Steam 'n' Rails**: more track types, conductors, semaphores, couplers, wide and narrow gauge, and train utilities.
- **Delightful Creators**: more Create/Farmer's Delight automation recipes.
- **Gauges and Switches**: decorative redstone gauges, levers, sensors and detector switches.
- **Pipez**: simple, fast item/fluid/energy/gas pipes with upgrade tiers and filters.
- **RFToolsBase**: base for RFTools: machine frames, dimensional shards, information screen.
- **RFToolsBuilder**: builder and shape cards: quarries, area clearing, structure copying, spaceship-style moves.
- **RFToolsDimensions**: design and build your own dimensions from dimlets, powered by FE.
- **RFToolsUtility**: RFTools utilities: teleporters, crafters, screens, environmental controller (flight, regen and other area effects).
- **Wireless Redstone**: transmitter/receiver pairs that send redstone signals over distance by frequency.

## Food & farming (9)
- **[Let's Do] Vinery**: grow grapes, run a winery, drink wines with effects.
- **Brewin' And Chewin'**: keg fermentation: beers, meads, cheeses and the Tipsy effect.
- **Croptopia**: about 60 new crops and hundreds of foods; seeds from grass and wild crops.
- **Croptopia Delight**: Croptopia recipes in Farmer's Delight cookware.
- **Delightful**: Farmer's Delight compat foods and extra meals.
- **Drink Beer Refill**: placeable beer mugs and beer brewing with effects.
- **Farmer's Delight**: cooking pot, skillet, cutting board; Comfort and Nourishment meal buffs.
- **More Delight**: more Farmer's Delight tools and dishes.
- **Tobacconist**: grow tobacco and make smokables.

## Building & decoration (10)
- **Chipped**: thousands of block variants via workbenches.
- **Comforts**: sleeping bags (skip night without moving spawn) and hammocks (skip day).
- **Curious Lanterns**: lanterns you can wear on your belt for light, plus more lantern types to build with.
- **Dawn Of Time**: historic building blocks from world cultures.
- **Decorative Blocks**: beams, palisades, seats, chandeliers, brazier.
- **Handcrafted**: furniture: couches, tables, cupboards, trophies.
- **Immersive Furniture**: design custom furniture in-game or pick from an online library.
- **Macaw's Furniture**: hundreds of furniture pieces.
- **Macaw's Lights and Lamps**: more lighting and lamp options.
- **Supplementaries**: vanilla-plus functional blocks: jars, sacks, rope, bombs, slingshot, cannon, quiver, globes, clocks and more.

## Storage, travel & utility (14)
- **Corpse**: your items stay in a corpse when you die.
- **corpsecurioscompat**: Curios items go into your corpse too.
- **FallingTree**: breaking one log fells the whole tree.
- **Fast Travel Waypoints**: travel between waystones from Xaero's world map.
- **Iron Chests**: bigger chests: iron to gold to diamond to crystal/obsidian/netherite.
- **Logprot**: invulnerability for a few seconds after logging in.
- **Nature's Compass**: locate any biome.
- **Polymorph**: choose between conflicting recipe outputs.
- **Saplanting**: saplings lying on the ground plant themselves.
- **Simple Login**: server login/password system.
- **Sit**: sit on slabs and stairs.
- **Sophisticated Backpacks**: upgradeable backpacks (magnet, feeding, crafting, stack and more).
- **Sophisticated Core**: core for Sophisticated Backpacks.
- **Waystones**: teleport network of waystones, warp stones and scrolls.

## Interface & info (18)
- **AppleSkin**: hunger/saturation preview.
- **BetterF3**: readable F3 debug screen.
- **Drippy Loading Screen**: the pack's custom loading screen.
- **EnchantmentDescriptions**: explains enchantments in tooltips.
- **FancyMenu**: the pack's custom menus.
- **Inventory HUD+**: shows inventory/armor/potion HUD on screen.
- **Inventory Profiles Next**: inventory sorting, auto-refill and profiles.
- **Jade**: shows what you're looking at (block/mob info).
- **Just Enough Items**: look up any recipe with `R` or any use with `U`.
- **Just Enough Resources**: also called JER, ore distribution, mob drops and loot shown right in JEI.
- **Legendary Tooltips**: fancy tooltip borders for rare items.
- **libIPN**: library for Inventory Profiles Next.
- **Mob Plaques**: mob health/armor shown above their heads.
- **Mouse Tweaks**: better click-drag item handling.
- **Ok Zoomer**: zoom key `C`.
- **Patchouli**: in-game guidebook system.
- **Xaero's Minimap**: minimap with waypoints.
- **Xaero's World Map**: full-screen world map, key `M`.

## Visuals & audio (17)
- **3d-Skin-Layers**: 3D skin layers.
- **AmbientSounds**: rich biome ambient sounds.
- **Athena**: connected-texture model loader.
- **Better Animations Collection**: smoother mob animations.
- **Clean Swing Through Grass**: swing swords through grass.
- **Cull Less Leaves Reforged**: nicer leaf rendering.
- **CustomSkinLoader Bootstrap**: custom skin sources.
- **Distant Horizons**: renders far-away terrain as LODs for huge view distances.
- **Fusion**: connected textures for resource packs.
- **Magic Circles - ISAS Addon**: shows a glowing circle under you when you cast Iron's spells.
- **NotEnoughAnimations**: better third-person animations.
- **Oculus**: shader support (Iris for Forge).
- **Particular**: extra ambient particles (fireflies, cascades and more).
- **Presence Footsteps [FORGE]**: material-accurate footstep sounds.
- **Radiant Gear**: dynamic lights for curio items.
- **RyoamicLights**: dynamic lights from held torches and similar.
- **Sound Physics Remastered**: realistic echo/reverb and muffling through walls.

## Performance (15)
- **betterfpsdist mod**: smarter render distance shape.
- **Embeddium**: Sodium-based renderer, a big FPS boost.
- **EntityCulling**: skip rendering hidden entities.
- **Fastload-Reforged**: faster world loading.
- **Ferrite Core**: memory reduction.
- **Krypton Reforged**: network optimization.
- **Lightspeed**: faster pack loading.
- **Memory Leak Fix**: memory-leak fixes.
- **ModernFix**: load-time and memory fixes.
- **PacketFixer**: stops some connection errors in big modpacks.
- **Saturn**: memory optimization.
- **Smooth Boot (Reloaded)**: smoother startup threads.
- **Sodium Extras**: extra render options.
- **Sodium Options API**: options API for Embeddium.
- **Starlight**: faster light engine.

## Libraries (45)
Architectury, AzureLib, BaguetteLib, Balm, BCLib, Bookshelf, Botarium, BOZOID, Caelus API, Citadel, Cloth Config v10 API, CoFH Core, CorgiLib, CreativeCore, Cristel Lib, Cupboard utilities, GeckoLib 4, GTBC's SpellLib, Iceberg, Iron's Lib, Konkrete, Kotlin for Forge, McJtyLib, Melody, Moog's Structure Lib, Moonlight Library, Obscure API, ObsidianUI, OpenLoader, Phosphophyllite, Platform, Player Animator, Prism, Puzzles Lib, Quartz, Resourceful Lib, Resourcefulconfig, SmartBrainLib, SuperMartijn642's Config Library, SuperMartijn642's Core Lib, Wheel, WunderLib, YetAnotherConfigLib, YUNG's API, Zero CORE 2

Libraries and performance mods are listed too, just so you can see everything that's actually running.

> This is everything we found, not everything there is. Two hundred and fifty-six mods, layered together, make things none of them were built to do alone, and the world generates places nobody's seen yet.

> About this guide: built from the pack's own files (recipes, loot tables, configs, item and boss classes), then cross-checked against mod wikis and community discussion. Where they disagree, the pack's files win. Some boss numbers and stats come from wikis and are marked as such. We also checked the server pack, and it doesn't change any of the mods' server-side settings, so the defaults described here are what the server runs. An admin could still change them later.
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

# Mods that work well together

Most mods here work on their own. These are the places where two or three of them add up to something neither does alone. Nothing below is required, and none of it is a puzzle you have to solve in order.

## Enchanting on a conveyor (Create + Enchantment Industry)
- A **Mechanical Grindstone** disenchants gear, and a **Grindstone Drain** underneath catches the experience as **Liquid Experience**. An **Experience Hatch** collects what mobs and furnaces give off.
- Liquid experience moves in normal Create fluid pipes, so it can be tanked and pumped like anything else.
- A **Blaze Enchanter** applies enchantments, drinking that fluid. A **Blaze Forger** does the anvil work, repairing and combining.
- A Spout pouring liquid experience onto Mending gear on a belt keeps your armour topped up without you touching it.
- The Gem Cutter, Infuser, Brass Bookshelf and Affix Augmentor in that mod need Apotheosis, which this pack doesn't have, so ignore them.

## Getting electricity for RFTools (Create + Crafts & Additions)
- Create machines run on rotation. The RFTools Builder, teleporters and dimensions run on FE, and never the two shall meet on their own.
- An **Alternator** turns rotation into FE as long as you feed it at least 32 RPM. New Age's **Generator Coils** with **Carbon Brushes** do the same job.
- Going the other way, an **Electric Motor** turns FE back into rotation at whatever RPM you scroll it to.
- **Bigger Reactors** skips the conversion entirely and makes FE directly, which is why people building a quarry usually end up there.

## A forest that replants itself (FallingTree + Saplanting + backpacks)
- Break one log and **FallingTree** drops the whole tree, leaves included.
- The saplings that fall then plant themselves a couple of seconds later, because of **Saplanting**.
- A **Magnet upgrade** in a Sophisticated Backpack hoovers up the logs and apples while you walk away.
- Put together, a wood run is: walk, break one block per tree, walk on. The forest is back by the time you return.

## Source without babysitting (Ars Nouveau)
- **Agronomic Sourcelinks** take growth happening near them, so they sit in a farm and do nothing else. Mageblooms and Source Berries pay more.
- **Volcanic Sourcelinks** want lava nearby, which makes them the steady option once you're in the Nether or over a lava lake.
- **Fuel-based Sourcelinks** burn items fed from pedestals, useful when you have too much charcoal.
- Ars moves Source through Source Jars and relays, not through item pipes. Where it meets the rest of the pack is items: familiars and turrets drop things, and Create belts or Pipez carry them onward.

## Food chains that feed each other
- **Croptopia** grows the ingredients, **Farmer's Delight** cooks the dishes that actually buff you, and **Croptopia Delight**, **Delightful** and **More Delight** are the glue that lets one use the other.
- **Create Slice & Dice** automates Farmer's Delight cutting boards and cooking pots, so a farm can produce finished meals.
- **Brewin' and Chewin'** kegs can be filled and emptied by a Create Spout, which means a brewery is just another production line.

## Travel that compounds
- Waystones are free once found, and the wild ones now sit roughly every 24 chunks.
- **Fast Travel Waypoints** lets you jump to any of them from Xaero's world map, as long as you're standing near a waystone.
- Xaero's map also drops a **death waypoint**, and **Corpse** keeps your gear where you died, so a long trip going wrong costs time rather than everything you own.
- **Sharestones** work per colour and anyone can use them, which is what makes a shared hub worth building.

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

# Playing together

The server runs the pack as it ships, with a handful of settings that matter when other people are around.

## What other players can and can't take
- **Loot is yours alone.** Every chest that came with the world rolls separately for each player, so nobody can clear a dungeon out from under you, and going through somewhere already explored is still worth it.
- **Corpses are not private.** Anyone can open your corpse. A friend can bring your gear back, and someone else can walk off with it.
- **PvP is on.** Keep that in mind near a corpse, and near anyone you don't know.
- **The difficulty is Normal**, the same for everyone.

## Things worth sharing
- **Sharestones** link everyone using the same colour, which makes them the natural base for a public hub. Ordinary waystones work per player once found.
- **Chunk loaders** keep farms running while you're offline, but they switch themselves off after seven days without you. Long breaks mean stopped machines, not lost ones.
- **Villagers are people now.** Guards defend a village for whoever lives near it, and a village someone else has built up is a shared asset, so raiding it makes enemies faster than it makes emeralds.

## Being easy to play with
- Waystones you place are worth naming clearly, since everyone sees the list.
- Chunk loaders and big Create factories cost server performance. If things feel slow, that's usually the first place to look.
- If you find a structure nobody has mentioned, say so. Half of what's in this guide came from someone stumbling into something.

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

# Version history

What changed in the pack, newest first. Only the things that affect how you play.

## 4.1, September 2026
**Added**
- **Minecraft Comes Alive Reborn**: villagers became people with names, families, moods and guards. Existing villagers were converted, keeping professions, trades and levels. Villager experience and reputation discounts reset.
- **Fast Travel Waypoints**: travel to any waystone you've found from Xaero's world map, free, as long as you're standing near a waystone.
- **Curious Lanterns**: wear a lantern on your belt for light, plus more lantern styles to build with.
- **FallingTree**: one log fells the whole tree.
- **Saplanting**: dropped saplings plant themselves.
- **Create: Enchantment Industry** and **Create: Dragons Plus**: liquid experience, automated enchanting, and bulk fan processing.

**Changed**
- A waystone datapack: wild waystones roughly every 24 chunks in six biome styles, waystone plazas in Biomes We've Gone villages, and waystones in the Twilight Forest, the Graveyard's past and all four Northstar planets.
- Warping between dimensions costs 3 levels.
- Xaero's Minimap and World Map updated, which Fast Travel Waypoints needs.

**Removed**
- Falling Leaves and Farsight.

## Before that
The pack ran 251 mods through the summer. The magic update earlier in the year brought Ars 'n Spells, the shared mana bar, and the addon mods that grew both spell systems. Anything older than that isn't tracked here.

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

# Where to read more
This guide only covers the big picture for most mods. These are the wikis and docs worth bookmarking. Most are made by the mod authors or the main community for that mod, so they go a lot deeper. They're sometimes written for other versions, so for numbers trust this guide or the in-game tooltip. For how things work, they're great.

**Magic**
- Ars Nouveau: <https://www.arsnouveau.wiki/> (every glyph, ritual and familiar)
- Iron's Spells 'n Spellbooks: <https://iron.wiki/> (spells, schools, items and curios)
- Ars 'n Spells: <https://github.com/otectus/ars-n-spells> (how the shared mana works)
- Ars Nouveau source guide: <https://ars.guide/> (sourcelinks and automation, written for a newer version but the ideas hold)

**Adventure and creatures**
- Alex's Caves: <https://alexscaves.wiki.gg/> (the official wiki)
- Alex's Mobs: <https://alexs-mobs-unofficial.fandom.com/wiki/Alex%27s_Mobs_Unofficial_Wiki>
- Mowzie's Mobs: <https://mowziesmobs.fandom.com/wiki/Mowzie's_Mobs_Wiki>
- Twilight Forest: <http://benimatic.com/tfwiki/index.php/Main_Page> (official, with the progression and bosses)
- BetterEnd: <https://github.com/paulevsGitch/BetterEnd/wiki>

**Weapons and gear**
- Simply Swords: <https://moddedmc.wiki/en/project/simplyswords/latest/docs/introduction>
- Artifacts: <https://github.com/ochotonida/artifacts/wiki>
- Ensorcellation: <https://ftb.fandom.com/wiki/Ensorcellation>

**Tech**
- Create: <https://createmod.com/> and the community wiki <https://createmodminecraft.miraheze.org/wiki/Main_Page>
- Valkyrien Skies 2: <https://wiki.valkyrienskies.org/wiki/Main_Page>
- RFTools: <https://mcjty.eu/docs/mods>
- Create: Power Grid: <https://createpowergrid.miraheze.org/> (circuits, voltage and its generators)
- Create: Enchantment Industry: <https://modrinth.com/mod/create-enchantment-industry> (the machines and how liquid experience flows)
- CC: Tweaked: <https://tweaked.cc/>

**Space and vehicles**
- Northstar Redux: <https://www.curseforge.com/minecraft/mc-mods/northstar-redux>
- Clockwork: <https://wiki.valkyrienskies.org/wiki/Clockwork> (physics ships built from Create parts)
- Immersive Aircraft: <https://modrinth.com/mod/immersive-aircraft>
- Dragon Mounts: Legacy: <https://github.com/Kay9Unit/Dragon-Mounts-Legacy/wiki> (eggs, habitats, taming and raising)

**Everyday**
- Farmer's Delight: <https://farmersdelight.fandom.com/wiki/Farmer's_Delight_Wiki>
- Brewin' and Chewin': <https://github.com/ChefsDelights/BrewinAndChewin/wiki/Getting-Started>
- Croptopia: <https://croptopia.fandom.com/wiki/Croptopia_Wiki>
- Supplementaries: <https://github.com/MehVahdJukaar/Supplementaries/wiki>
- Waystones: <https://mods.twelveiterations.com/>
- Minecraft Comes Alive: <https://github.com/Luke100000/minecraft-comes-alive/wiki> (the mod's own docs)

For everything else, JEI in game (`R` for recipes, `U` for uses) is the most reliable source, because it shows this pack's actual recipes.

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

# That's it

That's everything we've got for now. Like we said at the start, it's not the full picture. There are structures nobody on the server has found yet and mod combos nobody's tried, so go explore and see what you run into.

If you find something cool, or you spot a mistake in here, say so in the server and we'll add it or fix it.
